- Peak (peaks) is a product research tool used to see products that are being sold and to shortlist product ideas. The lesson combined it with Amazon reviews and Facebook Ads Library checks. [L0577.030]
- To see the best sellers of a Shopify store, add the path collections/all with the parameter sort_by=best-selling to the store domain. [L0335.020]

#### Store Tracker and Sales Tracker

- Store Tracker setup: Store Tracker > Insert Store URL > Start Tracking. After that the tracker shows bestsellers, sales, traffic and market data for the store. [L1164.005]
- Sales Tracker Details shows the launch date, monthly revenue bracket, monthly visits, traffic by country, product and collection counts, pixels and stack, apps, similar stores and ad-rank partnership ads for a store. Use these fields together to judge the store's scale and marketing setup. [L1164.008]

### Website traffic and SimilarWeb

#### SimilarWeb

- SimilarWeb is used for competitor research and analysis. [L0255.054]
- SimilarWeb estimates a store's monthly traffic, traffic sources and rough revenue. Use it to check competitor traffic size before deciding on a niche. [L0871.050]
- SimilarWeb shows a store's monthly visits, its traffic split between direct, search, social and paid sources, and its country ranking. Use the ranking to identify the store's main market before choosing your own ad country, and check the traffic size before committing to a niche. [L0871.089]
- SimilarWeb shows a competitor's paid traffic sources, visitor demographics, sites where ads run, the first-seen date of each ad and how long it has run. Use it to collect most of the five campaign variables in minutes. The tool is the author's preferred choice at the time of writing, so check for alternatives if it stops working. [L0523.037]

Free search limits differ by route, and the sources give different values:

| Route | Free limit | Source |
|---|---|---|
| SimilarWeb site, free account (2025) | 15 searches per day; no workaround without a paid plan | [L0255.055] |
| SimilarWeb Chrome extension, free tier | 15 searches per week, shown as a counter such as "1 out of 15" | [L1332.004, L0808.047] |
| SimilarWeb free account (2026 advice) | 25 searches per week | [L0808.047] |

Use the limit that matches the route you are on. The 2026 source gives 25 per week for a free account without naming the route, so check the limit shown in your own account. [L0255.055, L1332.004, L0808.047]

#### SimilarWeb Chrome extension

- The SimilarWeb Chrome extension is free to install from the Chrome Web Store and shows traffic data for the website open in the browser. After you click its icon, a panel opens on the right side with global and country traffic ranks. It removes the need for a paid subscription or a free trial account on similarweb.com. [L0687.003]
- SimilarWeb Chrome extension, country check: scroll down to the country rank section and read the rank for the country where you plan to do business (the lesson uses the United States). [L0687.004]
- Install the Chrome extension to see a site's traffic and country ranking without entering a URL. Click the icon on the website you are viewing to open a sidebar. [L0808.047]
- Install the SimilarWeb Chrome extension, open a competitor's website, click the extension icon and scroll to Country Rank to view their ranking in your target country. If you are not using Chrome, access SimilarWeb.com with a free account. [L0051.005]
- The extension is free and needs no account or email address. It works only in Google Chrome and returns the same data as the free trial on similarweb.com, so it is a fallback when that trial cannot be set up. [L1332.001]
- Because the extension needs no account or email, it avoids the setup problems some users have with the similarweb.com free trial. Use it as an alternative route to the same data when the trial fails. [L1332.006]
- The extension's ranking section shows a competitor's traffic rank, including the country rank. It is used to run the traffic rank test on future competitors. [L1097.022]
- The SimilarWeb Chrome extension is used to view estimated traffic and related website data for competitor and niche research. [L0817.003]

SimilarWeb extension install procedure:

1. Use Google Chrome as the browser; download and install it first if it is missing. [L1332.002]
2. Open the SimilarWeb Website Traffic and SEO Checker listing in the Chrome Web Store (search Google for "SimilarWeb Chrome extension" if no link is at hand). [L1332.002]
3. Click "Add to Chrome". [L1332.002]
4. Click "Add extension" in the confirmation prompt. [L1332.002]
5. Click the Extensions icon in the Chrome toolbar and find SimilarWeb in the list. [L1332.002]
6. Click "Pin" so the extension stays in the navigation bar; its icon is a blue and orange logo. [L1332.002]

#### Alexa (older)

- Alexa.com (free version) shows the global rank of a competitor's website. A global rank inside one million is considered enough traffic to be worth studying, and the tool does not show exact visitor numbers. (Older advice, 2018.) [L1085.045]
- Use a competitor research tool that aggregates domain rank data instead of paying for Alexa. If a supplier or competitor domain shows no global or domestic rank there, it is simply not ranked. (Older advice, 2019.) [L1086.057]
- Checking a competitor's traffic rank on Alexa.com (older advice, 2018): [L1085.046]

1. Open Alexa.com and scroll to the bottom right of the page. [L1085.046]
2. Click Browse or Search Sites. [L1085.046]
3. Type the competitor's domain name and click Find. [L1085.046]
4. Read the global rank: if it is inside one million, the site gets enough traffic to be worth your attention. [L1085.046]

- A competitor research tool returns no data when a competitor site is too small. Try larger websites in the niche instead, because a missing result means there is not enough volume for that site. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0968.052]

### Technology stack lookups

- BuiltWith (builtwith.com) is a free tool that identifies all software, platforms and tools used by any website. Search for a competitor's domain to see their Shopify version, apps, payment processors and other integrations. [L0253.030]
- BuiltWith is a website lookup tool that shows which platforms, apps and technologies a competitor's site uses. [L0984.002]
- BuiltWith is a free website that shows the apps and technologies used on any URL, such as the Shopify apps on a competitor's store. Cold-email agencies use the same kind of tool to contact every Shopify store, which is why such outreach happens. [L0965.055]
- BuiltWith can show which apps and technologies a Shopify or other website uses, which helps identify stores built recently by agencies. [L1115.054]
- Cola is a Chrome extension that shows which apps a store has installed. Use it on a competitor's store to see whether it runs a print-on-demand supplier app, though some apps stay undetected. [L1214.025]
- Pricing and testing apps seen on competitor stores: Intelligems for A/B testing of pricing and layouts, and Kaching Bundles for quantity-break bundles. [L1164.036]
- Marketing and review apps seen on competitor stores: Klaviyo for email and SMS, Judge.me for product reviews, a Product Amazon Reviews app for importing reviews, and Triple Whale for analytics. [L1164.037]

### Competitor creative research, swipe files and monitoring

- A swipe board, for example in the Foreplay app, lets you save competitor ads into boards for later reference. Build one board per angle or per competitor so creative briefs start from real examples. [L0524.069]
- Clipping services collect everything published about a product category, including competitor ads and claims, so the ad writer sees the full competitive picture. Use them as a source of competitive research before writing ads. [L1261.049]
- Google Chrome 'Search image with Google' (right-click an image) is used to find the same product image on other sites, which often reveals the brand name of a product. [L0805.056]
- AMZ Shark and AMZ Tracker track competitor products, showing their sales velocity, reviews, pricing and trends over time. They are useful for ongoing competitive monitoring but not essential when starting. [L0228.030]
- SearchMonitor is a paid tool that monitors competitors' Product Listing Ads and can flag sellers pricing below MAP, replacing manual SKU-by-SKU price checks. It is an option for stores that want constant price data. The alternative is to audit ad accounts every one to two weeks and investigate when sales drop, which can reveal a competitor breaking MAP by around 10%. (Older advice, 2022.) [L0429.082]

### Amazon research tools

- Amazon publishes a fee lookup tool on its seller site. Use it to check all fees for a product before choosing a niche and calculating profit. [L1153.071]
- The Amazon Best Sellers page shows the top 100 products in each category and its subcategories. Use it to find product ideas and check what sells. [L1153.072]
- Seller Pulse is an Amazon research tool used to find top-selling products on the marketplace. Amazon Ads sponsored listings can place products at the top of search results for shoppers. [L0788.047]
- Jungle Scout is a product research tool for FBA. Like AMZ Shark, it is helpful for detailed analysis but not required to launch your first product. [L0228.031]
- Review Index is a Chrome extension that adds a summarize button to an Amazon listing and shows the pros and cons from customer reviews, which helps spot common complaints. [L1158.025]
- Amazon Reviews Summarizer setup: install the Chrome extension (find it by searching 'amazon review summarizer' on Google and opening the first result). On an Amazon product page, click the extension's summary just below the product name and price to list the pros and cons customers mention. [L1183.027]
- A ChatGPT plugin that reads Amazon reviews returned summary themes such as product quality, effectiveness and a self-cleaning feature, which were judged shallow. Exporting all reviews to a spreadsheet with a Chrome extension and pasting them into ChatGPT in batches gave richer audience insight. [L0353.056]
- Axiom is a browser extension that scrapes product review data into Google Sheets by selecting fields such as star rating, date and review text on a product page. [L1115.055]

Procedure for scraping product reviews with the Axiom extension:

1. Open the Axiom extension on the product page and click Get current URL. [L1115.056]
2. Click Select for the first review field, such as star rating, then click a second matching element so the rest auto-fills. [L1115.056]
3. Repeat for other fields such as date and review text, clicking two matching elements each time. [L1115.056]
4. Set the scrape limit, such as 20 reviews. [L1115.056]
5. If reviews are paginated, click Find pager and select the show more reviews button so the extension moves through all pages. [L1115.056]
6. Click Complete, then paste the results into Google Sheets and sort by the rating column. [L1115.056]

Kalodata has no price on the page and is excluded. The screen list also says more than 40 other tools are included, but gives no prices for them. [L1387.015]

Adding the 19 priced screen-only tools gives $1,255.99 per month at list price if Answer The Public is counted at $199, or $1,089.99 if it is counted at $33. Added to the $2,149.99 of the 18 priced sales-page tools, the combined list total of the 37 priced tools is $3,239.98 to $3,405.98 per month. The claimed saving of $2,000 to $3,000 per month only holds for a buyer who would otherwise pay for most of these tools. [L1387.016]

- The claimed monthly savings of $2,000 to $3,000 assume the buyer would otherwise pay for almost all listed tools. A seller who uses only two or three of them saves far less than the headline figure. [L1387.005]
- The savings claimed for a bundle are measured against buying every tool at list price. The real saving for one seller is the list price of the tools they would have bought anyway, minus what they pay. Shared subscriptions only save money on tools you would actually use. [L1387.002]
- Extras advertised with a bundle, such as 10,000+ templates, 10,000+ paid courses, 1,600+ Shopify themes or a winning product every day, are marketing claims with no cash value in a savings calculation unless they are used. Do not add them to the savings total. [L1387.006]
- The tool list and prices differ between the course page and the on-screen capture. Winninghunter appears on screen at $90 per month but is marked as not included on the page. Answer The Public shows $199 in one capture and $33 in another. Check the current list and price on each provider's own pricing page before budgeting. [L1387.008]
- Before paying for a bundle, check the promo codes quoted in the lesson at checkout, because they differ: the spoken code is ecommerce10 and the course page shows Source D10. Confirm which code is accepted and what discount it gives on the final price before relying on it. [L1387.009]
- The bundle promises 24/7 access with no downtime, multiple accounts when needed, shared credentials with auto login, new tools every month and quick support for anything. No uptime percentage, support response time or notice period for removed tools is stated, so treat these as promises that need written confirmation before you pay. [L1387.014]

### Shared accounts and their risks

- Shared-account services such as ShareTool split the cost of an ad account among several users. For example, a tool priced at about 100 per month can cost about 20 per month when five users share it. Shared accounts are much cheaper, but other users can see anything saved in them. [L0361.047]
- Do not save winning products inside a shared ad account, because other users can see them. Keep saved product research in your own browser or elsewhere. [L0361.048]
- Access through shared accounts is not guaranteed, even when a bundle promises 24/7 access and no downtime. A suspended shared account can cut off a whole workflow, so export and save research data locally and avoid building a single critical process on one shared login. [L1387.011]

### Choosing Tools by Job

- The lesson's tool list serves four main jobs: product and ad spying, keyword and SEO research, Amazon product research, and content creation. [L1387.018]
- Product and ad spying: Pipiads, PPSpy, Adspy, Dropispy, Foreplay, Adsparo, Pinspy, Peeksta, Dropship.io, Shophunter, Kalodata, NicheScraper. [L1387.018]
- Keyword and SEO research: Answer The Public, WooRank, SpyFu, Exploding Topics. [L1387.018]
- Amazon product research: Helium 10. [L1387.018]
- Design, video, audio and AI content creation: Canva Pro, Capcut Pro, Clipchamp, Picsart, Photoroom, Runway, Submagic, ElevenLabs, Midjourney, Pika Art. [L1387.018]
- Rules for subscriptions:
  1. Match each subscription to the job it does for your store. [L1387.018]
  2. Cancel any tool that has no current job. [L1387.018]

### TikTok Shop and Ad Discovery Tools

- PeakStar's TikTok ads search finds running dropshipping ads by country, like count, recency and keywords. [L1436.007]
- PPAds is a larger ad library used for the same purpose. [L1436.007]
- PPAds has offered a trial with full access for 3 days. The trial price was $1 earlier and later $7, so the figure that applies depends on when you sign up. [L1436.008]
- FastMoss is used for product research. It scans TikTok Shop and Shopify data to reveal trending products and their sales figures instantly. [L1459.007]

### Kalodata

- Kalodata is a TikTok Shop research tool that ranks products, videos and creators by revenue, views and ad metrics. [L1479.001]
- Set the region to United States before reading any ranking, because results differ by market. [L1479.001]
- Newer versions add a product detail summary and a buyer review summary. [L1479.001]
- Kalodata filter groups named in the lesson (2025 interface): [L1479.003]

| Filter group | Options |
|---|---|
| Dates | Last 30 Days |
| Revenue Filters | Revenue Uptrend, Revenue Growth, Revenue |
| Video filters | High ROAS, High Organic Traffic Sales Videos, Low-Follower Sales Videos, Video & Ad Livestream |
| Ad Filters | Ad Spend, Ad ROAS, Ad View Ratio |
| Creator filters | Creator Debut Time, Creator Followers, Creator Number, Creator Conversion Ratio |
| Category and shop | Category (search by category name), Shop Creator |
| Pricing | Pricing (price range) |
| Product tabs | Top New Products, High Potential Affiliate, Sales Grow Rapidly, Top Video Products, Local Warehouse Dropshipping |
| Advanced | Advanced, Save selected filters |

### AI-Assisted Niche Analysis

- ChatGPT can produce a rough market analysis of a niche, including top competitors and top brands. [L1446.017]
- The output is not fully reliable. Use it only as a starting point, then check it against real analytics. [L1446.017]

## Keyword and SEO Research Tools

### Choosing Tools by Budget

- Google Search Console is free and sufficient for starting out, before investing in AH Refs or SEM Rush. [L0255.060]
- Free keyword research tools are sufficient for basic keyword research, and paid versions are not needed for it. [L0665.049]
- At least one paid third-party SEO tool is required for an active SEO strategy, but you do not need to use all of them. Each tool has a different price point, and the return on investment is usually easy to achieve if the tool is used regularly. [L1272.006]
- The choice of a third-party SEO tool depends on budget and preference, but at least one such tool is needed. Premium tools offer more data depth than budget tools. [L1272.014]
- The data on links and search terms in Google Search Console and Bing Webmaster Tools is very limited. Third-party SEO tools provide far more data on links, search terms and competitor sites. [L1272.005]
- Paid SEO and outreach tools are worth their cost because automating research and email collection can save hundreds of hours of manual work if the method is used regularly. Free or cheaper options can be used at first, and paid tiers can be added once the workload justifies them. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.013]
- Free starting source: if you have run campaigns for some time, your own Google Ads search term data, for example from Google Shopping campaigns, already shows search volume for terms in your niche. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.009]
- Outsourcing keyword research to a freelancer on Fiverr is a cheap alternative to doing it yourself. You give the freelancer your root terms, they pull the keyword data and send it back, and you then group and allocate the terms to pages. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.014]
- Budget tier, Ubersuggest: the budget option among third-party SEO tools. A paid plan costs roughly $10 to $15 per month (price at time of recording). It offers less data depth than premium tools, but it is enough for many basic tasks. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1272.007]
- Ubersuggest price points differ by source (older advice, 2021-22):
  - About $10 per month. [L0877.010]
  - Roughly $10 to $15 per month. [L1272.007]
  - About $7 US dollars per month, or about 10 Australian dollars per month. [L1485.009]
- Free Ubersuggest accounts: the free account gives very little usable data, so a paid plan is recommended even on a tight budget. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1272.008]
- Ahrefs, premium tier, price: a premium third-party SEO tool at around $100 USD per month (price at time of recording). It is essential for link building and content research and is used heavily when running an active SEO strategy. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1272.009]
- Ahrefs, necessity, conflicting views:
  - Ahrefs is considered the best option for keyword research, but it is not strictly necessary. Ubersuggest is a lower-cost alternative whose reports cover the same information. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0878.010]
  - Ahrefs, Moz and SEMrush are expensive paid tools. They are more accurate and show more data points per term, but they are not necessary for content keyword research. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.013]
- SEMrush: a premium alternative to Ahrefs at a similar price level. Its advantage is paid search features such as ad research, which Ahrefs did not offer at that time. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1272.010]
- Raven Tools: a suite that costs somewhat less than Ahrefs and SEMrush. Its SEO audit tool analyzes a website and lists areas that can be improved. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1272.013]
- Screaming Frog: an older, thorough SEO tool that runs as a paid desktop application, not online. It can handle most SEO tasks, but analyzing a very large website can slow down the computer and take a long time. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1272.011]
- For large SEO projects, an online tool is preferable to a desktop application, because a desktop crawl of a very large website can strain the computer and run for a long time. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1272.012]
- Most SEO research tools provide the same core reports: keyword, competitor and backlink reports. Their scoring of domain authority and keyword difficulty can differ slightly between tools, but the results should still be usable for the method. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.004]
- Moz domain analysis: the free version of Moz.com's domain analysis tool shows the domain authority of any website. It allows 3 reports per day and needs no sign-up or payment. [L1227.004]

### Free Keyword Research Tools

#### Google Keyword Planner

- Google Keyword Planner is free for anyone with a Google Ads account and provides relevant search volume data. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.011]
- It is used to check search volume for niche keywords before choosing a niche. (older advice, 2021) [L0801.114]
- It is used, together with Ubersuggest, to check search volume and demand for niche keywords. (older advice, 2020) [L0664.074]
- Volume ranges depend on account history:
  - New accounts: very broad volume ranges, for example 10,000 to 100,000. (older advice? Source unlabelled) [L0359.048]
  - After the account has spent money on ads: the range narrows to within about 1,000. [L0359.048]
- Sign-up: Google Keyword Planner sign-up asks for a company name and a website. If you do not have a website yet, enter the domain name you plan to use. (older advice, 2019) [L1275.012]
- Keywords Everywhere can replace Google Keyword Planner when that tool will not let you sign up. (older advice, 2019) [L1275.011]
- Keyword and trend data pulled through third-party APIs can fail intermittently. One trend-data source was reported to return results only about 9 percent of the time over a few days. Retry the search after a few minutes first. If failures continue, switch to another data source such as Google Keyword Planner, which requires a Google Ads account. [L1097.047]

#### Ubersuggest

- Free version: Ubersuggest has a free version with a limit on searches per period. It gives keyword volume ranges within roughly 1,000 right away, which makes it useful as a free keyword tool before a Google Ads account has spent money. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0359.047]
- Free searches, 2021: as of 2021, Ubersuggest offered five free keyword searches per day, which was an alternative for countries not covered by the program's keyword tool. (older advice, 2021) [L0671.028]
- Free access for full data: a free account is needed to see the full data. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1485.009]
- Ubersuggest is used to estimate monthly Google search volume per country, and later for other marketing research during the business build. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1485.009]
- Ubersuggest is used to fill the competitor analysis worksheet by showing where a competitor's website gets its traffic from. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0441.010]
- Metrics: Ubersuggest shows search volume, SEO difficulty, paid difficulty, average CPC, keyword ideas and CSV export. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.010]
- Keyword Discovery page: three checks per keyword. Use them together when judging a keyword, not volume alone. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1485.017]
  - Traffic volume: how many buyers search for it.
  - Trends: how search volume has changed over time.
  - Difficulty: whether a keyword is easy to rank for.
- Market setting: in the language and country selector, choose the market (English / United States for a US store). It appears in the URL as locId=2840 for the United States. Change it to the target country, such as Australia, before every export, because search volume and difficulty differ by country. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0758.019]
- Keyword overview: shows searcher age ranges, mobile versus desktop volume, a 12-month volume chart, and the average backlinks and domain score of the top 10 ranking pages. For 'surfboards' the top 10 averaged 14 backlinks and a domain score of 54, while for 'wetsuits' the figures were 33 backlinks and a domain score of 72. Use these benchmarks to estimate how much link building a term will need. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0758.021]
- Top-ranking URLs: the keyword overview also lists the top ranking URLs with estimated visits, backlinks, domain score and social shares. Use it to see what kind of page ranks, such as marketplaces, Wikipedia, or store collection pages, before deciding whether to target a term with content, collection or product pages. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0758.022]
- Feature gap: Ahrefs and Semrush both allow keyword ranking reports with a featured snippet filter. Ubersuggest does not offer this filter, so it is unsuitable for this step. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0662.005]

#### Google and YouTube Auto Suggest

- Google and YouTube Auto Suggest are free and show popular searches related to a root term. They are a good source of keyword ideas, and YouTube keyword research works through its own search suggestions. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.012]

#### Keywords Everywhere

- Free browser extension: Keywords Everywhere is a free Chrome extension (also described as a free browser extension for Chrome or Firefox) that shows keyword data next to Google search results. Use it during niche research and campaign planning, to research search volume and estimated cost-per-click before a new Google Ads account gains access to the keyword planning tool. (older advice, 2019) [L0243.042, L1275.011]
- Pricing changed, conflicting views: Keywords Everywhere switched to a paid model, while free keyword tools with similar purposes exist. Use one of those alternatives for niche keyword research if the paid option is not worth it. (older advice, 2019) [L1110.021]
- Where both views apply: the 2019 units describe the tool as free, and the later note says it moved to a paid model. Check current pricing before relying on it. [L0243.042, L1275.011, L1110.021]

#### Other Free Research Sources

- Google Gemini was suggested as a starting point for keyword lists, based on the idea that Google's own model may have better access to Google search data. Verify any keyword list it generates before using it in campaigns. [L1287.035]

### Paid Keyword and SEO Suites

#### Ahrefs (Keywords Explorer and Site Explorer)

- Ahrefs trial: Ahrefs offers a 14-day trial, which is enough time to run the keyword process for 10 articles. You can cancel after the trial if the results are not useful. [L0382.046]
- Ahrefs usage in plan, limits: Keywords Explorer shows these limits in the plan used: 100 daily reports and 1,000 monthly SERP updates. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0878.011]
- Overview metrics: Clicks, CPS (clicks per search) and RR (return rate) are shown alongside search volume and CPC. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0878.011]
- Bulk keyword checks: Keywords Explorer accepts a bulk list of keywords, pasted separated by commas or on new lines, or uploaded as a CSV or TXT file. This is useful for checking many candidate topics at once. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0878.025]
- Keyword lists: you can save candidate keywords into keyword lists, with up to 20 lists per account. Use these lists to group topics for each niche or project. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0878.026]
- Ahrefs for collection terms: Ahrefs and SEMrush are both used to quantify monthly search volume for a candidate category before a collection page is created. The same tools can check volume for hybrid keywords on a recurring basis. [L1389.016]
- Backlinks: Ahrefs shows backlinks to any domain, including the sources of the links. Use it to see where competitors get their links and whether they publish content on other domains. (older advice, 2022) [L0963.051]
- Ahrefs and the skyscraper method: Ahrefs is a paid SEO tool used for keyword research, competitor analysis, backlink reports for individual sites, and metrics such as domain authority and keyword difficulty. It is one of the more expensive paid tools in this link-building toolkit. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.001]
- AH Refs (written as "AH Refs" in the source, most likely Ahrefs):
  - Used by teams to track organic keyword rankings and analyze SEO data. [L0255.058]
  - Costs approximately $300 per month for team access (2025 pricing). [L0255.059]
  - Price conflict: the $100 per month Ahrefs figure from 2021-22 and the approximately $300 per month figure from 2025 do not match. The 2025 figure applies to team access, and the 2021-22 figure applies to a single plan at that time. [L1272.009, L0255.059]
- Bulk exports, procedures (Ahrefs), see the export and cleaning procedures below. [L1443.012, L1443.013]

#### Semrush

- SEMrush is a widely used SEO and SEM research tool that offers keyword, competitor and backlink reports, though its report layouts differ and you may need to look around to find the equivalents. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.003]
- Its advantage over Ahrefs is paid search features such as ad research. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1272.010]
- Its featured snippet filter for keyword ranking reports is available. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0662.005]
- It is listed among the expensive paid tools that are not necessary for content keyword research. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.013]
- Ahrefs and Semrush used for collection volume checks. [L1389.016]

#### Moz

- Moz is listed among the expensive paid tools with keyword research functions. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.013]

#### Other tools (Answer The Public, Source A, Research Vault, Source A, BuzzSumo)

- Answer The Public (answerthepublic.com) generates question and preposition keyword variations for a topic and can be exported as CSV to plan content for subniches. (older advice, 2022) [L0665.046]
- Source A keyword research tool: gives search data by country and keyword. It works for any keyword where data is available, and bugs can be reported to support. (older advice, 2022) [L0665.048]
- Research Vault search volume tool: shows how often a keyword is searched. Use it to test whether generic, non-brand terms in a non-English product language are popular enough in English-speaking markets. [L0941.044]
- Source A keyword tool:
  - Used to find search terms and niche ideas before building a store. (older advice, 2021) [L1089.006]
  - Keyword research is done with it, and it should always return results even with zero search volume. If it does not, contact support. (older advice, 2022) [L0969.054]
- In-house keyword research tool: gives members unlimited searches when they log in with their member account and select the target country. Logged-out use is limited to a free version, so keyword checks should be done after logging in. (older advice, 2021) [L0962.150]
- BuzzSumo does for blog content what vidIQ does for YouTube, showing trending articles and their performance. It is used to research topics before writing blog posts. [L0487.063]

### Keyword Research Workflow in Spreadsheets

- Export keyword ideas from Ubersuggest for each root term:
  1. Enter the root keyword in Ubersuggest and open the keyword ideas view. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.017]
  2. Check that the country setting matches your target market. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.017]
  3. Click Export to CSV and save the file to your computer. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.017]
  4. Repeat for every root term in your business, including terms for product types and product uses. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.017]
- Keyword ideas view tabs. The results are grouped into five tabs: Suggestions, Related, Questions, Prepositions and Comparisons. Each tab has its own count. The SUGGESTIONS count shows how many similar terms were found (for example 255 for '4x4 accessories'). Each keyword row shows KEYWORD, VOL and CPC columns, a Filters option sits above the list, and an EXPORT TO CSV button exports the full list. The Questions tab is a good source for problem-based content topics. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.030, L1485.018, L0758.020]
- Suggestions and Related: the Suggestions tab is the narrowest list of closest matches, while the Related tab can contain many times more terms, most of them off-topic. Scan Related for relevant terms instead of exporting it wholesale, and use the Questions tab for question-form terms that suit problem-based content. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.032]
- Master keyword workbook layout (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.028]

| Tab | Columns | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Raw Data | Keyword, Volume, CPC, SEO difficulty (from CSV export) | All exported terms combined, sorted by volume, minimum 100 per month |
| Content | Keyword, Volume, CPC, SEO difficulty | Informational, problem-based and product-use terms for content pieces |
| Collection | Keyword, Volume, CPC, SEO difficulty | Broad product type, branded and specific product type terms |
| Product Page | Keyword, Volume, CPC, SEO difficulty | Branded, model-specific and very specific product terms |

- Summary formulas: summarise the volume and CPC columns in each tab with SUM, AVERAGE, MIN, MAX and COUNTA. This shows the total search volume per page type, the average and range of CPC, and how many keywords each group contains. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0877.029]

### Search Console and Bing Webmaster Tools

#### Google Search Console

- Google Search Console is free (search.google.com) and is used to verify domain ownership, submit a sitemap, see indexed pages, free organic impressions and clicks, search keywords and site errors. [L0735.011]
- Keyword tracking: Google Search Console is a free tool to track keyword rankings and organic traffic. [L0255.060]
- Search terms vs Google Analytics:
  - Google Analytics shows many search terms as "not provided" (2020) or "not set" (2021), because of privacy. Use Google Search Console with the same Google account to see the keywords that bring clicks. (older advice, 2020-21) [L0468.079, L0834.052]
  - Google Search Console shows more search terms with impressions and clicks than Google Analytics. (older advice, 2021) [L0834.052]
- Submitting new content: Google Search Console (Google webmaster tools) can be used to submit new content URLs so they get crawled sooner. [L0970.094]
- Import into Bing: when importing from Google Search Console into Bing, see the Bing setup below. [L0303.006]

#### Bing Webmaster Tools

- Bing Webmaster Tools, found at bing.com/webmasters, is Bing's free dashboard for seeing how Bing crawls and indexes a website. Its main areas are Search Performance, Site Explorer and error monitoring. [L0303.001]
- Search Performance and Site Explorer: Search Performance shows how the site appears in Bing search results, and Site Explorer lets you inspect the site's pages. Both are used to find pages that have not been indexed and to spot issues that need fixing. [L0303.010]
- Sign-in: Bing Webmaster Tools accepts sign-in with a Microsoft, Google or Facebook account. Use the same email address you used for your Google accounts so you can find and manage all accounts consistently. [L0303.004]
- Same email, what it does and does not do: using the same email address for the Bing login does not link the accounts automatically. Its only benefit is consistent login credentials across services, which makes it easier to remember and access each account. [L0303.005]
- Setup flow, button and label names as shown:
  - Get started: first screen. [L0303.003]
  - Import your sites from GSC or Google Search Console: import option on the Welcome page. [L0303.003]
  - Allow: sharing approval. [L0303.003]
  - Import: domain confirmation. [L0303.003]
  - Done: shown after the success message. [L0303.003]
  - Update now: in the profile section. [L0303.003, L0303.014]
- Importing from Google Search Console:
  - Bing identifies the Google account by its email address, so choose the account that was used to set up the Search Console property. If several domains are linked to that account, Bing lets you pick which one to import. [L0303.006]
  - After importing, Bing may need up to 48 hours to process the data and reports. Until processing finishes, the dashboard data may be incomplete. [L0303.007]
- Profile section: Update now opens fields for personal information, professional details and company information. The same section also contains a delete account option, which should not be clicked by accident. [L0303.014]

### Competitor and Backlink Research

- Backlink export, Site Explorer (older advice, 2021-22):
  1. In Site Explorer, open Backlinks. [L1443.013]
  2. Set the link filter to Dofollow. [L1443.013]
  3. Set Group similar links to One link per domain. [L1443.013]
  4. Click Export. Choose Quick export for the first 1000 rows or Full export for all rows, with CSV format. [L1443.013]
- SERP export: in Ahrefs, the export button sits at the top right of the SERP results table. Clicking it downloads a CSV. Choose UTF-16 for Microsoft Excel or UTF-8 for Open Office and Libre. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1443.012]
- Removing duplicates in Google Sheets: install the Remove Duplicates add-on via Add-ons > Get add-ons, then open Add-ons > Remove Duplicates > Find duplicate or unique rows. Choose Duplicates (exclude first instances), select the URL column, and tick that the table has headers. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1443.014]
- Removing duplicates in Excel: Excel has a built-in Find duplicate rows feature that works the same way as the Google Sheets add-on, and it is the simpler choice when available. Apple Numbers does not offer an easy remove-duplicates function. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1443.015]
- Excel duplicate modes: the duplicate tool is in the menu and offers four modes: Duplicates, Duplicates + 1st occurrences, Uniques, and Uniques + 1st occurrences. Choose Duplicates (exclude first instances) to keep one copy of each URL and remove the later copies. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1443.020]
- Filtering by Domain Rating: in Google Sheets, apply a filter with Data > Create a filter, then use the Domain Rating column to keep only rows with values from 10 to 75. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1443.016]
- Example case: when removing duplicates from a merged Ahrefs keyword sheet of just over 100 SERP results, delete the duplicate rows outright instead of copying them to another location. In the example, this removed 19 duplicate rows. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1443.021]
- Other tools: the Site Explorer backlink report can be replaced by any SEO tool, and the same workflow works with those exports. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1443.017]
- Competitor benchmarks, Ubersuggest: see the overview metrics above. [L0758.021]
- Moz domain analysis, free: see the free-tools section above. [L1227.004]

### Email Finding and Outreach Tools

#### Hunter.io

- Hunter.io finds email addresses associated with a domain by searching the web. The single Domain Search page handles one domain at a time, while Bulk Domain Search processes whole lists. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.011]
- Hunter.io is the primary email address scraper for the skyscraper method. It finds email addresses for websites you want to contact, and it works best for small to medium-sized sites and companies, including bulk searches. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.005]
- Free plan: Hunter.io offers a free entry-level plan that is useful for testing the tool. Its paid plans are tiered by how many emails you need to collect, so you can downgrade to the free plan in months with no outreach and upgrade for a large outreach project. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.006]
- Pay per result: Hunter.io charges only for successful results. Searching a domain or an email address that returns no result costs nothing, and you pay per email address actually found. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.007]
- Plan requirement: Bulk Domain Search in Hunter.io requires the Starter plan or a higher plan tier. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.013]
- Bulk setup, procedure (older advice, 2021-22):
  1. Log into Hunter.io on a Starter plan or higher. [L0601.023]
  2. Open Bulks and choose New bulk Domain Search. [L0601.023, L0601.014]
  3. Enter a List name; it is internal and any name works. [L0601.023, L0601.014]
  4. Set Type of input to Domain names. Company names can be used as input instead when you need contacts for one specific company. [L0601.023, L0601.014]
  5. Paste the copied root domain list into the domain field, or click Select a file and upload a CSV, Excel, Numbers or TXT file. [L0601.023]
  6. Set Maximum email addresses per domain to 10. [L0601.023, L0601.015]
  7. Set Email addresses type to Generic and personal. [L0601.023, L0601.015]
  8. Leave Departments on All departments; leave Include the sources in the result off unless you need sources. [L0601.023, L0601.015]
  9. Click Upload and wait while the file is prepared. [L0601.023]
  10. Compare the estimated deliverability tiers and select one. [L0601.023]
  11. Download the result as a CSV file. [L0601.023]
- Bulk filter settings, in detail: Maximum email addresses per domain set to 10; Email addresses type set to Generic and personal; Departments set to All departments; Include the sources in the result is optional and was left off. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.015]
- Do not set the maximum emails per domain to a high number such as 100. Hunter.io deducts one plan credit for every successful email it finds, so large domains consume credits for addresses you will never use. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.016]
- Do not restrict the search to one email per domain, because companies often have several addresses and the single address returned may not be the best one for outreach. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.017]
- Speed: a bulk search of 78 domains finishes very quickly, while bulk searches with thousands of domains can take up to 10 to 15 minutes. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.018]
- Deliverability tiers from a bulk result for 78 domain names requested (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.019]

| Estimated deliverability | Email addresses | Domain names |
|---|---|---|
| 95% | 102 | 28 |
| 93% | 134 | 37 |
| 89% | 181 | 44 |
| 85% | 200 | 50 |

- Threshold: estimated deliverability of 85 percent is acceptable for outreach, while anything below 80 percent is too low to use. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.021]
- Trade-off: choose the tier that gives the most usable domains while staying above the deliverability threshold. Higher tiers such as 95 percent give fewer addresses, while 85 percent gives more addresses. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.022]
- CSV result: the Hunter.io CSV result lists every requested domain with all email addresses found for it, including name, position and confidence score, and it is the file used for manual selection. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.024]
- Confidence score: it is Hunter.io's estimate of how likely an address is to be deliverable. Use it together with the address itself rather than on its own. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.026]
- Additional CSV columns: the bulk CSV also lists Department, Position, Type, Number of sources, Pattern, Twitter handle, LinkedIn URL and Phone number. Position and Department help check whether a contact fits a content or link request, and Pattern shows the naming format the domain uses for its addresses. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.034]
- Bulks history: Hunter.io keeps every bulk search listed under Bulks with its list name, creation time and number of domains requested, and a completed search can be deleted from that list. This makes it easy to find an earlier run for the same project or remove test runs. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.036]

#### Voila Norbert

- Voila Norbit (also written Voila Norbert) is a secondary email finder for locating the address of a specific person when you know their name and the website or company they work for. It is useful when a large business returns too many generic addresses in Hunter.io and you want to contact one named editor or employee. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.008]
- It is not required for the skyscraper method and is only needed in rare cases where you want to reach a particular individual. Hunter.io should remain the default email finder. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.009]
- List cleaning, Mailshake's List Cleaner, powered by Voila Norbert, checks addresses before sending and sorts them into Ok, Risky (higher chance of bouncing) and Bad (almost certain to bounce). An Ok result does not guarantee deliverability. The first 500 emails are free, then the price is $10 per 5,000 credits, with one credit per email. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.026]
- List cleaning is optional, but it is recommended to protect deliverability. Always remove Bad addresses, and remove Risky addresses according to how much risk you accept. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.027]

#### Mailshake

- Mailshake is a cold email outreach tool built for sending bulk emails to people who have not contacted you before. It also manages the outreach process and provides reporting on campaign results. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.010]
- Mailshake is a bulk and automated outreach tool that sends personalised cold emails from your connected mailbox and runs automated follow-ups. It is used for sending link-building outreach, with the sending mailbox kept separate from the plan. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0574.011]
- It is used to send the outreach emails to the matched contacts when pursuing backlinks for skyscraper content. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.032]
- Do not send cold outreach emails through a regular email marketing or automation platform such as Klaviyo or Active Campaign. Cold emails to unknown recipients can generate spam complaints and dead addresses, which can damage the deliverability and performance of your main email account. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1388.011]

##### Mailshake plans and prices

- Basic plan: 39 USD per month when paid monthly, or 29 USD per month when paid yearly, for one user (2021 to 2022 pricing). Basic includes Gmail and G Suite accounts, unlimited Gmail aliases, open, reply and click tracking, personalisation, advanced scheduling and throttling, automated follow-ups, Lead Catcher and CRM integrations via Zapier. Mailshake also offered a 30-day full refund guarantee at the time. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0574.012]
- Pro plan: 44 USD per month per user (2021 to 2022). It adds A/B testing, conversion tracking JavaScript, higher API limits, Salesforce and Slack integrations and 5,000 list-cleaning credits monthly. Extra reopen-cleaning credits were sold at 10 USD per 5,000 reopens. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0574.013]
- Pro also adds filter campaigns by teammate and campaign labels, which the Basic plan does not include. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0574.026]
- Omni plan: 75 USD per month per user at the time (2021 to 2022). On top of Pro, it adds custom tasks, social selling, a contacts view, a higher recipient limit, an onboarding phone call, a phone dialer and 10,000 list-cleaning credits each month. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0574.025, L0574.013]
- Choosing the plan: sign up for the Basic plan, not Pro or Omni. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0574.014]

##### Mailshake account setup, procedure (older advice, 2021-22)

1. Sign up for the Basic plan, not Pro or Omni, and enter payment and account details. [L0574.014]
2. On the Connect a mail account screen, choose GMAIL / G SUITE (any Google account) and click Next. [L0574.014, L0574.024]
3. Sign in to the Google account when asked and click Allow so Mailshake can access it. [L0574.014]
4. Set the sending calendar (sending limit, time between sends and sending pace) and click Next. [L0574.014]
5. Add the signature, including the unsubscribe placeholder, and click Next. [L0574.014]
6. Confirm the account under Mail Accounts. Check the sending limit there with the calendar icon before starting your first campaign. [L0574.014]

##### Sending options

- The Connect a mail account screen offers four sending options: [L0574.024]
  - GMAIL / G SUITE: any Google account.
  - GMAIL ALIAS: any address set up as an alias in Gmail.
  - MICROSOFT: Outlook, Exchange or Office365 accounts.
  - SMTP: most email addresses.
- For a G Suite mailbox, pick GMAIL / G SUITE and click NEXT. [L0574.024]

##### Sending calendar presets

On the sending calendar, the cold start preset is the default. Choose the warmed-up preset only if the domain and mailbox have an established sending history. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0574.015]

| Setting | New email address or domain (cold start) | Warmed up, experienced sender |
|---|---|---|
| Sending limit | 20 / day | 300 / day |
| Time between sends | 12 min | 30 min |
| Sending pace | 1 email at a time | 5-20 emails at a time |

- Changing the daily send volume: change it in Mailshake under Mail Accounts, using the calendar icon next to the account, then edit the rules to change the maximum emails per day. Save, and also adjust the minutes between sends and the number of emails sent at once if needed. Start at 20 per day and raise the cap to 50 and later to 100 at the right times. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0574.018]

##### Campaign creation and import

1. Never work in the template file. Make a copy of the outreach batching template for every outreach task. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.017]
2. Rename the copy with the batch date, for example 2020.01.12. Any date format is fine. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.017]
3. Keep all column names exactly as they are in the template. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.017]
4. Open the Campaigns area and click the pink New campaign button. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.023]
5. Enter an internal title, for example the topic and the start date. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.023]
6. Select the from-address. Only one from-address should exist in the account. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.023]
7. Drag in or select the CSV file and wait for the upload to complete. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.023]
- CSV mapping: set the special column 'Email address' to the Email Address column and 'Full name (optional)' to the Name column. All other batch columns are imported as text replacement columns: Email Address, Name, Your Article, Topic, Prospects URL, Prospects Root Domain, Competitors URL, Competitors Root Domain and Date. Uncheck any column you do not want to import, then click Next. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.024]
- Import time: importing a campaign list takes 1 to 2 minutes for a small list and up to about 5 minutes for a larger one. Mailshake may then ask whether to remove generic addresses, such as editorial@ or info@. Keep them when they are the only contact for a site, and click ignore. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.025]

### Outreach Spreadsheets

#### Outreach Email Filtering template

- The Outreach Email Filtering template is a Google Sheets file that can be copied into Google Drive; the same layout also works as an Excel file. It strips URLs to root domains and holds the Hunter.io output for matching. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.004]
- Keep the outreach filtering template blank and duplicate it for each new project, so every project starts from a clean sheet. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.005]
- Template layout (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.006]
  - Column A: labelled 'Enter List of Referring Page URLs Here'.
  - Column B: returns the Referring Page URL Root Domain.
  - Column C: labelled 'Enter List of Link URLs Here'.
  - Column D: returns the Link URL Root Domain.
  - Columns E and F: hold Email Address and Name.
  - A second tab named 'Hunter Output' receives the Hunter.io results.
- Row alignment: columns A and C must be filled from the Ahrefs output sheets with row alignment exact, so each referring page URL stays on the same row as its matching link URL. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.007]
- Protected columns: deleting cells in the protected columns of the template triggers a warning. Only remove rows in the URL input columns and leave the calculated columns B, D, E and F alone. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.010]
- Root domain formula, reconstructed from a blurry screen. It has the same logic as the sheet's own version, which is already in the file. It removes the protocol, a leading www., and everything after the first slash, leaving only the root domain. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.008]

```
=IFERROR(IF(ISERROR(FIND("://www.",A2)),MID(A2,FIND("://",A2)+3,FIND("/",A2&"/",FIND("://",A2)+3)-FIND("://",A2)-3),MID(A2,FIND("://",A2)+7,FIND("/",A2&"/",FIND("://",A2)+7)-FIND("://",A2)-7)),"")
```
[L0601.008]

- Preparing root domains, procedure (older advice, 2021-22) [L0601.009]
  1. Duplicate the blank Outreach Email Filtering template for this project.
  2. Copy the referring page URL column from the Ahrefs output and paste it into column A under 'Enter List of Referring Page URLs Here'.
  3. Read the root domain for each referring page from column B.
  4. Copy the link URL column from the Ahrefs output and paste it into column C under 'Enter List of Link URLs Here'.
  5. Read the root domain for each link URL from column D.
  6. Remove rows you do not need from the URL columns, but do not delete anything in columns B, D, E or F.
  7. Copy the complete root domain list from column B to use as input for Hunter.io.
- Maintaining the email filtering spreadsheet, procedure (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.021]
  1. Paste the SEO tool output into columns A and C, making sure each row lines up exactly with its source.
  2. Do not delete anything in columns B, D, E and F.
  3. Enter the Hunter output in the second tab, named Hunter Output.
  4. When finished, remove the URLs from columns A and D. The other columns refresh automatically.
- Moving filtered data into the batch spreadsheet, procedure (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.019]
  1. Open the email filtering spreadsheet and filter the rows so that rows with a blank email address are hidden or removed. Rows without an email cannot be contacted.
  2. Select all data rows, excluding the heading rows, and copy them.
  3. In the batch spreadsheet, click the first cell under the Prospects URL column, not under Your Article.
  4. Use Paste special and choose values only, because formulas do not work after they are moved.

#### Outreach batching spreadsheet

Column order (do not rename any column) (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.018]

| Column | Content |
|---|---|
| Your Article | URL of the article you want linked |
| Topic | Topic from the original research |
| Prospects URL | URL of the prospect's page that links to a competitor or relevant source |
| Prospects Root Domain | Root domain of the prospect URL |
| Competitors URL | URL of the competitor page where the prospect found the link |
| Competitors Root Domain | Root domain of the competitor URL |
| Email Address | Contact email address of the prospect |
| Name | Contact first name, if known |
| Date | Date the outreach is sent |

- Filling the remaining batch fields, procedure (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.020]
  1. Enter the send date in the Date column and copy it down the column.
  2. Enter the topic from your original research in the Topic column.
  3. Enter the URL of your article in the Your Article column.
  
  Mailshake will insert these values into the emails through the matching fields. [L0946.020]
- Export for Mailshake: in Google Sheets, download the file as CSV. In Excel or another spreadsheet tool, save or convert the file as CSV before uploading. (older advice, 2021-22) [L0946.022]
- CSV export from Hunter: see the Hunter.io bulk download above. [L0601.024]

### Shopify SEO Apps

#### Smart SEO

- Smart SEO is a Shopify SEO app built by a company called Sherpas. It bundles several SEO functions (meta tags, alt tags, image compression, page speed, broken links, backlink reporting, structured data) into one tool. Other SEO apps exist, so using it is a choice, not a requirement. [L1337.001]
- Smart SEO is a Shopify app used for image optimization, meta tag generation and JSON-LD settings on pages, blog posts and articles. It handles the on-page technical steps in this workflow. [L1321.002]
- Use Smart SEO for SEO work on product pages, blog posts, and pages, including image compression. Do not run Tiny SEO alongside it; uninstall Tiny SEO once Smart SEO is installed. [L0808.048]
- Smart SEO is the Shopify SEO app used for setting up JSON-LD structured data in the current SEO lesson. The older JSON-LD app is no longer on the Shopify app store. [L0856.070]

##### Plans and pricing

- Smart SEO has a free plan and a Pro plan at $9.99 per month. The JSON-LD structured data feature is only available on the Pro plan, so start with Free if you are unsure how much SEO work you will do and upgrade later. [L1337.002]
- Choosing Free means the JSON-LD section is not available, so no structured data gets sent to Google. You can switch to Pro at any time later. [L1337.012]
- Pro plan approval screen: when you select the Smart SEO Pro plan at $9.99 per month, the Shopify approval screen shows a 7-day free trial and the price that applies after it. Read that screen and approve the charge only if you accept the monthly price once the trial ends. [L1337.024]

##### Features

- Image compression: Smart SEO compresses images so their file size drops while the visual quality stays acceptable. Smaller images load faster, which improves page speed scores and helps users on slow connections. [L1337.003]
- Meta tags and alt text: Smart SEO can generate meta titles and descriptions with AI, but this is optional and can be done manually. The app also lets you edit image alt texts in bulk. [L1337.004]
- Meta tag generation, procedure: [L0368.016]
  1. Open Smart SEO > Meta Tags > Collections.
  2. Select collections, then click Edit collections (bulk editing is available).
  3. Enter the main keyword, related keywords and tone of voice (for example playful).
  4. Click Generate to write the meta title and description. Try again gives alternatives.
- Predictive page loading and broken links: Smart SEO includes predictive page loading, which preloads pages to make the site load faster. It also detects and fixes broken internal links, which becomes more important as the store grows over months and years. [L1337.006]
- Backlink report: Smart SEO produces a backlink report that shows which sites link to your store. This becomes useful once content grows and more external sites link to you. [L1337.007]
- Left navigation: after Smart SEO is integrated, its left navigation lists separate sections for meta tags, alt tags, image optimization, page speed, broken links and more. Each option has built-in video tutorials inside the app, so new users can learn each function there. [L1337.023]

##### Installation, procedure

1. Open the Smart SEO listing in the Shopify App Store using the link provided. [L1337.009]
2. If you have several Shopify accounts, make sure you are signed into the correct one. [L1337.009]
3. Click Install. [L1337.009]
4. Review the access the app requests and approve it, since it must be able to work on your store. [L1337.009]
5. On the plan page, choose Free or Pro. [L1337.009]
6. If you choose Pro, confirm the 7-day free trial and the price shown after the trial, then approve the charge. [L1337.009, L1337.024]
7. Open the app and click Set up. [L1337.009]
8. If the app status says Smart SEO is not fully integrated, click Integrate now. [L1337.009, L1337.010]
9. Confirm the status shows the app as integrated in your store. [L1337.009]

- After activation the app shows Current plan (for example Pro) and App status. If App status says Smart SEO is not fully integrated, the app is not yet installed correctly and you must click Integrate now. [L1337.010]

#### Tiny SEO

- Tiny SEO is an SEO app for when the store is scaling. The lesson says to add it once a store reaches about 30K gross in sales. [L1327.039]
- Conflict to note: the Source D lesson (2026) suggests adding Tiny SEO at about 30K gross, while Source A's rule says not to run Tiny SEO alongside Smart SEO and to uninstall it once Smart SEO is installed. Use Tiny SEO only as an alternative to Smart SEO, not alongside it. [L1327.039, L0808.048]

#### Shopify image sitemap

- A Shopify image sitemap app generates a separate sitemap listing all product images. On Shopify, install such an app from the app store and submit its image sitemap URL in Google Search Console alongside the standard sitemap, not instead of it. Other platforms may not have this limitation and need their own check. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1333.003]

### Schema, Noindex and Page Audit

- Schema markup:
  - If a Shopify store already runs a working schema markup app, no action is needed. If it does not, install a schema app rather than relying on manual code or a developer. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1349.015]
  - Hiring a developer to add schema markup creates two problems: the store owner cannot easily verify that the work was done correctly, and every future markup change requires going back to that developer. A schema app avoids both because its developers update the markup automatically. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1349.016]
- Noindex on Shopify: the recommended way to add noindex to selected pages is a low-cost Shopify app rather than hand-editing theme.liquid. Manual code edits carry a risk: a small error can noindex the entire domain, causing search traffic to disappear overnight and requiring a long re-indexing process. (older advice, 2021-22) [L1372.008]
- Google Sheets page audit tracker, columns (older advice, 2021-22) [L1372.018]
  - Page Title
  - Page URL
  - Duplicate Content?
  - Thin Content?
  - Fix Content?
  - Delete?
  - If delete, redirect to where?
  - No-Index?
  - Separate sheets or columns for Page Audit and Orphan Pages.

### Bulk and AI-Assisted Tools (Source E 2025-26)

- SEO Gets MCP connects Search Console data to Claude, so query-level metrics can feed opportunity analysis and striking-distance keyword selection. It is used to build the targets for title and intro rewrites. [L1269.038]
- Firecrawl integration scrapes the current titles, meta data and intro text of blog pages. It is used to compare current content with the new rewrites before any change is made. [L1269.039]
- Matrix 5 is a Shopify app for bulk imports and is useful for large catalogs. Meta fields prepared in a spreadsheet can be imported in one batch instead of editing each page by hand. [L1269.040]
- Shop by Markets is a Shopify app that handles canonical and hreflang setup for market or language storefronts well. Use it only when the store runs localized subfolders or markets, such as /en-gb or /fr-fr. A single-market store with a standard domain can skip this step. [L1269.048]

### Ubersuggest: Access and Cost

- A free Ubersuggest account is not enough. You must sign up for an account to access the good data. [L1486.013]
- At the time of recording (2021-22, older pricing), the paid plan cost about USD 7 per month or about AUD 10 per month. [L1486.013]
- If the monthly cost is a concern, subscribe only for the market research phase and cancel afterwards. [L1486.014]
- Keeping the subscription is recommended, because it stays useful for ongoing marketing and keyword research after the store is built. [L1486.014]

### Running a Keyword Search in Ubersuggest

1. Open Keyword Ideas. [L1486.016, L1486.028]
2. Enter the keyword in the search field. [L1486.016]
3. Choose the country from the dropdown before clicking Search, because volumes are country-specific. [L1486.016, L1486.017]
4. Click Search. [L1486.016]
5. Read the results in the tabs SUGGESTIONS, RELATED, QUESTIONS and PREPOSITIONS. [L1486.016]
6. Export the list: click EXPORT TO CSV to download the keyword list with its volumes. [L1486.028]
7. Use the Filters button and the COMPARISONS tab, next to the export button, to narrow and compare keyword sets. [L1486.028]

### Reading the Keyword Columns

| Column | What it shows | Use |
|---|---|---|
| KEYWORD | The keyword suggestion | The list to work through |
| VOL | Estimated searches per month in the selected country [L1486.017] | Demand for the keyword |
| CPC | Typical cost per click for the keyword [L1486.017] | Planning paid ads later [L1486.017] |

### Assessing Competition with the Keyword Overview

- The Keyword Overview panel lists the Google top 10 results for a keyword, with their backlinks and domain authority. [L1486.018]
- The average values for the top 10 pages indicate how hard the keyword is to rank for and how much competition there is. [L1486.018]

### Trends and Difficulty

- Trends shows how search volume for a keyword changes over time. [L1486.027]
- Trends can show whether demand for a keyword is rising or falling. [L1486.027]
- Difficulty helps find keywords that are easier to rank for. [L1486.027]

#### Further details

- The Yoast SEO plugin for WordPress checks each article against SEO steps and shows a green or red light, for example whether the keyword appears in the headline. Its fit with Shopify was not confirmed in the lesson. [L0382.038]

- For keyword research, use free tools such as Google Keyword Planner or Ubersuggest, which provide search volume data without a subscription (2020 information; verify current access). [L0468.086]

- Ubersuggest is a lower-cost alternative to Ahrefs that can produce most of the same keyword, competitor and backlink reports needed for the skyscraper method. [L1388.002]

## Store Building: Themes, Page Builders and Store Settings

### Shopify plan and admin basics

- Under Settings > Plan the current Shopify plan is shown, and it can be upgraded or downgraded there. Paid app subscriptions are managed in the subscriptions section of the same area. [L1306.006]
- For a new store the Shopify basic plan is the recommended starting plan, and it can be changed later as needs grow. [L1306.007]
- Shopify Plus is only for high-volume stores with complex functionality. It is expensive and not needed for the stores this course builds. [L0964.082] (2023)
- Shopify hosts the store and offers a 14-day free trial. The Shopify back end shows visitor and session data for analysing traffic and conversion. [L1444.056]
- A Shopify partner offer from November 2024 gave three days free, then three months at 1 USD per month for a new account, a total of 3 USD. Check the current Shopify terms before relying on it, because promotional deals change. [L0807.007] (2025)
- Shopify includes SSL. A separate SSL check or certificate purchase is not needed; check the website's HTTPS status in Shopify. [L0934.174] (2023)
- Shopify also covers email marketing, order tracking, live chat and visitor analytics inside the same platform, so separate tools are not required for these functions. [L1062.007]
- Under Sales channels only Online Store should be shown by default. If Point of Sale appears and the store does not sell in person, remove it via the three-dot menu and Uninstall. [L1306.021]
- Installed apps appear automatically under Settings > Apps and sales channels as sales channels. The list grows over time; on a new online-only store only Online Store should be shown. [L1306.025]
- The store is created first as a demo and made public by removing its password. [L0393.019]
- A password-protected store cannot run as a live store. The storefront password step only matters for demo or development stores. A live store should not sit behind a password once Smart SEO is set up. [L1337.011]

### Admin workflow: files and preview

- Content > Files holds all uploaded images in one place. The file picker in each section draws from this list, so upload first to save time. [L1309.053]
- The theme customizer has a live desktop preview and a separate phone preview. Use the phone preview after each edit to catch layout problems early. [L1309.054]

### Choosing a theme

- The Shopify Theme Store (themes.shopify.com) lists free and paid themes with search and filter tools. Paid themes typically cost around 180 USD (one example was listed at 180 USD) and usually include more built-in features and design flexibility than free themes. Older advice from 2021 to 2022. [L1292.006]
- Free theme or paid theme, by use case:

| Situation | Source statement | Ids |
|---|---|---|
| Product testing | A basic free Shopify theme is acceptable if the required sections are on the page. | L1194.034 |
| Comparison test | The Source F theme is a free Shopify theme used as the comparison for a PageFly-built page. | L0773.011 |
| Full store, many features | A premium theme may include most features that otherwise need separate apps (see below). | L0709.041 |

- A premium Shopify theme was described as including a bundle builder with a discount for adding more items, upsells, cross-sells, a shipping calculator, free shipping options, a quiz, color swatches, infinite scroll, voice product search, a timeline section and custom headings. Stores that need these features often pay for them as separate apps, so checking the theme first can cut monthly app costs. [L0709.041]
- Some premium themes can be paired with a store mobile app that works with the theme. This is an option for stores that want their own app next to the website. [L0709.043]
- Feature requests can be collected by letting customers vote through a form that asks which feature they need and why. If enough people request the same feature, the theme developer builds it into the product. [L0709.040]
- Conversion apps are often billed monthly, for example around 20 USD per month each. A premium theme is a single one-time payment that can be customized without limit. Replacing several apps with theme sections reduces recurring costs. [L1330.002]
- Prefer a Shopify theme with a product page you can lightly edit over page builder apps, which often slow the site down and add work. If a page builder is unavoidable, Zipify Pages costs about 80 USD per month, and PageFly is free to start. Older advice from 2019. [L1110.025]
- Two themes were named as options: the Source A theme and themes from Out of the Sandbox. Custom features such as a related products section or a dynamic checkout button usually need edits to the base theme code. Older advice from 2019. [L0356.028]
- Debutify is a Shopify theme and store solution with a plug-and-play setup for quick launches. It is aimed at owners who want to skip long website development. [L1251.031]
- The Shopify theme Shrine has quantity discounts built in, so no extra app is needed for bundles. Use the theme's built-in quantity discount settings instead of installing a separate discount app. [L1316.039]

### Store-builder tools

- Elixir is the store builder used to create high-converting stores quickly. A full build guide of about 45 minutes covers the store setup. [L0491.018]
- Elixir is the tool the Source D program uses to build the Shopify store before it is submitted for review. [L1452.010]
- ClickFunnels builds product-specific landing pages with payment processing included. It was used with a free plus shipping offer and a follow-up upsell to the store. [L0643.040]
- ClickFunnels is a tool for other business models and is not part of the dropship store setup. You do not need to build a dropship store on it. Older advice from 2021. [L0968.113]
- Sources disagree on ClickFunnels. L0643.040 uses it as an optional landing page for a free plus shipping offer with upsell. L0968.113 says it is not needed for the dropship store itself. Use it only for a landing page offer; the store does not depend on it. [L0643.040, L0968.113]

### Shrine theme setup (sequence from the units)

1. Create the account on the register page with an email address, a password and a password confirmation, then click Create Account. Users who already have an account choose Sign in on the same page instead. [L1330.023]
2. License keys follow the pattern SHRINE-PRO- followed by two five-character groups, for example SHRINE-PRO-4AP05-2X84Z. They arrive by email from a noreply sender with the subject Your Shrine License Keys. Enter them under Add key in the Shrine dashboard to activate the license. [L1330.022]
3. Whitelist your URLs under Keys > Accounts > Whitelist your URLs. The form has three fields:
   - Main URL, for example https://yourstore.com
   - Secondary URL (Optional), for example https://yourstore.co.uk
   - Shopify URL, for example https://store.myshopify.com
   [L1330.008]
4. Every URL entered in the whitelist must start with https://. Entering it without this prefix can cause the whitelisting to fail. [L1330.009]
5. Find the Shopify myshopify.com URL under Settings > Domains in the Shopify admin, in the domain list at the top. The custom domain of the store appears in the same list. [L1330.011]
6. Whitelist both the Shopify myshopify.com URL and the custom domain of the store in Shrine. This is required to manage the Shrine account and its keys. [L1330.010]
7. Click Save and generate token. The token is required for the theme download. Save it somewhere safe; losing it means the access steps have to be repeated. [L1330.012]

### Theme code editing

Procedure for finding and editing code in the theme:

1. In Shopify, go to Online Store > Themes, click the three dots next to Customize, and choose Edit code. [L0667.037]
2. Use Filter files to search the theme folders, but note that it may not find text inside files. [L0667.037]
3. Open each file one at a time and press Cmd+F on Mac or Ctrl+F on PC to find the text. [L0667.037]
4. Before editing or deleting anything in the code editor, copy the entire original code into a document so you can restore it. [L0667.037]

- Custom liquid is a block for inserting custom code into the product page. It is an advanced feature that is not needed at the start. [L0976.041]

### Page builders: choice and testing

- Test page builders in this order:
  1. Start with PageFly.
  2. If no results appear, switch to Source F Team.
  [L0209.009]
- Use only PageFly and Source F Team for page building. Ecomposer has not been tested operationally. [L0209.011]
- Ecomposer charges a monthly subscription. PageFly's free tier eliminates this cost. [L0209.012]
- PageFly offers a free version covering one store, with included tutorials for all core functions. [L0209.010]
- PageFly is a page builder for creating product and landing pages, used for split tests against the standard store page. Prestige and Refresh were the page builders the source recommended most. [L0388.015]
- PageFly is a free landing page builder used to create product pages for split-testing against a Shopify theme. [L0773.011]
- PageFly is free and makes sense for single-product tests. For a general store selling several items with a multi-buy offer, a landing page may not be necessary. [L1212.038]
- PageFly suits single-product storytelling pages for emotional or niche products. It is recommended over a standard collection page setup. [L1158.049]
- PageFly and Replo build landing pages without code. They work for prototypes but usually cannot handle complex features such as bundle builders. [L0709.034]
- PageFly and Jam Page can rebuild a product page. Any builder works as long as the required sections are present on the page. [L1194.033]
- Page 5 (PageFly) was used as the landing page builder instead of GemPages, because it was described as better and cheaper. [L1218.022]
- Gem Pages or PageFly are product page builder options. Loox reviews offers a two-week free trial for product reviews. [L0820.036]
- Learning PageFly's basic workflow takes about one hour, so plan a separate block of time for it when building a store. [L1017.043]
- Gem Pages and PageFly are store design apps. The owner should learn how to use them before delegating store design. [L1358.011, L1360.012]
- PageFly has ready-made product page templates. Use one to assemble a landing page with the sections a product page needs: headline, lifestyle images, trust badges, star ratings and short bullet copy. [L1382.069]
- Use PageFly's manual layout editing when a theme or template does not show a banner or section correctly. [L1219.022]
- PageFly includes live chat support, even on the free plan. Support is reliable for troubleshooting page build issues, styling problems and design questions. Contact them through the PageFly app dashboard when stuck, instead of resorting to workarounds. [L0321.044]

### PageFly layout and styling

- In PageFly, click Plus, then Layout, to insert pre-built rows with 1, 2, 3 or more columns. Each row stacks on top of the previous one, and its columns sit side by side on desktop. [L1462.002]
- Select a row by clicking its name in the breadcrumb at the top of the editor. Set mobile columns per line on the row, and set the desktop column count in the row's General tab. [L1462.003]
- To make identical elements match in one step:
  1. Copy the style from one element (right-click or the style menu).
  2. Paste the style to all other identical elements.
  
  This updates colors, sizing and formatting across the page at once and is faster than editing each element individually. [L0321.024]
- A hex color is a six-character code with a leading hash that encodes red, green and blue values. A Chrome color picker can read any on-screen color and copy its hex code for PageFly color fields. [L1462.023]
- To set transparency with custom CSS, open the Advanced section at the bottom of the element settings and add this line, where 0 is fully transparent and 1 is fully opaque:

```css
opacity: 0.5;
```
[L1462.047]

- PageFly's slideshow arrow colors may not be editable in the editor. Ask PageFly support for a custom code snippet to recolor the arrows. [L1462.051]
- Joy Pixel is a browser extension that inserts emoji and icon symbols into PageFly text fields and buttons without leaving the editor. Use it for call-to-action icons, benefit bullets or section headers instead of searching external icon libraries. [L0321.043]

### AI page builders and visuals

- Instant AI Page Builder builds listicle pages, which work as pre-landers that warm up customers before the product page. [L1327.038]
- Shopify Sidekick theme generator template sections can build the comparison chart or other AI-generated visuals directly into the theme, instead of making them ad hoc. [L0886.022]
- When an AI tool turns a page section into a visual, it can pick up fonts, colours and style from the live store, so a basic request can already give on-brand output. A more specific request that names colours, fonts and the desired look gives better results. Download the finished visual and upload it to the site, or have the theme build it as a section. [L0886.034]

### Product page elements

Tabs:

- EasyTabs creates tabbed sections on product pages, so content such as product description, shipping policy, warranty information and return policy sits in separate clickable tabs. Older advice from 2020. [L0245.032]
- Product page tabs are built with the EasyTabs app in the theme. Set up and check the tabs for product description, specifications, shipping details and Why Buy From Us. Older advice from 2021 to 2022. [L1145.038]
