- Easy Tabs by NexusMedia adds tabs to product pages in three ways: standard tabs built from the product description, static tabs shown on all products or selected collections, and integration with third-party review apps. It also includes a Layout section for padding, colors, borders, typography, responsive behavior and custom CSS. Older advice from 2021 to 2022. [L1300.010]

Sticky add-to-cart:

- Sticky Add to Cart is a Shopify app (find it in the Shopify App Store) that keeps the add-to-cart button visible on mobile and while the visitor scrolls. The lesson reports about a 25 percent increase in add-to-cart rate after adding it. [L1259.022, L1260.035, L1345.056]

Video and third-party embeds:

- For product page videos, embed from YouTube rather than uploading directly. Suppliers often provide YouTube links; use the embed code in the Shopify product description. Older advice from 2022. [L0272.018]
- For app integrations such as scarcity timers (VitaLabs, Countdown Timer) and product reviews (LAI product reviews), place the app's HTML code manually instead of using automatic plugin integration. Automatic integrations often display elements in undesired locations or break page layouts. Copy the HTML the app provides and paste it into the page builder (PageFly, Shopify) to control exact placement. If integration issues persist and consume time, contact app support rather than building workarounds. [L0307.031]

Complementary products:

- The complementary products block recommends items such as paddles, leashes or cases. It needs the Search & Discovery app to link the products you want to show. It is not needed until the store sells products with accessories. [L0976.042]

Filters and price tags:

- Shopify apps for product filtering are available for most requirements. If the theme does not include a filter, install an app rather than building a custom feature. Older advice from 2020. [L0664.077]
- To find a filter app for an All products page, search the Shopify App Store for filter and compare the options, since no single app is clearly the standard. Some stores skip a filter app and rely on tags instead. [L0666.059] (2024)
- A savings percent tag next to a sale price depends on the theme, and the method differs per theme. On the Source A theme it is an option in settings. On other themes, check the theme creator's documentation or search for code. Older advice from 2020. [L0664.078]

### Shipping and access rules

- Do not place a shipping app with a UPS account unless you offer carrier-based shipping rates, because free shipping is typically included in the price. Older advice from 2020. [L0664.079]
- Shopify shipping profiles set default shipping rates and options for specific products without a third-party app. Use them to offer free standard delivery and upgrades such as white glove delivery. Older advice from 2021. [L0853.095]
- Excluding products or collections in specific countries on Shopify may need custom code, since it may not be a built-in function. Contact Shopify support to confirm available options before you plan a launch in multiple countries. Older advice from 2020. [L0961.080]
- To restrict collections, search the Shopify App Store for password protected collection access or wholesale password apps. These lock collections or products until a password is entered. Older advice from 2021. [L0962.039]

### Apps: cost and integration

- An urgency app and a review app are installed on Shopify to add urgency cues and customer reviews to product pages. A financing app is applied for to offer buy-now-pay-later style payment options at checkout. [L0393.020]
- Custom features and app choices should follow the cost rule above: check the theme first, then add apps only for what the theme lacks. [L0709.041, L1330.002]

### Translation and new markets

Procedure for activating a new market with the Shopify Translate app:

1. Use the Shopify Translate app instead of building a new store.
2. Activate a new market, for example the UK, Australia or another market.
3. Translate the store.
4. Run English ads to that market with a budget of about 100 euros to see if performance improves.

[L1161.041]

- Shopify Translate & Adapt is a free app in the Shopify App Store, rated 4.6, for translating the store and adapting content to international audiences. It is listed as an option when adding store languages. [L1250.017]
- Adding translations improves cross-border conversion by an average of 13 percent, according to the lesson. [L1327.042]

## Product Upload, Supplier Data and Fulfillment Apps

### Supplier Directories, Sourcing Search and Outreach

- Source A membership includes a free drop shipping supplier directory in the Bonuses section. Log in with your membership credentials and do not buy external drop shipping directories. [L0276.044]
- TSNN.com is a worldwide trade show directory. Search by keyword or industry, open the trade show websites, and use their exhibitor lists to find manufacturers and suppliers to contact. *(Older, 2021.)* [L0962.044]
- The AliExpress camera (image search) finds matching products from a photo. Use it on product images from a competitor store to find the same items on AliExpress. [L0388.018]
- AliTool is a Chrome extension that runs an image search on AliExpress from a competitor's product image. Use it to find the AliExpress listing and supplier price without searching manually. [L1174.033]
- Taobao is a Chinese marketplace linked to AliExpress that recently added English conversion inside the app. Sorting books by highest number of purchases gives a usable demand signal, since purchase volume counts even on a Chinese platform. [L1195.060]
- Avoid Spocket for US suppliers. Overlo, which Shopify once promoted, was shut down and is also not recommended. [L0964.081]

#### Supplier contact plan tool

1. Open your completed master supplier list. [L0936.003]
2. Open the supplier contact plan tool through the link below the lesson. [L0936.003]
3. In the top section, enter your name, company name (include the LLC name if you formed one), store name, store URL, business phone number and email address. Use the email format firstname@yourstorename.com. [L0936.003]
4. In the bottom section, enter the supplier company name, supplier website, supplier phone number (if available) and supplier tier. [L0936.003]
5. Set the supplier tier to Silver. [L0936.003]
6. Click the button labeled generate my contact plan and wait a few seconds. [L0936.003]
7. Review the day-by-day schedule, which lists what to say and what to do each day. [L0936.003]
8. Click the button labeled download contact plan before contacting the first supplier, to keep a saved copy. [L0936.003]
9. For each further supplier, create another plan in the tool. Your top-section details are stored, so you only replace the supplier fields. [L0936.003]

### Preparing Supplier Product Data

- Converting supplier product data into CSV files for upload to the store is the most time-consuming part of store operation. *(Older, 2022.)* [L0969.091]
- A supplier CSV is not synced automatically with the store. Prices, images and stock must be updated by hand or by re-uploading the reformatted file. *(Older, 2019.)* [L1069.028]
- Use Google Sheets, Excel or Apple Numbers to edit CSV files before importing into Shopify, and download the result as a CSV. Use Excel for very large files. [L0964.079]
- ChatGPT can write spreadsheet formulas for Google Sheets or Excel. Upload the spreadsheet, describe the result you want, and it returns the formulas or a reformatted spreadsheet. [L0667.048]
- Canva is a simple tool for editing documents such as spec sheets, for example to remove supplier contact details. [L0808.049]

#### Manual preparation of a supplier spreadsheet (older, 2019)

1. Take the spreadsheet the supplier sends. [L1069.027]
2. Rename or rearrange the columns to match the Shopify product CSV standard. [L1069.027]
3. Check that images, prices and inventory data are included in the file. [L1069.027]
4. Upload the file to the store. [L1069.027]

If all required data is present, this takes less than an hour. If images must be collected product by product from the supplier's website, the work takes far longer. *(Older, 2019.)* [L1069.027]

### ChatGPT CSV Reformatting

#### Procedure (version A)

1. Obtain the 'reformat.csv product upload prompt'. [L0106.007]
2. Open ChatGPT or your preferred AI tool. [L0106.007]
3. Attach both files: the supplier's original CSV and Shopify's blank template CSV. [L0106.007]
4. Paste the complete prompt text. [L0106.007]
5. Send for processing (typically 2-3 minutes). [L0106.007]
6. Review the generated import-ready checklist, which notes AI assumptions (for example, inventory set to 0 if missing from source data). [L0106.007]
7. Ask ChatGPT to correct any assumptions before download if needed. [L0106.007]
8. Download the converted spreadsheet, ready for Shopify upload. [L0106.007]

#### Procedure (version B, same workflow)

1. Copy the provided AI prompt and paste it into ChatGPT. [L0128.010]
2. Attach the supplier's CSV and Shopify's template CSV as files. [L0128.010]
3. Run the prompt. [L0128.010]
4. Review the AI's output CSV and assumptions checklist. [L0128.010]
5. Ask the AI to update any missing or incorrect fields. [L0128.010]
6. Refine until the CSV is fully correct. [L0128.010]

#### Example

- Converting a misformatted supplier spreadsheet to Shopify template format took 2 minutes 26 seconds. The AI flagged assumptions made during conversion (inventory defaulting to 0, vendor field left blank) in an import-ready checklist, allowing manual correction before final download and Shopify upload. [L0106.011]

#### Principle: refine before downloading

- After ChatGPT converts a supplier CSV to Shopify format, keep asking ChatGPT to refine the spreadsheet within the same conversation rather than downloading immediately. [L0106.013]
- Address multiple flagged assumptions together, for example 'set inventory to [quantities]' and 'fill vendor with [brand name]', before downloading the final version. [L0106.013]
- This iterative refinement through ChatGPT resolves most CSV issues without requiring post-download manual spreadsheet editing. [L0106.013]

### Bulk Editing Tools

- Bulk Edit Shopify Products is a free or paid app for bulk tagging, bulk editing and CSV management without exporting and reimporting files. [L0253.031]
- Bulk tag editor apps such as Power Tools can change product tags across many Shopify products at once. Before installing one, read recent reviews and search community reports, because some users have reported that the app blocked editing of their products. *(Older, 2024 entry; treat as a current warning.)* [L0856.124]
- The Able Star Bulk Product Editor is a Shopify app that can write product descriptions from basic product details using ChatGPT. [L0970.093]
- Matrixify is an Excel-based app for bulk product uploads and updates, collections and metafields. When checked in this lesson it had 549 reviews and a 4.8 star rating. [L0667.047]
- Matrixify is a Shopify app for bulk importing and exporting products, collections and other store data through spreadsheets. It is useful for managing large catalogs. [L0675.003]

#### Bulk price changes

- Bulk price changes can be done by exporting the product CSV from Shopify, editing it, and importing it back to update everything at once. Discount scheduling can be done inside Shopify without extra apps. [L1283.087]

### Outsourcing Uploads

#### Fiverr

- Fiverr is a freelance marketplace for outsourcing Shopify product uploads. Freelancers post services with transparent pricing, star ratings and product counts. [L0106.003, L0255.051]
- Example: a freelancer with a 4.9-star rating from 204 reviews offers 50-product uploads for $10. Fiverr Shopify product upload pricing starts at $10 per service. [L0106.003, L0255.050]
- You provide the supplier's product data. The freelancer handles store uploads while you maintain security by issuing a collaborator code instead of login credentials. [L0106.003]
- Search for 'Shopify product uploads' and review ratings and pricing. [L0255.051]

#### Procedure for outsourcing on Fiverr

1. Find a service provider with good reviews. [L0255.052]
2. Record a video of your process using Loom.com. [L0255.052]
3. Send the Loom video to the freelancer so they understand your exact process. [L0255.052]
4. The freelancer uploads products according to your method. [L0255.052]
5. Double and triple check their work before publishing. [L0255.052]
6. Verify that no information is mixed up between products. [L0255.052]

#### Other hiring options

- Upwork and onlinejobs.ph are used to hire people for product uploads or customer service. Check their work, since outsourced uploads can carry pricing mistakes. *(Older, 2022.)* [L0854.075]
- If the format is unfamiliar or the upload volume is large, a freelancer can be hired to handle CSV bulk uploads. [L0966.070]
- If a freelancer needs more than about two hours to upload a complete supplier CSV with images to Shopify, the work is taking too long and the scope or quote should be questioned. *(Older, 2019.)* [L1069.029]

### Native Shopify Import and Troubleshooting

- Shopify does not need an extra app or connector to import supplier products. A CSV in Shopify's product format can be uploaded directly through the Shopify admin. [L1114.072]
- Shopify's help pages on importing and exporting products cover Using CSV files, Importing products, Exporting products and Solutions to common import problems. Check the troubleshooting section when a CSV import fails. *(Older, 2021-22.)* [L1166.043]
- If the data comes from the Oberlo app, follow Shopify's separate guide on dropshipping with Oberlo instead of the standard CSV steps. *(Older, 2021-22.)* [L1166.043]

#### If a large CSV fails to upload

1. Split the file into smaller files. [L0964.080]
2. Upload the first 200 rows first and test. [L0964.080]
3. Upload the rest in smaller parts. [L0964.080]
4. If it still fails, contact Shopify live chat. [L0964.080]

### Stock and Price Sync

#### Approaches

| Approach | When the sources use it | Sources |
|---|---|---|
| Manual stock checks | Check inventory on the 20 percent of products that sell consistently, not all listings. | [L0964.076] |
| Supplier-specific sync app | Check the Shopify app store for a tool built for your supplier, because each supplier uses a different inventory system. No universal app updates stock from every supplier. *(Older, 2021.)* | [L0469.063] |
| Multi-supplier sync app | Stock Sync updates Shopify stock from supplier feeds, including weekly spreadsheet uploads, and can keep stock accurate across multiple suppliers. | [L1115.053] |
| Stocksync | Keeps product inventory synced with supplier stock levels, which helps prevent selling items that are out of stock. | [L1105.009] |
| Spreadsheet import app | When a supplier sends messy Excel or CSV files, search the Shopify App Store for an app that imports spreadsheet stock levels. Several apps do this, so compare their features before installing one. *(Older, 2021.)* | [L1112.076] |

- The sources differ on whether one app fits all: one says no universal app exists, while another describes a sync app that works across several suppliers. Use the supplier-specific option when the supplier has its own tool, and a multi-supplier app when you manage several feeds. [L0469.063, L1115.053]
- Without a sync app, a supplier CSV does not update the store on its own. [L1069.028]

#### Troubleshooting sync apps

- If a product sync app sends repeated 'file format not supported' emails from Shopify, message its live chat while logged into the store admin. These apps usually sync through an API, so the support team can check the account settings. [L1097.023]

#### Price monitoring

- Price-monitoring services track competitor and supplier prices and send alerts when prices change. They reduce manual checks of MAP compliance across many SKUs. [L0806.045]

### Fulfillment and Dropshipping Apps

#### Fulfillment providers

- Unified Dropshipping is a verified fulfillment agent. Minimum order quantities range from 100 to 1,000 units depending on product cost: higher-priced items have lower MOQs. Access is through Skype with a referral introduction. [L0309.019]
- Other fulfillment companies mentioned as options are Unified Drop Shipping, USA Drop and Zendrop. The lesson only demonstrates CJ Drop Shipping and Hyper Rescue. [L0685.005]
- CJ Dropshipping is used as a product source and fulfillment supplier, with a processing time of about three days and delivery of about two weeks. AliExpress is used to source products and compare suppliers, and it often has more variety. [L1444.055]
- DSers and CJ Dropshipping can set up automatic size conversion on the back end, so the supplier ships a size that fits the customer's order. Check whether your supplier and app combination supports this setting before relying on it. [L1128.015]

#### Installing a fulfillment app

1. Go to Apps > Sales channel settings > Shopify App Store. [L0635.013]
2. Search for the app, for example CJ Dropshipping or Hyper Rescue. [L0635.013]
3. Click Install. [L0635.013]
4. Create the supplier account when the app prompts you. [L0635.013]

#### AutoDS

AutoDS is an automation app that lists supplier products in the Shopify store, imports them and fulfills incoming orders automatically, notifying the supplier when an order arrives. It can map add-on variants so that orders from multiple items are sent to the supplier together. [L0635.028, L0577.028]

It works as an automation system rather than a support system, so it offers little help with refunds, returns or damaged packages. A fulfillment company app with live agent support is preferred for those cases. [L0635.028]

**Setup: connecting the store and importing products**

Method A: Chrome extension

1. Search Google for 'AutoDS dropshipping helper' and add that extension to Chrome. [L1297.002]
2. After installing the app, connect the store in AutoDS. Then install the AutoDS dropshipping helper Chrome extension. [L0477.002, L1297.002]
3. On the supplier product page, click the extension button that appears. [L1297.002]
4. Choose your Shopify store in the extension and click Save. [L1297.002]
5. Wait for the import. The product then appears under Drafts in AutoDS. [L0477.002, L1297.002]

Method B: single product by URL

1. In AutoDS, click Add products at the top left, then Single product. [L1297.003]
2. Choose your store and click Next. [L1297.003]
3. Paste the supplier product URL. [L1297.003]
4. Click Publish to store. [L0477.002, L1297.003]

**Editing a product in AutoDS before import (saves time)**

Before importing, edit the product in AutoDS:

1. Open the draft with the arrow on the left side. [L1297.004]
2. Set the title (from a prepared list), collection and tags. [L0477.003, L1297.004]
3. Delete the default description and paste your prepared description. [L0477.003, L1297.004]
4. Make sure monitoring is on for stock, price and auto order. [L0477.003, L1297.004]
5. Deselect variants you do not want (for example the UK or EU plug) and variants that are out of stock. [L0477.003, L1297.004]
6. Select which images to import, and add more with add image if needed. [L0477.003, L1297.004]
7. Check the item specifications. [L1297.004]
8. Click Save and import. The product then appears on the store product page. [L0477.003, L1297.004]

**Shipping methods and automatic fulfillment**

- Shipping methods are found under Settings > Lister and offer three options: cheapest, cheapest with tracking, and fastest with tracking. Pick the option your supplier actually offers. The other settings can be left alone at the start. [L0477.004, L1297.005]
- Under Settings > Orders, turn on both boxes so that orders are marked as fulfilled by AutoDS automatically. The question mark icons next to these options explain what happens. [L0477.004, L1297.007]

**Funding automatic fulfillment**

- Automatic fulfillment is paid from a balance, so the account needs funds. Either link a credit card or top up the balance under Balance, because each automatically fulfilled order is charged to it. [L0477.005, L1297.008]

**Notifications**

- Turn on all order notifications under Notifications: failed orders, tracking numbers received, new orders, and successfully processed orders. [L0477.006, L1297.009]
- Also turn on stock and price change notifications and set the email frequency to daily. [L0477.006, L1297.009]

### Middleman Apps: The Course's Position

- **Position A (direct supplier model):** Shopify dropshipping apps are not used or recommended in this model because they act as middlemen between the store and the supplier. The store should instead work with direct supplier relationships and the tools covered in the store setup. [L0360.087]
- **Position B (app-based fulfillment):** Source C builds the workflow around AutoDS, CJ Dropshipping and similar fulfillment apps, which automate listing, importing and order forwarding. [L0477.002, L0635.028, L1444.055]
- **When each applies:** Position A fits when you work with suppliers directly and want no intermediary between store and supplier [L0360.087]. Position B fits when you want orders forwarded automatically, accepting that the app is an automation system and not a support system, so refunds, returns and damaged packages need a fulfillment company app with live agent support [L0635.028].
- Paying for a supplier integration app is worth it only when the brand ranks high on competitor sites and sells well. Check the monthly price first and judge it against expected sales. [L0808.015]

### Order Forwarding and Supplier Order Emails

- An order forwarding app works independently of the store theme. A store can change its theme without changing its order forwarding setup. *(Older, 2019.)* [L0960.073]
- Shopify Email and Shopify automations may be able to send supplier order emails natively, but this is untested. A separate email platform such as Klaviyo can also handle this, so check both options before choosing one. [L0805.063]

### Freight and Shipping Quotes

| Tool | What the sources say | Sources |
|---|---|---|
| Freightcenter.com (quote aggregator) | Returns quotes from several carriers based on origin, destination, package size and weight. | [L0970.090] |
| Freightcenter.com (shipments and BOL) | Used to create freight shipments and generate the Bill of Lading (BOL) for supplier-shipped freight. | [L0805.057] |
| Freight Center (heavy products) | Enter a warehouse address or zip code and several destination zip codes to get estimates for shipping heavy products. | [L1115.058] |
| Freight Club and Freight Center | Described as freight carriers that quote the same carriers with different markups. Compare both quotes. *(Older, 2021.)* | [L0834.057] |
| Red Hawk and freightcenter.com | Provide shipping quotes that can be compared against supplier shipping prices. | [L0966.067] |
| Interparcel (Australian example) | Online freight broker for booking pickup and delivery of shipments you arrange yourself, with a quick quote showing price options. | [L1291.008] |

- Freight broker and freight quote sites, for example freightcenter.com, can reduce freight shipment costs considerably. The speaker reported savings of about 100 dollars or more per shipment. *(Older, 2022.)* [L0665.054]
- The sources describe Freight Center differently: as a quote aggregator for several carriers [L0970.090, L1115.058] and as a carrier whose quotes differ in markup from Freight Club [L0834.057]. Compare its quote with the other option before choosing a carrier. [L0834.057, L0970.090]

### Marketplaces and Print on Demand

- Shopify has built-in multi-channel selling that can list products on marketplaces such as eBay and Amazon. Before using it, check every brand's agreement, because many brands prohibit selling through third-party platforms. *(Older, 2020.)* [L0358.035]
- Printful is a large print on demand provider with a reputation for good product quality. It is an option if print on demand products are added to a store. [L1097.024]

## Product Options, Personalization and Digital Products

### Choosing an options app

- Shopify's built-in option system is limited to three option types per product, so products with variations that cannot be handled natively need an app. [L0853.112]
- *Older (2021):* Bold Product Options is the default for any product with variations that cannot be handled natively. [L0853.112]
- Shopify allows up to 100 variants per product. For more variants, or for clickable color swatches, use an app such as Infinite Product Options or SC Product Options. [L0837.056]
- *Older (2020):* Having many product options is not a problem by itself. [L0551.031]

| App | Use | Price and terms as stated | Units |
|---|---|---|---|
| Bold Product Options, Premium | Variants, bundles, options, conditional cross-sell | $19.99/month with 14-day free trial [L0240.012]; about $20/month with 14-day free trial [L1076.031] | L0240.012, L1076.031 |
| Bold Product Options, cheaper version | Options without conditional logic | About $10/month; lacks conditional logic, so do not use it for step-by-step cross-sell [L1076.032] | L1076.032 |
| Bold Product Options, shipping options | Shipping options and fixed shipping charges | About 5 USD/month. *Older (2020).* [L1277.064] | L1277.064 |
| SC Product Options | Upsells, variant options, swatches, cross-sell | $15/month in July 2025 [L0837.058] | L0837.058, L1083.007, L1116.018 |
| Infinite Product Options | More than 100 options and color swatches | About $13/month with 14-day free trial; July 2025: 4.8 stars from almost 2,000 reviews [L0837.057] | L0837.057 |
| Global Product Options | Free personalization fields | Usable on the free plan [L0634.041] | L0634.041, L0476.033 |
| Extend | Extended warranties on high-ticket products | Commission on warranty sales; provider handles fulfillment [L0251.044] | L0251.044 |

### Bold Product Options

- Bold Product Options is a Shopify app that creates product variants and bundling options. The Premium edition costs $19.99/month with a 14-day free trial. Plan 10-20 minutes of configuration per product and 30-60 minutes per week for analysis and optimization. [L0240.012]
- Bold Product Options adds checkboxes and drop-down menus to product pages. Use it to offer upgrades such as White Glove delivery on specific products only. *Older (2021).* [L0247.081]
- Bold Product Options is a Shopify app for creating customization options such as sizes, colors and add-ons like assembly services. It can be combined with Bold Product Upsell. [L0251.046]
- Bold Product Options is a Shopify app for managing many product options in a store. [L0551.031]
- Bold Product Options is a Shopify app for setting up product options simply, such as sizes, colors or add-on choices. [L0855.111]
- Bold Product Options is a Shopify app for adding custom product options, such as variants or personalization fields, to product pages. *Older (2021).* [L1089.004]
- Bold Product Options is a Shopify app for product add-ons and options, such as extra choices a customer can add to a product page. *Older (2021).* [L1090.056]
- Bold Product Options is used to display add-ons and product options on the product page. *Older (2020).* [L0468.082]
- Bold Product Options is listed as a resource in the December 2020 coaching call for adding product option selectors to a store. The lesson names it without describing its features. *Older (2020).* [L0426.010]
- Bold Product Options is an app that lets customers choose custom sizes and materials on a product page. Preset option values can update the price in real time, while free-text entry may not. Check with the app's support before relying on it. *Older (2019).* [L0467.048]
- Bold Product Options is an app that lets you add optional extras, such as bulbs, to a product page as a paid add-on. [L0894.036]

**Naming conflict:** Two 2024 sources disagree on which name came first. One says Bold Product Options was formerly SC Product Options [L0253.032]. The other says SC Product Options was formerly Bold Product Options [L0856.069]. Both names are used in this handbook as the sources use them.

**Pricing conflict:** The sources give different prices for different versions, so apply each figure only to the version it describes:
- $19.99 or about $20/month: the Premium version, with conditional logic and a 14-day free trial [L0240.012, L1076.031]
- About $10/month: the cheaper version without conditional logic. Do not use it for cross-sell [L1076.032]
- About 5 USD/month: shipping options and fixed shipping charges. *Older (2020).* [L1277.064]

### SC Product Options and Infinite Product Options

- SC Product Options is the Shopify app for product page upsells and variant options on product pages. [L0856.069]
- SC Product Options adds visual swatches to variant options on product pages, such as color circles or images chosen with a color picker. [L1116.018]
- SC Product Options cost $15/month in July 2025. It was the app used by the coaching team for more than 100 options with swatches. [L0837.058]
- SC Product Options is an app that displays other products on a product page so customers can add related items to their order, which is used for cross-sell bundles. It is quick to set up when you are already authorized to sell the items. [L1083.007]
- Infinite Product Options costs about $13/month with a 14-day free trial. In July 2025 it had 4.8 stars from almost 2,000 reviews and supports more than 100 options and color swatches. [L0837.057]

### Global Product Options and personalization fields

**Setup procedure A (free app, product scope set per product):**

1. Install the app from the Shopify App Store, scroll down and click Install app, then click Continue as free. [L0476.032]
2. Open the product you want to personalize, and click Add element. [L0476.032]
3. Choose Text field as the element type and set the field label (example: type in your names). [L0476.032]
4. Open advanced options and add a placeholder or help text showing the expected input, for example a list of sample names. The help text appears below the field. [L0476.032]
5. Click Next. [L0476.032]
6. Choose which products get the option. Pick All if the store sells only one product. [L0476.032]
7. Open the theme editor, activate the app embed and click save. [L0476.032]
8. Check the product page to confirm the text field appears. [L0476.032]

**Setup procedure B (option sets, from a second lesson version):**

1. Install Global Product Options on the Shopify store. [L1220.024]
2. Open Option sets and create a new option set. [L1220.024]
3. Add a text box with a title, for example Name of boyfriend or girlfriend. [L1220.024]
4. Click Show advanced options. [L1220.024]
5. Add either a text placeholder (shown inside the box) or a help text (shown below the box) to explain what to type. [L1220.024]
6. Optionally add an image upload option. [L1220.024]
7. When an order comes in, forward the customer's text to the supplier exactly as typed. [L1220.024]

**Notes on the two versions:**
- Both procedures set up the same app. Follow the menu names your app version shows. [L0476.032, L1220.024]
- Example for a single-product store: label "Type in your names", with sample names such as Daniel, Sarah, David, Michelle as the help text, so customers see how it works. Set the product scope to All. Then activate the app embed in the theme editor and save. [L0634.028]
- Global Product Options adds custom input fields such as text fields to product pages, so customers can enter personalization details like names before they buy. [L0476.033]
- Global Product Options is a free Shopify app for personalization, such as a name field on jewelry. [L1220.023]

**Other personalization tools:**
- Global Variants is a Shopify app that allows product customization, such as choosing a mug handle, color or text, on a product page. It is used for personalized products rather than standard single-variant listings. [L1172.017]
- A free Shopify text-box or photo-upload customization app lets buyers enter a name, number, message or photo on a personalized product. The seller then forwards the order details to the supplier. (The lesson does not name the app.) [L1469.026]
- Bold Product Options adds text fields to product pages so customers can enter engraving or personalization text. The order arrives with that information, and the supplier engraves and ships the item, so you do not stock it. *Older (2021).* [L0962.026]

### Add-ons, upsells and cross-sells

**Setting up the offer:**
- Use the Shopify apps Product Accessories and Product Options by Bold Apps to add accessories and product options to product pages. These apps help raise AOV without changing the core product listing. [L0920.024, L1357.027]
- The Shopify apps Product Accessories and Product Options by Bold Apps work well for offering add-ons and product options. [L1357.027]
- Bold Product Options is the app used for cross-sell conditional logic on product pages. [L1076.031]
- Conditional logic example: if the customer is on Product A, show Product B; if they add B, show Product C. [L0253.032]
- Conditional logic is what makes the step-by-step cross-sell work. Do not use the cheaper version, which lacks it. [L1076.032]
- The premium version's 14-day free trial is worth using to test the setup before paying. [L1076.031]

**Upsell types:**
- Add-ons such as warranties, maintenance plans or accessories can be priced right on the product page. *Older (2022).* [L1281.091]
- Bold Product Options is a Shopify app that lets you add extras to a product page as a paid add-on. [L0894.036]

**Supplier-fulfilled and bundled items:**
- Bold Product Options adds bundled items or variant options to a product, so the extra item ships automatically with the main order. It is preferred over manual fulfillment when order volume grows. *Older (2019).* [L1002.029]

**Warranties via Bold Upsell (procedure):**

1. Create a product named extended warranty. [L1076.044]
2. Link it to the suppliers who provide the warranty. [L1076.044]
3. Add it as an upsell at checkout. The setup takes about 5 minutes. [L1076.044]

**Extended warranties with Extend:**
- Extend.com is a Shopify app for selling extended warranties on high-ticket products. You earn a commission on warranty sales, and the app provider handles warranty fulfillment. Multiple Source A members recommended it. [L0251.044]

**Special cases:**
- Bold Product Options can offer White Gloves delivery on specific products only. *Older (2021).* [L0247.081]
- *Older (2020):* Bold Product Options can show shipping options and fixed shipping charges on product pages. [L1277.064]

**Mistake to avoid:**
- Product option apps such as Bold may become incompatible with split-payment apps that let customers pay with two cards. Ask both app providers for a compatible integration rather than assuming one will work with the other. [L0470.094]

### Creating ebooks and digital bonuses

**Tools for writing and layout:**
- Claude (claude.ai) can generate a complete, professional ebook in minutes from a single prompt, so the store owner does not need to write the ebook manually. [L0860.020]
- Canva lays out the AI-generated ebook text with a simple ebook template and exports the finished file as a PDF for upload to Shopify. [L0860.021]
- Canva is used to design ebook covers and the layout of the PDF product. [L1131.049]
- ChatGPT generates ideas, lists and text for ebooks and offers. Give it concrete length targets, such as about 1000 words, to control output length. [L1131.047]

**Prompt for a dog-recipe ebook (use as given, fill in the placeholders):**

```
Create a list of 20 recipes for dogs that support [PROBLEM, e.g. gut health, skin, dental health or joint problems] using everyday ingredients that are easy to buy in a regular supermarket in [COUNTRY]. For each recipe, give a title, an ingredient list with amounts, three to five short preparation steps, and one sentence explaining why the ingredients suit this problem. Write in [LANGUAGE] and keep each recipe under 100 words, with a total length of about 1000 words for the whole list. Do not make medical claims or promise cures, and add a note that owners should check any recipe with a veterinarian.
```

[L1131.048]

**Sourcing ready-made content:**
- PLR sites are marketplaces that sell private-label-rights ebooks for about 2 dollars 70 cents each. They can be rebranded with your own logo and sold or given away as bonuses. [L0577.029]
- Free ebook websites provide educational ebooks that can be rebranded and offered as a bonus to customers. [L0650.050]
- Fiverr can be used to hire someone to rebrand a free ebook to match the store, for a low cost. [L0650.051]

### Delivering digital products

**Choosing a delivery app:**
- Digital Downloads is a free Shopify app made by Shopify. It delivers an uploaded file to the customer by email immediately after purchase. It can be used to sell a digital product alongside a bundle. [L0862.018, L0861.023]
- Digital Downloads is installed by searching for it in the Shopify App Store. [L0861.023]
- Digital Downloads automatically delivers a PDF or digital product after purchase, so it does not have to be emailed manually. [L1432.011]
- Digital Downloads works for a free ebook offered as a bonus with a physical product. [L1428.023]
- Install Digital Downloads only if the store sells digital files. The lesson's store installs it for its digital sales. [L1327.030]
- Digital Downloads has been used to sell and deliver digital files such as audiobooks, artwork and videos. [L0888.012]
- EDP (Easy Digital Products) appeared as an alternative to Digital Downloads in the Shopify App Store search, with a free plan available. Compare its features and reviews with Digital Downloads if the free delivery workflow needs more options. [L0861.044]

**Setup procedure (Digital Downloads):**

1. Install the app from the Shopify App Store by searching for "digital downloads." [L0862.019]
2. Open the e-book product in Shopify, creating it first if it doesn't exist yet. [L0862.019]
3. Inside the app, open the product, choose Add asset, then File. [L0862.019]
4. Upload the PDF. [L0862.019]
5. In the product's Shipping section, switch off "Physical product" so it reads "Not a physical product." [L0862.019]
6. Click Save. [L0862.019]
7. Configure the notification email that goes out when a digital product is ready for download, plus the email used for updates. [L0862.019, L0861.024]
8. Set how digital products are accessed and downloaded after checkout. [L0862.019, L0861.024]
9. Add the e-book product as the free gift in the Kaching Bundles bar. [L0862.019]

**Settings and plan requirement:**
- The Digital Downloads settings let you write the notification email for a ready digital product and the email for when a purchased digital product has been updated. They also let you choose how digital products are accessed and downloaded after checkout. [L0861.024]
- Adjusting the checkout is only possible on a Shopify plan. If the app shows "To customize your checkout, you need to select a Shopify plan," select a Shopify plan first. [L0861.024, L0862.020]

**Link products:**
- Digital Downloads can create a link product that sells access to externally hosted content, sold alongside the files attached to a product. Use this when the gift is an online course or membership area rather than a downloadable PDF. [L0861.043]

**Bonuses without a delivery app:**
- Use Google Drive links to PDFs, unlisted YouTube videos, or unlisted pages to deliver free digital bonuses without an app. Paid digital products with automated delivery need a Shopify app built for that purpose. [L0934.169]

**Delivering a digital bonus after purchase with Klaviyo (procedure):**

1. Create a flow in Klaviyo triggered by the purchase of the product. [L0934.170]
2. Add an email containing the access link. [L0934.170]
3. Use a Google Drive link to PDFs, an unlisted YouTube video, or an unlisted page as the link. [L0934.170]
4. Test the flow before going live. [L0934.170]

**Online classes (older):**
- *Older (2020):* For a weekly class sold online, create a Shopify digital product with a price, then edit the order confirmation email with access instructions. For a private online class, a private Facebook group with video streaming is an option. [L0664.076]

**Free gifts and subscriptions (Kaching):**
- Kaching Bundles is a Shopify app that can deliver a free digital gift, such as an ebook, bundled with a product. Upload the finished file to the app to use it as a product bonus. [L1323.013]
- Kaching Subscriptions adds subscription purchases. It works well with products like supplements. For a book store it is not needed at the foundation stage. [L1327.032]

**Fallbacks and risks:**
- A digital product such as a PDF bundle may not reach customers if no delivery app or order confirmation link is set up. Check whether delivery is automated before selling it, and check support emails for complaints. [L1432.012]
- Alternatives to a delivery app: place a download link to the PDF in the order confirmation so the buyer can click and download it immediately, or send each buyer a separate email with the PDF manually until automation is in place. [L1432.013]

## Design, Branding and Image Tools

### Choosing the design tool

- Canva is the fastest option for simple social posts, product graphics and quick exports. It ships with templates, animated icons, graphics, and stock photos and videos inside the app, so beginners can build without outside assets [L0754.001].
- Canva is weak at layering and blending. Shadows and gradients come only as presets, and you have little control over how one object blends into another. Plan designs that do not need deep blending when working in Canva [L0754.002].
- Canva has no pen tool and no real brush, and its draw tool does not auto-correct, so straight lines and custom hand-drawn shapes are hard to make. Build custom shapes in Illustrator or Photoshop [L0754.003].
- Photoshop works mainly with pixels and is built for image manipulation such as cutouts, colour grading and photo compositing. It can also create vector shapes, which is why many designers choose it when they can learn only one of the two programs [L0754.005].
- Illustrator creates scalable vector graphics from mathematical paths, so enlarging a logo or shape does not pixelate it. It is the right tool for logos, icons and custom drawn shapes [L0754.004].
- Choose the tool by the job: Canva for speed and ready-made elements, Photoshop for image manipulation and advanced compositing, Illustrator for custom graphics and shape building. One designer can combine all three and move files between them [L0754.006, L0754.064].
- Photoshop and Illustrator have a steep learning curve. Do not try to learn every tool first. Learn the basic controls, then look up the specific technique you need for the current problem [L0754.009, L0754.065].
- Older advice (Source B, 2021 to 2022): professional software has unnecessary complexity for basic ecommerce tasks such as resizing and cropping, so simpler online tools are more practical for non-designers [L0300.005].
- Online editor PicMonkey (older, 2021 to 2022): resizes, crops and adds text to images with no software installation or design experience needed. Free trial available; $7 per month for the basic plan [L0300.004].
- Snappa is a simple online tool for custom article images. It has a free version and a paid version with extra features, is much simpler than Photoshop, and includes a curated set of stock photos [L0382.037].
- Canva is browser-based. Its free plan is enough for basic store assets such as logos, favicons, banners and icons. Beginners without design training can use it; experienced designers can use Photoshop or another editor [L0342.001].

### Accounts, plans and costs

| Tool | Price or plan as stated | Notes | Ids |
|---|---|---|---|
| Canva free | $0 | Free plan is sufficient for demo product images and offers a drag-and-drop interface. Crown icons mark paid features. No built-in resize tool. | L0036.005, L0098.005, L0098.018, L0342.001 |
| Canva paid | About $10 to $12 per month, 30-day free trial | Adds more features, stock photos inside Canva, transparent-background PNG export, and resizing of existing designs. | L0098.005, L0342.002, L0518.003 |
| Canva trial | One use per account | After the trial, crown elements, transparent PNG downloads, resizing existing designs and stock photos inside Canva need a paid subscription. | L0342.039 |
| Photoshop, Canva, PhotoRoom | Roughly $10 per month each | Combined AI tool subscriptions add up quickly. | L0353.033 |
| PhotoRoom | About $10 per month or $49 per year | As stated in the lesson. | L0353.030 |
| Clipping Magic | About $5 per month | Background removal for cut-out product PNGs. | L0418.016 |
| Brandfetch free plan | $0 per month, 100 API requests | One website's brand data uses 1 to 2 requests. | L0322.002 |
| Placeit | Monthly subscription | Unlimited downloads of mockups. | L0577.026 |
| Bulk Resize | Free browser tool, no sign-up | Batch resize and compress. | L0418.017 |
| ImageOptim | Free, Mac desktop app | Older advice (2021 to 2022). | L0300.006 |
| Unsplash | Free | Free stock photos. | L0477.043 |
| Pixabay | Free | Royalty-free source. Older advice (2020). | L0468.083 |
| 123RF | $2 to $3 per photo | Use when supplier images are unavailable or low quality. Older figure (2021). | L0270.054 |
| Google Fonts | Free | Licensed for commercial use. | L0317.011 |

- Canva Pro is listed with a free 30-day trial [L0518.003].
- Crown-icon elements and Pro-only search results are hidden or unusable on the free plan [L0342.003].

### Gemini API key setup

Set up the key in Google AI Studio:

1. Open Get API key, then API Keys.
2. Click Create API key.
3. Choose the Gemini API Key option.
4. Select an existing project or create a new one.
5. Click Create key.
6. Copy the key from the API keys list when a tool needs it. [L0794.001]

- Treat the Gemini API key like a password. Never share it, paste it only into tools you control, and keep tool files private because the key is embedded in them. If a key leaks, revoke it in Google AI Studio and create a new one before running anything else [L0794.002].
- Downloading course HTML tool files from Google Drive: click the three-dot menu on each file and choose Download. Do not open the raw code. Download every file of the set before first use, then open the HTML file locally in a browser [L0794.004].
- Gemini image generation (Nano Banana) runs in the browser tools on the model gemini-3.1-flash-image-preview. Model names change. If a request returns a 404 error, check the Gemini API models documentation and update the model field in the tool [L0794.005].

Adding the key through a skill file (source procedure):

1. In Claude, open Settings > Customize skills, click Add, and add the image skill folder.
2. Open https://aistudio.google.com/apikey and sign in with a Google account.
3. Click Create API key.
4. Copy the key, which starts with AIza.
5. Open the SKILL.md file and find the API Config section.
6. Replace the placeholder line API KEY = 'YOUR GEMINI API KEY HERE' with your key, then save the file. [L0794.051]

- Conflict: the skill-file procedure above places the key inside a file. The same key placed in a skill file travels with that file, so anyone you share the skill with can run jobs on your key and your billing account. Use the file method only for a private skill that is never shared or uploaded. Otherwise enter the key in the tool's key field at run time and never place it inside a file you share or upload [L0794.051, L0794.052].

### Brand foundations: colours, fonts and brand kit

**Colour**

- Color Hunt (colorhunt.co) shows pre-made palettes. Search by colour name or browse trending palettes. Hover over a palette to see all its hex codes, ready for design tools and websites [L0317.004].
- Canva's free resource page at canva.com/learn/100-color-combinations offers curated palettes with hex codes, mood associations and design examples. Useful when unsure of brand colours. Older advice (Source B, 2021 to 2022) [L0323.014].
- Canva palettes are arranged around the hue at the top. Tint moves the hue toward white, tone moves it toward grey, and shade moves it toward black. Use these controls to soften a colour instead of choosing a random new one [L0754.018].
- Browser colour-picker extensions read the exact button colour from a store's website, so a pop-up button can match it [L0418.018].
- The Eyedropper in the Canva colour palette picks any colour from an image [L0754.017].
- Photoshop: press X to swap foreground and background colours and press D to reset them to black and white. Brushes, masks and gradients use these colours, so resetting avoids painting in the wrong colour [L0754.044].

**Canva Brand Kit**

- The Brand Kit is available on the free plan and allows three brand colours. Path: Brand Kit > Brand colours > add colours. Black and white can be used alongside them. Saved colours then appear under the text colour option, so exact codes do not need to be remembered [L0342.004].
- The Brand Kit lets you upload a logo, a colour scheme and fonts, then apply them to any design with one click. When a brand colour is missing, Canva can pull colours from an uploaded image [L0754.017].

**Fonts**

- Google Fonts (fonts.google.com) offers free fonts licensed for commercial use. Search by name or filter by style such as serif, sans-serif or display. Download and upload them to design tools or Shopify themes without licence risk [L0317.011].
- Canva's Text panel lets you search fonts by style, such as handwriting, corporate, display, serif, sans serif, luxury, elegant or vintage, and includes most popular Google Fonts. Speaker favourites named in the lesson were Lato, Montserrat, Open Sans and Rubik [L0754.026].
- Use Open Sans as a plain sans-serif font for readable ad copy, and swap in your brand font if you have one. Keep body copy in a clean sans-serif rather than decorative fonts so text stays legible on small screens [L0343.009].
- Use the brand serif font for packaging text so the packaging matches the brand's typography [L0884.036].

**Kittl brand guidelines**

- Kittl has free brand guidelines templates. Workflow: Templates > search "brand guidelines" > choose a free template > open it. In the left sidebar, open the Project Colors panel, click a colour, replace its hex code with your new one and press Enter. All instances of that colour update automatically [L0317.009].
- Hold Option (Mac) or Alt (Windows) and drag an element to duplicate it. This is faster than copy-paste when creating multiple texture variations in brand guidelines [L0317.023].
- Brand guidelines templates do not support batch font replacement across the document. Update each text instance individually and plan for manual font updates [L0317.024].
- The Brand Guidelines template shows project colours in code format. The lesson's screenshot shows the values 540863 and 24984o, which must be checked before use. The Browse Color Palettes option gives more palettes [L0884.030].

**Brandfetch MCP (brand data extraction)**

- Brand Fetch MCP is a data extraction tool. It has two main functions: get_brand returns the visual identity as JSON (logos, colours, fonts, social handles, company metadata), and get_brand_context returns voice, ICP, positioning, product catalogue, tagline, mission and target audiences [L0318.002].
- Brandfetch MCP is a Model Context Protocol connector for Claude. It extracts brand identity from any website: logo, hex colour codes, fonts, social media links and brand summary. It generates two files, brandkit.json and voice.md, in 5 to 7 minutes [L0322.001].
- Processing takes 5 to 7 minutes before the brand kit and voice files are returned [L0322.003].
- Naming difference: L0318.002 names the two functions get_brand and get_brand_context. L0322.015 lists six tools (Search Brands, Build Logo URLs, Identify Merchant from Transaction, Fetch Asset as Base64, Get Brand Details, Get Brand Context). The read-only tools extract logos, hex colours, fonts, social links and merchant data when you run the brand kit prompt [L0318.002, L0322.015].
- Competitor research: use Brand Fetch MCP to study competitor brands' positioning, colours and voice. Do not copy their visual identity or tone. Use it as competitive intelligence to understand market positioning [L0318.017].

Install in Claude (setting):

- Customize > Connectors > click the plus button > enter the name Brandfetch MCP > paste the MCP server URL from developers.brandfetch.com/dashboard into the Remote MCP server URL field > click Add > click Connect and authorise. [L0322.004]

Full setup procedure:

1. Go to brandfetch.com.
2. Click Join Brandfetch.
3. Click Are you a developer? Get API keys (not Get started).
4. Sign up via Google, GitHub or email to access developers.brandfetch.com/dashboard.
5. Go to Plans & Billing and confirm the free tier ($0 per month, 100 requests).
6. Go to Keys & MCP and copy the Brandfetch MCP URL.
7. Open your Claude Cowork Project.
8. Go to Customize > Connectors.
9. Click the plus button (Add custom connector).
10. Enter Brandfetch MCP as the name.
11. Paste the MCP URL into Remote MCP server URL.
12. Click Add.
13. Click Connect and complete authorisation. [L0322.005]

Verification after pulling data:

- If Brandfetch returns incomplete data or cannot detect a field such as the heading font, offer to have Claude visit the live website to verify and retrieve the missing information. This is faster than asking the user to supply details they may not recall [L0322.016].
- Verify in this order before saving the final brandkit.json file, and fix any discrepancies:
  1. Primary brand colour: show the hex code and ask the user to confirm it matches the colour on their main buttons.
  2. Heading or title font: if Brandfetch detected it, confirm with the user. If not, request it directly or retrieve it from the live site.
  3. Social media links: confirm each one (Instagram, Facebook, etc.) is current and correct. [L0322.017]

### Logos

- Canva is a simple option for creating a logo yourself when the design is basic [L0357.053, older: 2019].
- Canva's free plan is enough for logos, favicons and icons [L0342.001].
- Fiverr is the recommended platform for logo design at startup. It balances affordability with quality and offers a curated marketplace where you can browse portfolios, compare prices and read reviews before buying. Older advice (Source B, 2021 to 2022) [L0323.013].
- Filter Fiverr's logo section by your maximum budget (set to 100 USD in the source), desired style and delivery time. This narrows the 183,000+ available services to your needs [L0323.019].
- When evaluating Fiverr designers, prioritise those with 1,000+ completed jobs and 4.8+ star ratings. Designers with 50+ solid reviews are also dependable [L0323.020].
- Many Fiverr logo designers specialise in one aesthetic: minimalist, mascot, hand-drawn or vintage. With a specific style preference, search for designers who specialise in that style rather than generalists [L0323.027].
- Request a source file in Photoshop or Adobe Illustrator format from the designer, so the logo can be edited later without starting from scratch [L0323.018].

Fiverr logo procedure:

1. Choose three hex colour codes (primary, accent, button) and record them.
2. On Fiverr.com, navigate to Graphics & Design > Logo Design.
3. Filter by budget (under 100 USD) and review designer portfolios, ratings and reviews.
4. Click on a designer whose style you like and review their package details and revision policy.
5. Click Continue to proceed to checkout and provide your business name, colour codes, business description and target market feel.
6. Complete payment via credit card or PayPal.
7. Receive your logo files in the specified timeframe.
8. Request revisions if needed, then download and accept the final files. [L0323.021]

Building logos by hand:

- Illustrator's Shape Builder merges overlapping shapes into one outline when you drag across them. Hold Alt or Option while dragging to remove unwanted areas. It is the main method for building logos from several overlapping shapes [L0754.056].
- Use separate shapes for each part of a logo or icon, then combine them with the Shape Builder, rather than drawing the final outline in one go. This makes it easier to correct one part without redrawing the whole mark [L0754.063].
- Canva logo export: when downloading a logo, choose JPG as the file type and raise the quality slider from the default 80 percent to 100 percent. The default export size shown was 500 by 500 pixels [L0471.063, from 2024].
- For a logo on coloured backgrounds, a transparent PNG is the cleanest option. Make one by using a free online background remover (search for background image remover), which deletes a white background so the PNG becomes transparent [L0342.011].

### Product images: sourcing

- Use royalty-free image sources for product photos, such as Google's royalty-free image search or the free stock site Pixabay. These are safer than competitor photos. Older advice (Source A, 2020) [L0468.083].
- Google Image Search with the filter Labeled for commercial reuse (Settings > Usage rights > Labeled for commercial reuse) gives free, legally usable product images for demo stores without buying licences. Older advice (2020) [L0245.033].
- Stock photo route (older, 2020): start with Google Image Search, then refine with free royalty-free stock sites (20+ options listed in the source). You need at most 10 images for 5 demo products plus a homepage banner [L0268.056].
- Use Canva for product images and Google royalty-free image search for product images without people when Canva has none. Older advice (2021) [L0834.058].
- Unsplash offers free stock photos. For a discount pop-up, search a keyword such as gift. For a chat avatar, search man or woman. Download the image and upload it into the app [L0477.043].
- 123RF has millions of images at $2 to $3 per photo. Use it when supplier images are unavailable or low quality. Older figure (2021) [L0270.054].
- Snappa includes a curated set of stock photos [L0382.037].
- Canva's built-in stock photos are paid only on the paid plan (see plans table) [L0342.002].

### Product images: AI generation

- Gemini (NanoBanana) is the recommended AI image generator for demo product images, although any AI image generator can be used [L0088.004].
- Visit gemini.google.com for Google's free AI image generation service. Copy the complete Source A Image Prompt and replace the placeholder product name with your product name. The unit records only the opening line of the prompt, so the full text must be taken from the source course [L0219.002].

```
You are a professional e-commerce product photographer creating images for a premium Shopify store
```

- Replace "product name goes here" in the full prompt with your chosen product name. [L0219.002]
- Gemini image generation costs little per image, which makes large batches affordable. For photorealistic UGC-style scenes, some 2026 testers rated ChatGPT's image model as more realistic. Run the same scene prompt through both on a small test set before choosing one for a full batch [L0794.049].
- ChatGPT (version 5.2 shown in the lesson) is used for generating photo prompts, product photos with image generation, packaging text, and packaging mockups from inspiration images and brand references [L0884.025].

Canva Magic Media:

- Canva Magic Media (AI image generator) gives 50 free unique images on the free account and 500 per month on the paid account [L0036.006].
- Canva Text to Image generates unique images from a text description. The images can serve as stock images for demo products without copyright issues, as long as the image is not for sale to anyone else [L0855.108, 2023].

Creating AI product images in Canva:

1. Open Canva and create a new project.
2. Click Apps in the sidebar and search for Magic Media.
3. Type a descriptive prompt (example: blue surfboard on white background).
4. Click Create your image and wait for generation.
5. Drag the result onto your canvas.
6. Click Share > Download as PNG. [L0036.007]

### Background removal and cut-outs

Canva background removal (Edit photo):

- Select the product photo, open Edit photo and use the background removal option to cut out the product automatically. The result is not always perfect, so use the adjust options to add back or erase parts of the cutout. Select Remove background, then use Adjust for small areas [L0341.007, L0343.005].
- Edit photo includes a Background remover that removes the background of an uploaded product image automatically. A brush tool then removes leftover parts. Auto-focus blurs the background around a subject, and the Shadow option adds a shadow to an element [L0754.022].

PhotoRoom (AI product scenes):

- PhotoRoom is an AI app for product photos. It cuts out an object, such as a coffee cup, and places it on instant backgrounds such as wood countertops, bathrooms and jungles. It creates reflections and shadows and is available on mobile and desktop [L0353.030].
- Product photos in PhotoRoom:
  1. Choose Start from photo, then Camera.
  2. Cut out the product.
  3. Choose Instant backgrounds and pick a background from the list. The app fills in several backgrounds.
  4. Adjust the image with the shadows and highlights controls and with background blur. [L0353.031]
- A phone snapshot can be pixelated, and a low-resolution source gives a poor result. Use a better source photo [L0353.032].
- In PhotoRoom's desktop editor, a Shadows drop-down adjusts shadows and highlights across the whole image. The background options include a blur switch that softens the backdrop behind the cut-out, which makes the product stand out [L0353.059].

Photoshop and other cut-out options:

- Clipping Magic (about $5 per month) removes image backgrounds to produce cut-out product PNGs for floating images [L0418.016].
- Unscreen (video background removal) and similar tools can extend a background image. A generative fill tool expands a cropped hero background [L0418.019].
- Photoshop's Select Subject command detects the main subject automatically. The Object Selection tool and Lasso tool add or remove areas. Use them together when the automatic result misses parts of the subject [L0754.034].
- Cut out a subject with Select and Mask:
  1. Open the Select menu and choose Subject, or use the Select Subject button in the toolbar.
  2. Click Select and Mask to open the refinement panel.
  3. Use the brush to add missing parts, and hold Alt or Option to subtract unwanted areas.
  4. In the edge settings, use Smooth to soften jagged edges, Feather for a soft edge, and Contrast to sharpen the edge.
  5. Drag Shift Edge inward slightly to remove white fringing around the outline.
  6. Click Output To and choose Layer Mask so the cutout stays editable. [L0754.038]
- Photoshop masks hide or reveal areas of a layer: white reveals, black hides. The invert shortcut is Ctrl+I or Cmd+I. Masks cut an object out without deleting pixels, so the edit can be undone at any time [L0754.039].
- Painting with the eraser on a normal image layer is destructive: removed pixels cannot be restored. Apply the removal to a layer mask so the original image stays intact [L0754.040].

### Product image editing and backgrounds

Canva:

- To set a photo as a background, drag it onto the page, right-click it (or two-finger click on a trackpad) and choose Set image as background. Then move the image so the important part is visible [L0342.020].
- Canva's undo reverts the last change, which allows experimenting with fonts, colours and sizes without losing work [L0342.034].
- Canva templates are ready-made layouts searchable by keyword, for example blog. Use Use Template or Add as new page. Templates speed up designs such as blog headers and sale banners [L0342.033].
- Canva Source A templates: open the provided link, which opens a template already sized for the Manhattan theme, then click Use Template to start editing [L0098.003].
- A free online background remover deletes a white background so the PNG becomes transparent. A transparent PNG is the cleanest option for logos and icons on coloured backgrounds [L0342.011].

Photoshop:

- Generative Fill can add or change elements of an image using AI, and it can be used alongside AI background tools [L0353.033].
- Generative Fill creates or replaces content from a selection and a text prompt. At the time of recording it was at an early stage with uneven results. Expect several iterations before accepting one [L0754.052].
- Adding realistic light falloff to a composited subject:
  1. Create an Exposure adjustment layer above the subject and lower the exposure so the subject matches the darker background.
  2. Clip the exposure layer to the subject so it affects only that subject.
  3. Press Cmd+I or Ctrl+I to invert its mask, which fills the mask black and hides the effect.
  4. Set a soft brush with low opacity and paint white over the areas that should be darker, such as parts not lit by the background light.
  5. Add a solid colour or Hue/Saturation layer clipped to the subject to match the colour of the background light.
  6. Set the layer to a blending mode such as Overlay or Color Burn, then use the Blend If sliders to blend the colour into the subject. Hold Alt or Option to split the sliders for a smoother blend. [L0754.046]
- Blend If sliders (in Blending Options) blend a layer based on the brightness of the layer below. Holding Alt or Option while dragging splits the slider for a smooth transition instead of a hard edge [L0754.047].
- Clipping mask: hold Alt or Option and click the line between two layers to apply an adjustment layer only to the layer beneath it. Without clipping, an adjustment layer affects the whole artboard [L0754.045].
- Adjustment layers change colour, brightness, contrast, levels, curves, exposure, hue and saturation, photo filters and colour balance without changing the underlying pixels. Each adjustment can be turned off or edited later, so it counts as a non-destructive edit [L0754.043].
- Scaling a rasterised pixel image makes it pixelated, while a smart object keeps its quality when resized. Convert the layer to a smart object before scaling. A smart object cannot be painted on directly [L0754.041].
- Right-click a layer and choose Convert to Smart Object. To edit its contents, click the smart object icon at the bottom right of the layer thumbnail, change the content and save. The change updates everywhere the smart object is used [L0754.042].

### Resizing and compressing for the store

- Canva free has no resize tool. For a different banner size: Canva Home > Create a design > Custom size, enter the dimensions, and recreate the banner [L0098.018].
- Canva paid resizes existing designs with its built-in resize tool [L0098.005, L0342.002].
- Bulk Resize is a browser tool with no sign-up and batch processing. It resizes and compresses image files, converts to PNG or JPEG, and can reduce size from about 2.3 MB to about 54 KB [L0418.017].
- Hero background image: resize to about 1,200 px wide as JPEG with reduced quality (about 40 to 50 KB) for faster load speed. Set the main page size to about 700 px on desktop [L0418.020].
- Compress image files before uploading to reduce page-load impact. ImageOptim is a free desktop app for Mac [L0300.006, older: 2021 to 2022].
- Windows has built-in image compression. Setup and usage instructions are in Microsoft's official support documentation [L0300.007, older: 2021 to 2022].
- Photoshop: to scale safely, convert to a smart object first. Adjust the Free Transform scale with Ctrl+T or Cmd+T, hold Shift to lock the ratio, and right-click the transform to choose Warp [L0754.035, L0754.041].

### Batch image tools (Gemini-based)

Set up and run first:

- Keep the HTML tool files and the key private. Enter the key at run time, not in a shared file [L0794.002, L0794.052].
- Download the tool files completely and open the HTML locally in a browser [L0794.004].
- Browser folder linking (Chrome or Edge only):
  - Click Link download folder, pick the target folder in the Select folder dialog, then click Allow when the browser asks Allow this site to edit files? [L0794.050]
  - Without a folder picker, outputs download automatically as JPG files. When a folder is linked, every output is written straight into it, which keeps batch files organised [L0794.015].

Tools:

- Brand Remap Studio is a local HTML tool with three inputs: 1 to 3 brand reference images, up to 5 competitor layout images and an optional book cover. It returns one rebranded image per competitor layout that keeps the layout and swaps in your colours, logo and typography [L0794.006].
- Book Scene Studio creates up to 50 scene photos of one book in a single batch, keeping the same cover on every shot. Upload 1 to 3 angles of the cover, enter one scene per line, set the aspect ratio and run the batch. A Shuffle button is available for the scene list [L0794.007].
- Batch Image Converter (A4) processes up to 50 of your own images in one batch. One reference image teaches the page layout and style. A format prompt is sent with every frame so all outputs look like one set [L0794.008].
- Image Proofer uses the vision model gemini-2.5-flash to read the text in an image, correct spelling and regenerate the image with the corrected text. It accepts up to 100 PNG files for ad creatives, advertorials, labels and social posts. It has an AUTO or ENGLISH language setting and outputs corrected JPGs [L0794.009].

Procedures:

- Brand Remap Studio run:
  1. Open the HTML file locally and paste your Gemini API key.
  2. Upload 1 to 3 brand reference images into box 1.
  3. Upload up to 5 competitor layout images into box 2.
  4. Optionally upload one book cover into box 3.
  5. Choose the aspect ratio: match competitor, 4:5, 1:1, 9:16 or 16:9.
  6. Set Concurrent to 2 and Pause (sec) to 0 as a start.
  7. Keep Relax content filters checked.
  8. Optionally click Link download folder.
  9. Click Remap all and watch the total, done, ok and failed counters.
  10. Read every output before using it. [L0794.010]
- Brand Remap Studio names outputs remap-01.jpg, remap-02.jpg and so on, in the order the competitor layouts were uploaded. If one layout fails, no file is written for that number, so rerun only that layout [L0794.053].
- Image Proofer run:
  1. Open the HTML file locally and paste your Gemini API key.
  2. Upload up to 100 PNG images (ad creatives, advertorials, labels, social posts).
  3. Set the language to AUTO or ENGLISH.
  4. Keep the vision model at gemini-2.5-flash.
  5. Set the JPG quality and the pause between batches, and keep concurrency low.
  6. Click Proof and regenerate, then check the corrected JPGs before using them. [L0794.011]
- Batch Image Converter run:
  1. Open the HTML file locally and paste your Gemini API key.
  2. Select the A4 portrait page format (210 x 297 mm).
  3. Upload one reference image that shows the wanted layout and style.
  4. Write the format prompt once. It is sent before every frame.
  5. Upload your own batch of up to 50 images.
  6. Optionally link a download folder.
  7. Click Convert all images and wait.
  8. Check every output and rerun any missing frames.
  9. Run the finished pages through Image Proofer, then assemble them into one PDF. [L0794.012]

Rate limits and output:

- Concurrency of 2 is the safest setting for the batch tools. Failed requests are retried automatically up to 5 times. If image generation returns 429 rate-limit errors, lower the concurrency or raise the pause in seconds [L0794.013].
- Repeated 429 errors mean the batch is sending requests too fast. Running at full concurrency wastes time and retries. Lower the concurrent count or add a pause between requests, then restart only the failed items [L0794.014].
- Image Proofer splits uploads into batches. Concurrent sets how many images run at once inside one batch. Pause sets the wait between batches. On 429 errors, lower Concurrent or raise the pause, then rerun only the failed images [L0794.054].
- Outputs are saved as JPEG at quality 0.92, with a white background behind any transparent areas. Batch limits: Remap up to 5 competitor images, Converter up to 50, Proofer up to 100 [L0794.017].

### Packaging and mockups

Packaging workflow:

1. Choose the mailer size that fits the product (here a 10 x 13 inch poly mailer).
2. Collect inspiration images of packaging from other brands.
3. Ask a chat AI for packaging text options based on the brand voice, attaching the inspiration images and the brand cover and palette.
4. Select one front line and one side line.
5. Ask a chat AI for a rough mockup using the same references, to get visual direction.
6. In Kittl, create a new artboard at the mailer size in pixels (3000 x 3900 px for 10 x 13 inch).
7. Set the background to the brand colour, for example the main purple.
8. Add the front text in the brand serif font and rotate it if needed.
9. Add supporting elements such as a circular badge, then copy it to the back.
10. Flip the copied element horizontally for the back so the front and back read like a book cover.
11. Optionally add social media app icons from the Illustration panel.
12. Use Smart Board to generate a final mockup on the mailer.
13. Keep the Kittl template so changes can be made later. [L0884.033]

Kittl packaging tool:

- Kittl is an online design tool used to build packaging artboards, add text and shapes, insert illustrations and generate mockups with its AI features. It also stores a brand guidelines template with project colours [L0884.026].
- In Kittl, the artboard size is set in pixels in the side panel, not inches. For a 10 x 13 inch poly mailer, set the artboard to 3000 x 3900 px [L0884.027].
- Formula: Artboard pixels = inches x pixels per inch. For a 10 x 13 inch mailer at 300 pixels per inch this gives 3000 x 3900 px, which matches the artboard size in the lesson's final mockup. Search to convert other sizes if unsure [L0884.028].
- The Illustration section in the sidebar holds icons, including social media app icons for the packaging design [L0884.029].
- The artboard side panel shows Front and Back views. The Tools section includes Add Mockup and Add Smartboard. Use Front and Back to build both sides of one mailer in a single project [L0884.048].
- Generating a Smart Board mockup:
  1. Select the finished artboard, then click the small arrow beside it.
  2. Choose Smart Board, which opens an AI prompt box.
  3. Enter the mockup request, using the prompt below.
  4. Click Generate and let the mockup finish processing.

  The result shows the design as a customer would receive it, because the artboard dimensions match the mailer. [L0884.031]

```
Create a mockup of this packaging artwork printed on a 10-by-13-inch poly mailer.
```

- While a Smart Board mockup generates, the panel shows a running timer and a percentage. Wait until generation finishes before judging the mockup against the artboard design [L0884.049].
- Use an AI mockup for inspiration and direction, not as the final design. Build the actual packaging in a design tool at the exact print dimensions [L0884.034].
- Packaging design does not require professional graphic design skills. Keep the layout simple and use AI mockups plus inspiration images to guide the result [L0884.035].

Mockup services and box design:

- Placeit is an online mockup tool for product and packaging designs. A monthly subscription gives unlimited downloads, and it provides template dimensions for packaging mockups [L0577.026].
- Canva creates custom-size graphics for product images and packaging templates. Each side of a box (top, front, side) needs a separate design because the dimensions differ [L0577.027].

### Canva technique reference

Selecting, moving and sizing:

- Hold Shift while clicking elements to select several at once, or drag a box across them to select all within it and move them as a group. Pressing Shift again on a selected object removes it from the selection [L0754.011].
- Drag a corner handle with Shift held to keep the aspect ratio locked while resizing. Corner handles resize both axes; side handles resize one axis. After the ratio is set, release Shift and drag a handle to reshape [L0754.012].
- Hold Alt on Windows or Option on Mac while dragging an element or handle to work from its centre point instead of an edge. The same key duplicates an element when dragged, which is faster than copy and paste for repeated items [L0754.013].
- Hold Shift while dragging to lock movement to one axis, so an element does not move up or down while you move it left or right. Zoom with Ctrl (Windows) or Cmd (Mac) while scrolling [L0754.021].

Alignment and grouping:

- Pink snap lines appear while dragging an element to align it to the left, centre or right edge of another element [L0754.014].
- Align and space objects:
  1. Hold Shift and click the objects you want to align, or drag a box around them.
  2. Open the Position menu in the top toolbar.
  3. Choose the alignment you need (left, centre, right, top, middle or bottom).
  4. Choose Space evenly horizontally or vertically to make the gaps equal.
  5. If the spacing still looks uneven, group the objects first and then apply Space evenly. [L0754.015]
- Press Ctrl+G (Windows) or Cmd+G (Mac) to group selected objects so they move as one unit. Double-click an object inside a group to isolate it. Ungroup with Ctrl+Shift+G or Cmd+Shift+G [L0754.016].

Layers:

- Right-click an element, open Layer, and choose Bring Forward or Send Backward. Ctrl+[ or Cmd+[ moves an element one layer back; Ctrl+] or Cmd+] moves it one layer forward. Adding Alt sends it to the very back or very front [L0754.019].
- Every element sits on its own layer, and stacking order decides what is on top. Text placed first sits under shapes placed later, so move layers deliberately when a design looks wrong [L0754.020].

Effects and editing:

- Shadows and gradients: search Elements for "shadow gradient" or "shadow" to get many ready-made options in dark and light, long and short versions. Place them behind text or a product to add depth [L0341.019, L0343.016].
- Speech bubbles: Canva has fewer layout and text-bubble options than Photoshop, so speech-bubble testimonials are harder to build in it. Plan the layout around that limit, build the bubble parts elsewhere, or keep the Canva version to plain quote cards [L0341.010, L0343.041].
- Canva Edit photo's Shadow option adds a shadow to an element [L0754.022].

### Photoshop technique reference

Shapes and transforms:

- Free Transform: Ctrl+T (Windows) or Cmd+T (Mac). Hold Shift to lock the ratio while scaling. Right-click the transform to choose Warp to bend or distort the shape [L0754.035].
- Press V to return to the Selection tool. Hold Alt or Option while dragging to duplicate an object. Hold Shift while drawing a shape to keep it square or circular. Alt or Option while dragging a shape from its centre draws it outward from the centre point [L0754.036].
- To make a shape blurred, such as a soft shadow, rasterise or convert it to a smart object, then choose Filter > Blur > Gaussian Blur. Lower the Fill opacity in the layer panel to make the shadow subtler [L0754.051].

Layers and blending:

- Double-click a layer to open Blending Options (Layer Properties). Darken removes whites from the layer above, Lighten removes darks, and Overlay merges the two layers [L0754.037].
