- Drop shadow: add it from the FX panel at the bottom of the Layers panel. Settings include angle, opacity, distance, spread and size, which control direction, strength and softness [L0754.048].

Repair:

- Spot Healing Brush removes blemishes, dust and small unwanted objects with one click or brush stroke. Clone Stamp copies pixels from a sampled area: hold Alt or Option, click the source, then paint over the spot [L0754.050].

### Illustrator technique reference

- Press V for the Selection tool, which moves whole objects. Press A for the Direct Selection tool, which moves single anchor points or line segments. Use the Pen tool with the anchor point tools to add, remove or convert points on a path [L0754.053].
- Brush tool: Tool Options has a slider between Accuracy and Smoothness. Move it toward Smoothness for smoother, less exact strokes, and toward Accuracy for strokes that follow the mouse path closely [L0754.054].
- Clipping mask: place a shape and an image together, press Ctrl+7 (Windows) or Cmd+7 (Mac), which hides everything outside the shape. Double-click the masked image to move or resize the image inside the shape [L0754.055].
- Polygon tool creates shapes with a chosen number of sides. Press the Up or Down arrow key while dragging to add or remove sides [L0754.058].
- Text tool: drag to create a text box of a set size, then type or use Lorem Ipsum placeholder text for mockups. Use the Properties panel to set size, line spacing and alignment [L0754.059].

### Adobe workflow and animation

- Illustrator, Photoshop and Premiere Pro work together. A file placed in one Adobe app can be opened in its original program, edited and saved, and the change updates in the other app. This makes it practical to build a graphic in Illustrator and use it in a video edit [L0754.007].
- Animation in Photoshop and Illustrator requires a separate program, After Effects. Canva offers simple built-in animations instead, such as rise, pan, fade and pop effects [L0754.010].
- Practical workflow: start with Canva for speed, switch to Photoshop for photos and composites that need control, and use Illustrator for custom graphics. Keep originals editable with masks, adjustment layers and smart objects so changes can be made later [L0754.064].

### Social and email assets

- Free stock photos for signup pop-ups, Klaviyo email headers and Tidio chat avatars can be found on unsplash.com. Search a keyword, such as gift for a discount pop-up or man or woman for an avatar, then download and upload into the app [L0477.043].
- Canva's Bulk Create takes a spreadsheet and produces many versions of one design, one per row. It is useful for social quotes or product cards in volume [L0754.023].
- Canva is used for simple social posts, product graphics and quick exports [L0754.001].
- Canva free is used for demo banners and basic assets. For blog headers and sale banners, use Templates [L0342.033, L0342.001].

### Choosing Between Your Own Work and an Outsourced Designer

- Canva is free and suits a store owner who has an eye for design, for making a logo and website banners. **Older (2019).** [L1068.046]
- Designing logos and banners yourself without design skill can cost many hours without a good result. Outsourcing is usually the better choice for a beginner. **Older (2019).** [L1068.047]
- Converting existing badges and logos to one common style is a standardization job on Fiverr that costs around $5. No design skills are needed to order it. [L0921.003]
- For an original logo or banner, do not choose a $5 project. Choose a designer in the $20-plus range and check their portfolio to confirm the style matches. Sources disagree on price: $5 applies to standardizing existing assets [L0921.003], while $20 and up applies to original design [L1068.048]. **Older (2019)** for L1068.048. [L0921.003, L1068.048]
- Avoid people who do not know what they are doing and people who overcharge. Both are common risks. **Older (2019).** [L1068.049]
- Freelance platforms such as Fiverr or Upwork can commission custom product images that are not copied from competitors. [L1346.014]
- 99designs is a library of logo designs you can browse for inspiration. You may recreate a style you like, but a fully original mark is preferred. [L1044.023]
- Initial mock-ups can be made by yourself. More detailed drawings and graphics are better made by a freelance graphic designer. [L1438.026]

### Hiring a Logo Designer on Fiverr

Use this when time is limited:

1. Find a logo designer on Fiverr. [L1044.024]
2. Share the brand look and feel. [L1044.024]
3. Share the tone of voice. [L1044.024]
4. Share the brand values. [L1044.024]
5. Give examples of the design styles you want. [L1044.024]
6. Let the designer create the logo. [L1044.024]

### Canva: Account, Plans and Basics

- A free Canva account is enough to create a basic Shopify store's logo, favicon, banners, mega menu icons, collection images, blog images, USP icons and product page graphics. Designs stay saved and can be re-downloaded later from All designs. [L1045.003]
- Canva is also used to make badges, logos and product visuals consistent yourself, without a designer. It also changes backgrounds and adapts product images for social media. [L0921.004]
- Paid features in the free plan are marked with a small crown icon. Canva offers a 30-day free trial of its Pro plan. [L1045.004]
- Canva Pro costs roughly 10 USD per month. It adds transparent background export, resizing of designs and paid stock photos. Pro is optional, and the whole asset set can be made on the free plan. [L1045.005]
- Canva Premium, the paid tier, is treated as a worthwhile investment for a dropshipping business because it covers much of the visual work. [L0953.001]
- Items with a crown icon are paid and cannot be used on the free plan. In the Elements panel, Graphics holds icons and simple shapes, and Photos holds stock images that can be set as backgrounds. [L1045.041]
- To find a logo template, open Designs in the top-left area and type "Logo" into the "Search Logo templates" field. [L0953.005]
- Typing "logo" into the search returns many logo templates. You can edit them with custom text, icons and fonts. Take a color from an icon in the design to build the palette around it. [L1044.022]
- Canva's Brand Kit template outlines the logo, the chosen typeface and the color palette in one place. It also has a section for testing color combinations in different contrasts. [L1044.021]

### Logo Creation

#### Quick logo in Canva

- Canva's Elements library includes ready-made logo assets. Search the niche plus "logo", for example "pet logo" or "gardening logo", to get usable icons and shapes. Combine and recolor them. [L0952.014]
- A white element built into a Canva logo, such as a white circle behind a shape, prevents a transparent PNG export from working on a dark background. Each background then needs its own recolored export. Plan separate light and dark versions from the start. [L0952.049]

#### Logo with ChatGPT

- Use the ChatGPT thinking model to generate wordmarks, favicons and a color palette in one chat. This replaces separate logo software. It is faster than building a logo by hand in Canva, and keeping subscriptions low matters at this stage. [L0954.002]
- Use this prompt to generate one concept from the list as a logo icon:

```
Generate concept [NUMBER] from the list as a logo icon, keeping the shape and mood of the concept described in the list.
```
[L1047.009]

- In ChatGPT, use Edit on an earlier message, change the request and send it. This creates a new branch from that point, so you can try a different version without losing the original branch. [L1047.013]
- Asking ChatGPT to regenerate a new image at the end of a long thread can make it confused and return an unrelated image. Go back to an earlier message and branch from there to keep results on track. [L1047.014]
- The ChatGPT 5.1 interface is shown in the lesson. ChatGPT can generate logo images from text prompts, recreate a previous image on request, and branch a conversation from an earlier message. [L1047.040]

#### Logo from scratch (for owners with design skills)

- Adobe Illustrator or Photoshop can be used to design a logo from scratch. [L1044.025]
- Adobe Color builds palettes from analogous schemes (similar hues) and monochromatic schemes (one hue, lightened or darkened). [L1044.025, L1044.026]
- Test colors in a typical website layout to check contrast and harmony before you finalize. [L1044.026]

#### Logo export in Canva

- A logo can be exported with "Download selection" so that only the logo is exported, without the extra blank space of the page. This avoids pushing a website header out of place. [L0952.015]
- Export the logo as follows:
  1. Select the logo elements. [L0952.016]
  2. Use Download selection (right-click, or the download option for the selection). [L0952.016]
  3. Choose PNG or SVG. [L0952.016]
  4. Enable a transparent background so the logo works on both light and dark sites. [L0952.016]
- Compressing a logo file is optional but good practice for website image sizing. Compress the exported PNG or SVG after the design is final, not while it is still being edited. [L0952.050]

#### Logo cleanup for transparency

- A free online background remover can strip the white background from a downloaded PNG. This gives a transparent logo without paying for Canva Pro. Search for "Google background image remover" or a similar free tool, upload the PNG and download the result as PNG. [L1045.012]
- After downloading the transparent PNG from ChatGPT, place it on a colored background or a photo to check that no white box remains. If the background is still there, request the transparent version again. [L1047.022]
- Use this prompt to get a transparent version of the same logo:

```
Generate this same logo again as a transparent PNG with no background. Keep the design, shape, colors and proportions exactly as they are.
```
[L1047.021]

### Vectorizing Logos

- A vector file scales to any size without losing quality. SVG is the common vector file format used when exporting a logo as a vector. This is the format the vector step aims to produce. [L1047.024]
- Kittl is a free design program with a free AI Vectorizer that converts a PNG into a vector file. Search for "Kittl vector generator" and open the AI Vectorizer result on the Kittl site. [L1047.025]
- Vectorize a logo in Kittl:
  1. Search for "Kittl vector generator" and open the AI Vectorizer result. [L1047.026]
  2. Upload the transparent PNG by drag and drop. The upload is free. [L1047.026]
  3. Log in to Kittl and open a project, so you can make variations there. [L1047.026]
  4. Drag and drop the PNG logo into the project. [L1047.026]
  5. Click Vectorize. The Colors panel shows the number of colors, and a single-color image converts in a moment. [L1047.026]
  6. Scale the vector up to check that the edges remain sharp. [L1047.026]
  7. Repeat the same process for the wordmark logo. [L1047.026]
- In Kittl, hold Option (Mac) or Alt (Windows) and drag an artboard to duplicate it. Use duplicated artboards as the base for a grid of variations, and delete any copy you do not need. [L1047.027]
- Select the icon and the text of a lockup, right-click and choose Group so they move and resize together. [L1047.032]
- Export logo files at 3x size, which the lesson describes as slightly bigger than normal. A larger export gives more pixels for sharper use across print and digital formats. [L1047.036]
- FigJam is a collaborative whiteboard where you can collect screenshots of finished logo variations on one board for review and reference. [L1047.037]
- Adobe Illustrator's Image Trace command, found in the Quick Actions panel for a placed image, converts a raster image into vector paths. The same panel offers Embed, Mask and Edit Original. This is another paid route for turning a PNG into a vector. [L1047.041, L1047.045]

### Colors and Fonts

- To read an exact brand color in Kittl, use the Eyedropper tool and click the color sample to read its hex code. The Project Colors panel lists pure white as FFFFFF and pure black as 000000, each at 100 percent. [L1047.029]
- A hex color code has six characters made of the digits 0 to 9 and the letters A to F. Copy the full six-character value exactly when matching a brand color across design tools. [L0952.048]
- In Canva's typeface picker, choose a heading font and a paragraph font separately. A sans serif style is a clean, modern default for most niches. Pick fonts that fit the niche, then check that the text stays clean, readable and balanced in the layout. [L1044.041]
- Avoid playful script-style defaults such as Comic Sans for website branding. [L1044.041]

### Adobe Illustrator for Logo Design

#### Setup and tools

- Adobe Illustrator is recommended as the best tool for professional, unique logo design. Place a mood board of reference logos on the artboard as inspiration before designing. [L0952.017]
- Selection Tool (V) moves and transforms all selected elements together, for example rounding the corners of the whole shape. [L0952.018]
- Direct Selection Tool (A) selects individual anchor points or line segments so they can be moved without resizing the whole object. [L0952.019]
- Pen Tool (P) draws straight or curved paths. A single click creates a corner point. Click and drag creates a curve with handles. [L0952.020]
- Pen Tool controls:
  - Hold Shift to constrain a straight line, or to lock curve handles to 45-degree angles. [L0952.021]
  - Hold Alt (Option) and drag a handle to change the curve direction. [L0952.021]
  - Hold Alt (Option) and click the middle of a curve point to convert it to a straight line. [L0952.021]
  - Hold Command (Mac) or Control (Windows) to move an existing point. [L0952.021]
  - Click an existing point while the pen is over it (minus sign) to delete it. Click on a path (plus sign) to add a point. [L0952.021]
  - Press Escape to finish a path. [L0952.021]
- While the Pen Tool is active, hold the Spacebar to pan the canvas without switching tools. This keeps drawing precise on a zoomed-in part of the artboard. [L0952.044]
- Shape Builder Tool merges or cuts overlapping shapes. Drag across shapes to combine them into one. Hold Alt (Option) and click a section to delete it. [L0952.022]
- Width Tool stretches a stroke to a varying thickness along its length. This creates a hand-drawn or sketch-like line, thickened in some spots and thinned in others. [L0952.023]
- Shift-click adds objects to the current selection, so several shapes can be selected before grouping, aligning or merging them with the Shape Builder Tool. [L0952.051]

#### Direct Selection and editing

- Select a path with the Direct Selection Tool (A), click an anchor point, and choose Convert in the control panel to turn a corner point into a smooth point. This rounds the edge at that point without changing the rest of the shape. [L0952.042]
- Selecting all objects and dragging a corner handle resizes the whole shape and can distort it. To reshape only part of a logo, use the Direct Selection Tool to move individual points. [L0952.045]

#### Strokes, fills and opacity

- In the stroke panel, set Cap and Corner to round so line ends and joints appear rounded rather than flat or sharp. [L0952.024]
- The fill and stroke swatches sit on the left and right of the toolbar. Use the switch button or the swap icon to toggle between a stroke-only line and a solid fill. [L0952.025]
- Stroke widths in the example logo: 10 points for circle and line outlines, and about 5 points for finer details such as the dog's head outline. [L0952.026]
- Lower layer opacity to around 50% while building shapes, so underlying elements stay visible while drawing. Return it to 100% when finished. [L0952.027]
- Eyedropper copies the format of an object, including its fill color. To apply a fill color safely, double-click the fill swatch to read its hex code, copy it, and paste it into the target's fill. [L0952.029, L0952.047]
- Using the Eyedropper on a source object with no stroke removes the stroke from the target object. Use the hex-code method above instead. [L0952.047]

#### Arranging and aligning

- Align tool: click an object to set it as the anchor so the others align to it. Use it to align shapes top to bottom or to distribute them evenly. [L0952.028]
- Shape order: Command+Shift+[ (Mac) brings an object to the very front. Command+[ sends it backward, and Command+] brings it forward. On Windows, use Control instead of Command. [L0952.030]
- To copy an object and paste it directly in front of the original, press Command+C (Control+C on Windows) and then Command+F (Control+F on Windows). This is faster than moving a pasted object forward layer by layer. [L0952.043]
- For symmetrical shapes, duplicate with Alt (Option) drag, then right-click the object, choose Transform > Reflect, and select Vertical to mirror it. Duplicating first keeps the original. [L0952.031]
- Group objects with Command+G (Mac) or Control+G (Windows) so they move together. [L0952.032]
- Smart Guides and snapping can make resizing less exact. Disable them when precise resizing is needed. [L0952.033]

#### Text and brand colors

- For text, the right-hand control panel shows character size, letter spacing, all caps and line height. Reducing line height compresses a two-line logo name so it sits tighter under or next to the symbol. [L0952.046]
- Illustrator has a Brand Kit or color palette where colors from an existing brand design can be saved and reused. Copy brand colors in from a Canva design via screenshot to keep the palette consistent. [L0952.034]

### Canva Design Building Blocks

- To center several elements at once, select all of them, click the three-dot menu, and choose Position, then Center. This centers the whole group on the page. [L1045.042]
- Make a simple button in Canva:
  1. Go to Elements and search for a square. [L1045.043]
  2. Add the square to the design, change its size and set its color. [L1045.043]
  3. Place the square behind the text. [L1045.043]
  4. Click Text, add a heading and type the button label, for example "Shop now". [L1045.043]
  5. Make the label smaller and drag it over the square. [L1045.043]
- Effects > Outline adds a border around the letters of a headline. Check the outline color against the background before saving. A light grey outline on white can look weak. [L1045.044]
- The Templates tab offers ready-made layouts. Add a template with "Add as new page", then drag a photo into its frame. Some layouts do not accept certain photo shapes, so pick another template if the photo does not fit. [L1045.045]
- In Canva, open Edit photo > Adjust to correct basic exposure with the Brightness, Contrast, Highlights, Shadows and White sliders. Canva's contrast slider is less precise than Lightroom's, so use Lightroom for fine tuning when it is available. [L1199.034]

### Kittl Templates and Editing

- Kittl is an online design platform with a large template library for social posts, stories, banners, newsletters and marketing plans. It can hold a brand guidelines document and has its own AI features. Canva or Adobe products can do the same job. [L0992.006]
- Template formats in a multi-design Kittl template and where each is used: [L0992.007]

| Format | Use |
|---|---|
| Instagram story | Vertical promo with a short headline and a sale badge |
| Instagram post | Square product image with headline, subheading and badge |
| Website hero banner | Wide banner with a short headline and a SHOP NOW button |
| Email newsletter, hero section | Top block with main headline and image |
| Email newsletter, feature section | Block that highlights a new product line |
| Email newsletter, promo section | Block for a time-limited offer, such as a 24-hour flash deal |

- Color change: select an element, then use the Selection colors panel and pick a color from the document's palette. Click See all colors for the full list, or Browse Color Palettes to find new palettes. Shift-select several artboard titles to change colors on all of them at once. [L0992.008]
- If you are unsure whether an element is centered, open the alignment tools on the side panel and click center. This moves the element exactly into the middle of its frame. [L0992.009]
- Read an element's size and position in the Transform panel, where W and H are shown in px. Type exact values to set width and height, for example W 1080 to match the full width of a 1080 px wide artboard. [L0992.026]
- Image tools on a selected photo: Reframe, Remove Background, Vectorize, Upscale, Border, Shadow and Photo Filter. Remove Background cuts out the subject, and Photo Filter adjusts brightness and saturation. [L0992.010]
- Extend a photo: open the Remix option on the image, choose Extend Photo, set the format to 9:16 vertical, and click Generate. The lesson's screenshot shows the Nano Banana Pro model used for the extension. If a cut-out is needed afterwards, run Remove Background on the extended image. [L0992.011]

### Product Photo Cutouts in Canva

- Quick background removal in Canva:
  1. Open the image and go to Edit photo. [L1258.001]
  2. Choose Remove background and let Canva process it. [L1258.001]
  3. Check the result. If the cutout is good, it is ready to use. [L1258.001]
  4. If the cutout misses parts or leaves specks, open Adjust, then Configure. [L1258.001]
  5. Use Remove to cut out more of the background, or Restore to bring back parts removed by mistake. [L1258.001]
  6. Change the brush size in the Configure menu and zoom into problem areas for finer work. [L1258.001]
  7. Use the Erase tool to delete small leftover bits, lumps or blemishes. [L1258.001]
  8. Optionally set a light grey background color that matches the website's color scheme. [L1258.001]
- The quick method is fast but limited. It cannot reliably remove blemishes on products or clothing, and shadows are harder to handle than in Photoshop. Use it when speed matters more than perfect edges. [L1258.002]
- Canva's quick removal is a trade-off of quality for speed. For high-end product photography with clean edges and no visible black lines, the Photoshop workflow gives better results. [L1258.003]
- Canva's quick cutout can leave thin black lines around edges and small lumps or blemishes. Check every cutout at zoom before publishing. [L1258.005]
- Standard products with simple shapes, such as a teapot, usually come out well with quick removal. Results depend on the quality and angle of the source photo, so a poor starting image limits the cutout. [L1258.006]
- Canva's background remover cuts a product photo out to a transparent background in one click. Export the result as PNG so it can be placed over any colored section. [L1462.019]
- Upload the photo, choose Edit photo, then Remove background. It is faster than Photoshop but gives less control over edges, hair and detail. [L1178.023]
- Use Canva's quick background removal only when Photoshop is not available. It gives less control over edge refinement and spot repair. [L1132.018]

### Photoshop Cutouts and Retouching

#### Photoshop cutout workflow

For product photos where Canva's quick removal is not clean enough:

1. Select the product with the selection tool. [L1258.008]
2. Clean up the selection and remove unwanted areas with brushes. [L1258.008]
3. Refine the edges with the Shift Edge refinement. [L1258.008]
4. Smooth the edges so no dark outline or rough border remains. [L1258.008]

#### Select and Mask

- Heavy Photoshop work with many open files can freeze or slow a laptop. For smooth work, use a more powerful machine, and check that the required Lightroom app is available in the Adobe account before starting. [L1132.017]
- Select Subject creates a fast automatic selection of the main subject and needs enough RAM to run smoothly. On a weak machine it can lag or stall, so save work often before running it. [L1178.006]
- The Select and Mask left toolbar includes Quick Selection (add or subtract), the Refine Edge brush for hair and fine edges, and Brush and Lasso tools for manual cleanup. Use the Refine Edge brush mainly on hair or a beard, where simple selection leaves stray pixels. [L1178.007]
- Object Selection tool (square icon in the left toolbar) selects a whole object in one smart step. Use it to add back an item such as a mirror, or to select a full body. It gives less control than the brush, so use it for rough selections, then refine the edges manually. The brush handles small gaps. [L1132.019, L1178.030]
- Polygonal Lasso draws straight-line selections, and the freehand Lasso draws free shapes. Both close gaps where enough pixels surround the area. Both leave a jagged, pixelated edge, so smooth the edge only after all cutout edits are finished. [L1132.020, L1178.008]
- In Select and Mask, the red overlay marks the unselected area. Change its color in the top-right panel, which also offers other view modes. A different overlay color makes hair and edge gaps easier to see against a dark or light product. [L1132.021]
- Brush add and subtract: the brush should show a plus icon by default, which adds to the selection. Holding Shift switches it to minus to remove. [L1132.022] The lesson's modifier keys conflict, so check the icon before each stroke. In Select and Mask, Alt subtracts and Shift adds. [L1178.026] If the icon appears flipped, add and remove are reversed. Check the icon before painting to avoid cutting away parts you want to keep. [L1132.022, L1178.026]
- Output To setting: choose Selection only to keep the background out without hiding it. Choose Layer Mask to hide the background non-destructively. Use Layer Mask for cutouts that can be re-edited later. [L1178.005]

#### Brush and retouching

- Brush size and feather shortcut: hold Alt, hold right-click, move left or right to change brush size, and move up or down to change feather. Alt plus mouse wheel zooms in and out. [L1178.004]
- Spot Healing Brush removes small spots, hairs and blemishes by blending them with the surrounding area. Feather it slightly so the fix blends in. Work with short, sharp clicks instead of dragging, since dragging smooths the area out. [L1178.009]
- Use the Spot Healing Brush with the same Alt plus right-click drag as the brush. Keep the brush small and use a little feather so the repaired spot blends with its surroundings. [L1132.025]
- Using Spot Healing with a large brush or on high-contrast edges changes the texture around it too much. Keep the brush small and check each click in areas with strong contrast. [L1178.011]
- Clone Stamp copies pixels from one area to another. Hold Alt to set the source point, release Alt, then paint over the target area. The copied area keeps its lighting, so it can look too bright or too dark when moved. Watch the light direction. [L1178.010]
- The Clone Stamp source moves with the cursor, so a long stroke copies the sampled spot repeatedly and creates visible duplicates. Sample a new source area with Alt-click often, and avoid dragging across the same sampled spot for a long distance. Sample from different points, and drag the source from the side where the light falls on the same surface. [L1132.026, L1178.012]

#### Adjustments

- Adjustment layers (circle icon at the bottom of the Layers panel) apply changes such as exposure, hue/saturation, black and white, or whites and blacks above the photo without altering its pixels. Dragging the adjustment layer to the trash removes the change and restores the original image. [L1132.023]
- In Hue/Saturation, hue sets the color itself, and saturation sets how strong that color is. To reduce a color cast on a garment against a white background, lower saturation so the color moves toward grey or black instead of changing the hue. [L1132.024]
- Lowering Saturation on a tinted area, such as a blue cast on a white T-shirt, neutralizes it. Used as an adjustment layer, the original photo stays unchanged. [L1132.024, L1178.031]

#### Lightroom and Photoshop round-trip

- Edit the image in Photoshop and save with Control+S so the edited version syncs back into Lightroom. Keep the originals in the cloud, so only picked images are downloaded temporarily to the laptop. [L1132.016]
- After editing in Photoshop, save with Ctrl+S, close the file, and the edited version returns to Lightroom. Quick crops and basic adjustments are faster in Lightroom. [L1178.021]
- Export a finished Photoshop image as PNG: File > Export > Export As, choose PNG, and save. [L1132.015]
- Without Lightroom, export with File > Export > Export As > PNG. This keeps the cutout with transparency for use in a store or design file. [L1178.022]
- Lightroom photos stored in the cloud download only temporarily when you open them. A laptop with limited storage can still work through a large shoot. Open only the shots you are editing. Lightroom must be installed and linked to the Adobe account. [L1178.032]
- Lightroom Crop tool: grab a corner and rotate or move the image within the frame to straighten a skewed photo. Use this to level the product before applying a 1:1 square crop. [L1199.033]
- Round-trip from Lightroom to Photoshop and back for a product image:
  1. Select the photo in Lightroom and send it to Photoshop to cut out the product. [L1199.035]
  2. Make the cut-out and background in Photoshop. [L1199.035]
  3. Save the file. [L1199.035]
  4. Close the Photoshop document, so the edited version updates in Lightroom. [L1199.035]
  5. Apply the final 1:1 square crop and light corrections in Lightroom. [L1199.035]
- Create a square product canvas in Photoshop:
  1. Select the Artboard tool and draw a square artboard. [L1199.036]
  2. Set the artboard size to 1080 by 1080 pixels, the standard for product images. [L1199.036]
  3. Move the cut-out product into the center of the artboard and draw the artboard around it. [L1199.036]
  4. Save so the file updates in Lightroom. [L1199.036]

### Image Resizing, Compression and Export

- Pixlr is a free web-based image editor that crops images to exact sizes. It is a free alternative to the more expensive Photoshop and is useful for resizing images before upload. **Older (2019).** [L0960.047]
- PicMonkey is an online image editor for resizing and cropping. Its resize function is called Smart resize. **Older (2021-22).** [L1310.012]
- In PicMonkey, open Edits > Canvas Edits > Smart resize. Enter Width and Height, use the percentage option if needed, then click Apply, or Cancel to discard the change. **Older (2021-22).** [L1310.013]
- ImageOptim is a desktop app for Mac that compresses images. Drag the image into its window, and it reduces file size without a noticeable loss in quality. **Older (2021-22).** [L1310.014]
- ImageOptim's default lossy minification is JPEG 84%, PNG 80% and GIF 80%. In the tested case, a JPEG of nearly 300 KB dropped to 102 KB, a saving of about 65 percent. **Older (2021-22).** [L1310.015]
- ImageOptim is an offsite tool for compressing images before upload. It is more reliable than Shopify apps that compress images inside the store. **Older (2021-22).** [L1522.038]
- Image processing on a Shopify store can be handled by an outsourced Photoshop automation rather than an app. On-site image apps can slow the store and reduce image quality. **Older (2021-22).** [L1456.027]
- Compress exported PNG or SVG logo files after the design is final. [L0952.050]

### Static Ads and Banners

- Snappa is a browser-based graphic editor for making static ad images quickly. Text and image elements can be moved and edited individually. Canva, Pixlr and similar simple online editors can do the same job. **Older (2021-22).** [L1169.021]
- Snappa text settings used for an ad: font Open Sans, text color #454545, line height 1.2. Shadow options include Offset, Blur, Color and Text Opacity, and Letter Spacing is also available. **Older (2021-22).** [L1169.022]
- Canva is free to use and useful for social media graphics. Adobe Photoshop is a more advanced option for image editing. [L1283.086]
- Canva is used to design authorized retailer badges and shipping carrier logo collages. The free version is enough. [L1050.003]
- Canva's free version is enough to create supplier authorized retailer badges and simple logos. Place the supplier logo with the words "authorized retailer" underneath. **Older (2021).** [L0962.040]

### AI Image Generators

#### ChatGPT and Nano Banana

- ChatGPT's built-in image generation renders readable real text inside images. Earlier versions could not do this and produced garbled, captcha-like letters. The lesson states this feature is available on every ChatGPT account. [L1075.009]
- Nano Banana or ChatGPT image tools can generate or modify logo and brand images for the store header and footer. Check that the output is usable as a logo file with a transparent background before uploading. [L1188.023]
- GPT image model version 2.0 (paid tier) is used for high-quality image changes, such as book covers and polished product visuals. Nano Banana is used for native static ads. [L1195.011]

#### Midjourney and DALL-E

- Midjourney creates unique AI images from text prompts. It is used for ebook visuals so images are not reused from other sources. It costs about 20 dollars per month. [L1131.050]
- Use this Midjourney prompt for a food image for dogs:

```
Create an image of a finished [DISH] made with [INGREDIENTS] for dogs, served in a bowl on a wooden table, bright natural light, appetising, photo style, square format --ar 1:1
```
[L1131.051]

- DALL-E can create variations of an existing image. It can also edit product images and generate new images for a print-on-demand (POD) store. [L1416.005]

#### Product and lifestyle imagery

- Mokker.ai is an AI image tool used to create or edit product and lifestyle images for stores and ads. It is listed as the recommended AI option for image work. (The source also writes it as "Mocker.ai".) [L1093.005]
- Mokker's free plan gives 40 free AI-generated product images. Upload a product photo, pick a background, and the tool places the product on it, without a credit card. The images are for social media and email, not for product listing ads. (Written "Mocker.ai" in the source.) [L1094.057]
- Canva's text-to-image app uses AI to generate product images that do not exist, which is useful for demo products. Output quality varies, so review each image before use. [L0970.092]
- Higgsfield (higgsfield.ai) and ChatGPT Image 2 generate lifestyle, hero, product and icon imagery for emails. Image generation is an optional layer in the stack. [L1361.009]
- Brand imagery is needed for emails to look distinctive. Emails built only from generic blocks without real lifestyle or brand images look bland. Gather existing lifestyle imagery before building templates, and generate more with image tools only if the brand lacks it. [L1361.010]

### Mockups and Packaging

- Canva can create product packaging mockups and lifestyle product images for a store, including placing a logo on the packaging. [L1381.056]
- Pagdora (pagdora.com) customizes packaging: post your logo, then download the mockups to upload into Shopify. [L1381.057]
- Photoshop was used to create initial drawings of designs before the tech pack. Initial mock-ups can be made by yourself. More detailed drawings and graphics are better done by a freelance graphic designer. [L1438.026]

#### Further details

- Canva has a free version that can be used to design simple graphics such as authorized retailer badges. [L0801.112]

- Create a mockup of this packaging artwork printed on a 10-by-13-inch poly mailer. [L0884.032]

## Video, Ad Creative and Content Production Tools

### Planning the Project Folder and Assets

**Organise assets before building any ad**

1. Create a Google Drive folder for the campaign. [L0308.034]
2. Inside it, create two subfolders: "Image Assets" and "Video Assets". [L0308.034]
3. Gather all images: AliExpress product photos, Instagram posts, user-generated content, screenshots. [L0308.034]
4. Gather all videos: TikTok product clips, Instagram Reels, YouTube Shorts, self-filmed footage. [L0308.034]
5. Add product images, lifestyle images and platform-specific clips. [L0308.034]
6. Reference this folder when designing ad creatives in Canva to keep creatives consistent and fast. [L0308.034]

**Editing project folder structure**

Set up the folders in this order before importing anything: [L1427.013]

1. Create a separate assets folder for images, emojis and graphics. [L0343.025]
2. Create a separate audio folder for voiceovers, music and sound effects. [L0343.025]
3. Create a separate video folder for stock footage and product clips. [L0343.025]
4. Keep these folders stable. Moving or renaming files breaks the links in editing software such as Premiere Pro, which then reports that the files cannot be found. [L0343.025]

- Renaming or moving a source file or its folder after import breaks the link to the clip, so keep raw footage in fixed locations. [L0341.031, L0343.026, L1427.013]
- Folders inside a Premiere Pro project bin only organise your work and do not affect links to source files. Use import folders such as assets, audio and video. [L0341.032]
- Google Drive, Dropbox or an external hard drive can store project folders. Cloud storage lets you reach the same files from a phone, for example to download clips or upload recorded voiceovers. [L1427.011]
- When importing downloaded clips into CapCut, choose "Stay in original location" in the Imported media prompt to keep the files in their download folder instead of copying them. [L0901.009]

### Recording and UGC Gear

**Low-cost lighting**

- A pair of second-hand LED lights (for example on Facebook Marketplace) costs about 20 USD in total. A basic ring light costs about 10 to 15 USD on average. [L1403.004]
- A simple coil light with an umbrella diffuser works well as a key light and softens harsh light. [L1403.004]
- Lighting affects product video quality more than camera hardware. [L1380.025]

**Phone recording and audio**

- A phone selfie stick costing about 20 USD extends and folds into a small tripod that stands on a desk, giving steady footage of a phone recording. [L1424.033] *(2021-2022, older advice)*
- A clip-on microphone for the iPhone's Lightning port has a companion app for adjusting its settings. It records good audio only when it stays fairly close to the phone. [L1424.034] *(2021-2022, older advice)*
- Voice Memos on iPhone records audio that can be shared to Splice. [L1383.049]
- Keynote or PowerPoint can build a slideshow video from product images and slides, with recorded narration over the slides, before uploading to YouTube. [L1424.032] *(2021-2022, older advice)*

### Sourcing Footage: Downloads, Stock and Creator Discovery

**Downloading source clips**

- 4K Video Downloader saves YouTube videos to your computer. Copy the YouTube link, paste it into the app and download the file. The app asked for payment on at least one download, so check its terms before relying on it. [L1427.009]
- The same app can download a sound effect as an MP3 (see the sound effect procedure under CapCut). [L1427.049]
- SnapTik downloads TikTok videos: paste the TikTok link and save the file. SnapInsta is an alternative for similar downloads. [L1427.010]
- Snaptik, ssstik and Downloader Bot download TikTok videos. The lesson's speaker uses Snaptik for this step. [L1252.010]
- A downloader such as SnapTik can save a finished TikTok so it can be reposted to Instagram Reels. [L1077.047]
- A video-to-text extractor converts the spoken script of a direct-response ad into text. Paste the text into an AI chat as a model and ask for a new ad for your own audience. This saves typing the copy by hand. [L0995.058]

**Stock footage and images**

- Pexels and Unsplash are free stock footage sites. Use them to find pain-point clips when you have no product footage yet, then add grayscale treatment and caption overlays. [L0398.054]
- Envato Elements is a third-party subscription library of graphics, stock video, imagery, sound effects and video effects for use in Photoshop and Illustrator projects. Photoshop and Illustrator do not include such a library, so a separate asset source is needed. [L0754.008]
- Envato Elements is a royalty-free stock library. Build ad visuals by cutting out stock images, adding backgrounds and placing text on top. This is slower than generating images with ChatGPT from reference ads. [L0972.103]
- Envato Elements is a monthly subscription for stock photos and other design assets with unlimited downloads. It is an alternative to buying single images or using supplier images. [L0804.051]
- Envato Elements was a subscription source for images, design assets and templates used by the team. *(2022, older advice)* [L0665.056]
- Envato stock video subscription costs about 200 USD per year and includes graphics, sound effects and stock footage. [L1427.057]

**Creator discovery**

- Modash finds creators by niche and engagement rate. Use it to shortlist creators for UGC content before contacting them. [L0398.053]

### Choosing an Editor

| Tool | Where it runs | Cost as stated | Best for | Source ids |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CapCut | Phone and desktop | Free basic plan; Pro at 10 USD per month | Fast beginner ads, many built-in transitions | L1423.001, L1423.002, L0343.027 |
| Premiere Pro | Desktop | About £50 per month in the Adobe bundle (with Photoshop and Illustrator), or about £20 per month alone. At the time of recording: 50 GBP and 20 GBP per month. | Professional work, pairs with After Effects for motion graphics | L0341.028, L0343.028 |
| DaVinci Resolve | Desktop | Free | Budget editing with most Premiere Pro functions for basic work | L0341.029, L0343.029 |
| Final Cut Pro | Desktop | Paid; price not stated in the source. Plan for the cost. | Covering watermarks, adding a logo, combining clips, exporting | L0888.011 |
| Splice | Phone and desktop | About $12 per month. One statement says $12 per year, so verify in the app store. | Short-form ads and TikTok ads | L1380.024 |
| InShot | Mobile | Not stated | Creator content for several platforms | L0784.023, L1453.002 |
| Edits | Mobile | Not stated | Instagram-oriented editing | L1453.001 |
| iMovie | Mobile and desktop | Not stated | Basic cuts and simple transitions | L0644.029, L1426.047 |
| Canva Pro | Web | 11.99 USD per month, includes a stock video library | Static graphics and budget-tight stock video | L1427.058 |

**Principles for choosing**

- CapCut is quick to start on a phone or desktop. Desktop editors such as Premiere Pro or DaVinci Resolve suit serious production. If you plan to produce ads regularly, pick one desktop editor and learn it properly. [L1427.018]
- Premiere Pro, Final Cut and DaVinci Resolve are heavier but offer more functionality. CapCut is cheaper and easier for beginners and already includes graphics, sound effects and auto captions. [L1423.003]
- Splice is practical for beginners making short-form ads, but it cannot place sound effects or cuts at split-second precision. For frame-level timing, use DaVinci Resolve or Final Cut Pro on Mac. Use Splice for most edits and switch to the desktop editor only when precise timing matters. [L1077.046]
- Test an editor before committing. Principles transfer between tools, so switching later is low-risk. [L0485.024]
- Premiere Pro often requires buying a transition package before transitions can be used. CapCut ships a large set of transitions such as zoom, wipe and shake that can be added directly. For beginners who need quick transitions without extra purchases, CapCut is the faster starting point. [L0344.050]
- InVideo and Contagion are video editing tools. The lesson's presenter used one of them to edit short ads. [L0513.031]

### CapCut

**Desktop or mobile**

- For ad creative work, CapCut desktop is preferred over the mobile app because all recorded footage can be imported at once, edited and saved in a project file. The free basic plan is sufficient for beginners. [L1423.001]
- CapCut on a phone is fast and capable, but it stores everything on the phone and is less efficient for managing video projects than desktop software. Large projects are inefficient on a phone. [L0341.030, L0343.027]
- CapCut Pro costs 10 US dollars per month and adds more transitions, extra effects and a larger library of graphics and sound effects. Beginners do not need it to produce Facebook ad creatives. [L1423.002]
- The free library covers basic transitions, effects and text animations. Text templates, some animations and additional pro transitions require Pro. For a beginner ad workflow, the free items are enough to start. Paid items are worth adding only once the basic edit is working. [L0344.044]
- Some animation options, such as advanced animated captions, may require a Pro account. Check whether a feature is locked before building an ad template around it. [L1427.077]

**Layout (desktop)**

- The media bin holds imported footage, sounds and images. The timeline at the bottom layers clips, the player in the middle shows the output, and the right-side control panel changes the selected asset. The control panel holds volume, speed, animation and other settings for the selection. [L0344.001]
- The timeline is at the bottom and holds all video, audio and graphics tracks. The preview window is in the middle. The left panel has the tabs Media, Audio, Text, Stickers, Effects, Filters, Adjustment and Templates. The right panel is the Tools area, which changes with the selected clip and controls position, scale and other properties. [L1423.005]
- Click any imported file in the Media panel to play it in the preview window. Review footage there before dragging it onto the timeline. [L1423.032]
- Scrub footage by dragging across the preview area before placing it on the timeline to check what each clip contains. Dragging a clip onto the timeline makes it the active track for layering. [L0344.003]

**Layout (mobile)**

- The video preview sits at the top, the timeline in the middle and the main menu at the bottom with Edit, Audio, Text and Stickers. Under Edit, the Split option cuts the clip at the playhead. [L1427.039]

**Frame rate and performance**

- Shoot and edit at 30 fps, but check the frame rate your camera or phone actually recorded and match the project to it. [L0344.002]
- Under the CapCut menu, Edit settings offers a proxy option that caches media so a weaker computer runs more smoothly. [L0344.002]
- Recorded footage at 1920 x 1080 pixels is the landscape format, useful when searching for horizontal footage. [L1427.023]

**Shortcuts and timeline controls**

- A: selection tool. B: blade (cut) tool. Ctrl+B: splits the clip under the cursor without switching tools. [L0344.004]
- Space: plays from the playhead, and pressing it again stops playback. [L0344.004, L1423.006]
- Left and right arrow keys step through frames. Shift plus arrow keys moves faster. [L1423.006]
- A selects and moves clips. B activates the blade to cut. [L1423.006]
- Ctrl/Cmd+Z undoes. Ctrl/Cmd plus mouse wheel zooms the timeline. [L1423.006]
- Zoom the timeline by holding Ctrl or Cmd and scrolling. This helps when checking small cuts or the audio waveform, and shows where speech starts and stops. [L0344.008]
- Keep Snap switched on as a beginner. It makes clips lock to the ends of neighbouring clips and avoids small gaps. Snap is the toggle near the timeline controls and can be turned off when free placement is needed. [L0344.005]
- Clips snap to the edges of neighbouring clips when you drag them. This helps line up B-roll and transition points without manual measuring. [L1423.034]
- On mobile, hold a timeline item with your thumb and drag. The item vibrates when it nears an edge, which helps it snap into position. [L1427.050]

**Cutting and trimming**

- The audio waveform under each clip makes pauses and silent gaps visible. Use it to find cut points for filler words before you play the A-roll back. [L1423.033]
- Remove filler or a mistake:
  1. Play the footage and find the mistake or filler. [L0344.006]
  2. Switch to the blade tool with B and cut at the start and end of the unwanted section. [L0344.006]
  3. Switch back to the selection tool with A and click the unwanted piece. [L0344.006]
  4. Press Delete or Backspace to remove it. The gap closes automatically, so the remaining clips move together. [L0344.006]
  5. Replay the section and repeat until the take is clean. [L0344.006]
- In selection mode, grab the edge of a clip and drag it inward or outward to shorten or lengthen it. This trims without the blade. [L0344.007]
- Deleting a clip with Backspace closes the gap it leaves, so the next clip shifts left. [L0344.007]
- Drag a selection box across several clips on the timeline to move or delete them together. [L0344.047]
- To select several layers, drag a selection box over them or hold Cmd (Mac) or Ctrl (Windows) while clicking each one. You can then move them or change their text style together. [L1423.036]

**Speed**

- To change a clip's playback speed, select the clip and use the Speed control in the video section of the right-hand control panel. The demo did not show a 2x timeline playback shortcut like the one in Premiere Pro, so speed changes are made on the clip itself. [L0344.046]
- On mobile, open Speed on a clip. Normal changes the whole clip evenly. Curve changes speed over time to create a speed ramp, for example slow at the start and fast at the end. [L1427.040]

**Keyframes and motion**

- A keyframe stores a value such as position or scale at one point in time. The clip animates between two or more keyframes, which creates movement such as following a subject or zooming in on a product. [L1427.042]
- Keyframes can animate more than scale, including opacity, position, rotation, size and alignment, using one keyframe at the start and one at the end. For example, a fade-in text is made by keyframing opacity from zero to full. [L0344.032]
- Add a moving zoom or pan in CapCut:
  1. Add a keyframe with the diamond button beside the play button. [L0343.035]
  2. Move the playhead. [L0343.035]
  3. Change the scale to zoom. [L0343.035]
  4. Repeat for each position change to make a moving zoom or pan. [L0343.035]
- The keyframe button sits beside the play button. Tapping it places a red diamond at the current position. To remove a keyframe, hover over the diamond until it turns red and tap the minus option. [L1427.043]
- Motion blur in the adjustment settings adds blur to movement so it looks more like real camera motion. Use a light setting, because heavy blur looks unnatural. [L1427.046]
- Fade out: grab the end of the clip, drag to adjust its length, then choose Fade from the bottom menu and select fade out so the clip ends by fading to black. [L1427.047]

**Transitions**

- Split a clip at the playhead, click the small white marker between the two clips and choose a transition from the Movement category, such as Pull out, Pull in, Shake or Zoom. Adjust the transition length after it is applied. [L0343.036]
- On mobile, tap the point between two clips to open the transition presets, which include zooms, flashes and burns. Choose one to join the clips. [L1427.045]

**Audio, sound effects and text-to-speech**

- Add a sound effect to a CapCut ad:
  1. Search YouTube for a sound effect, for example by typing sfx and the effect name. [L1427.049]
  2. Copy the video link and download it as an MP3 with 4K Video Downloader. [L1427.049]
  3. Import the MP3 into CapCut. [L1427.049]
  4. Place the sound on the timeline at the moment it should play. [L1427.049]
- Built-in text-to-speech: tap the text-to-speech button in the bottom menu, type the script and choose a voice preset. CapCut belongs to the company behind TikTok, so it often adds features suited to TikTok content. [L1427.048]
- Add a voiceover from ElevenLabs in CapCut: [L1315.059]
  1. Type the script into ElevenLabs and generate the voiceover. [L1315.059]
  2. Download the generated audio file. [L1315.059]
  3. In CapCut, use the audio import option to upload the file. [L1315.059]
  4. Merge the imported audio with your video track in the timeline. [L1315.059]

**Building a UGC-style ad**

Recommended order in CapCut:

1. Record or film the main talking-head footage (A-roll), either as UGC or as a typical ad, from a finished script. [L1423.004]
2. Cut out filler words, pauses and mistakes from the A-roll and play it through to check the script. [L1423.004]
3. Overlay B-roll product shots on top of the A-roll while the speaker talks. [L1423.004]
4. Add background music and sound effects. [L1423.004]
5. Add auto captions and style them. [L1423.004]
6. Add graphics such as icons, checkmarks and bullet points. [L1423.004]
7. Add transitions, cuts, zooms and keyframed motion. [L1423.004]

- CapCut is used to make video creatives by combining clips, text, descriptions and AI voiceovers. It is a common editor for building several ad variations quickly. [L1219.030]
- CapCut cuts downloaded ad videos down to their hook, combines clips from different ads into one new creative and exports the result. The workflow runs entirely on the downloaded files. [L0901.008]

### Premiere Pro and DaVinci Resolve

**Premiere Pro**

- Premiere Pro is a professional video editor used alongside After Effects for motion graphics. [L0343.028, L0341.028]
- Panels: the Project panel (bottom left) holds imported media and bins. The Timeline (bottom) shows clips in sequence, with visual tracks above and audio tracks below a divider. The Program monitor (center) previews the combined result. The Effect Controls panel (top) edits the selected clip. [L1427.016]
- A new sequence takes the aspect ratio of the first clip you add. Create a separate sequence for vertical video so the ratio is correct. Check the ratio before editing, because the sequence settings follow the first imported clip. [L0341.033, L0343.026]
- Set up a project:
  1. Create a dated project folder for the product. [L1427.024]
  2. Create bins for video, audio and assets inside the project. [L1427.024]
  3. Import the downloaded clip and drag it onto the timeline. [L1427.024]
  4. Keep visual tracks above the divider and audio tracks below it. [L1427.024]
  5. Place extra footage such as B-roll on higher visual tracks. [L1427.024]
- The selection tool grabs and moves clips on the timeline. The razor tool cuts a clip where you click. Its shortcut is C in Premiere Pro and X in DaVinci Resolve. [L1427.026]
- Effect Controls for a clip include position (left and right, up and down), scale, rotation, opacity or visibility, and clip speed. Adjust these per clip in the Effect Controls panel. [L1427.027]
- Scrubbing means dragging the playhead along the timeline to move back and forth through footage. This is how you review and find cut points. [L1427.028]
- Duplicate a text layer:
  1. Select the text layer. [L1427.037]
  2. Hold Alt on Windows or Option on Mac. [L1427.037]
  3. Drag the layer out to create a copy. [L1427.037]
  4. Repeat to build a set of text layers, then edit each one's wording and position. [L1427.037]
- Essential Sound panel: use it to set each audio clip's type, such as Dialogue, Background music or Sound effects, and apply the matching preset for balanced voice levels. [L0343.030, L1427.031]
  - Set a voiceover clip to Dialogue and choose the preset "balanced male voice" to clean up and balance the recording. Adjust the volume afterwards if needed. [L0341.034, L1427.033]
  - Lower the clip volume first if the audio is too loud. [L0341.034, L0343.030]
  - Keep settings basic. Adjust clip volume, and turn reverb or echo off if they are not needed. [L1427.031]
- Tick-tok style text overlay: add a text layer with Open Sans Bold and adjust the line height in the text settings. Place the text with the Vector Motion position controls so it sits clear of the product. [L0343.032]
- A fast zoom transition can be added from the Effects panel using a zoom blur preset, placed between two clips. Third-party presets such as Film Impact or Premiere Composer provide more zoom transitions. [L0343.033]
- Create a zoom-in keyframe:
  1. Select the clip and open Effect Controls > Transform. [L0343.034]
  2. Move the anchor point onto the product or subject you want to zoom towards. [L0343.034]
  3. Set a keyframe on Scale at the start of the move. [L0343.034]
  4. Move the playhead forward and change the Scale value to zoom in, which creates a second keyframe. [L0343.034]
  5. Check the motion in playback and adjust the anchor point if the subject drifts off centre. [L0343.034]
- Right-click a keyframe and choose Ease In or Ease Out to create smooth speed curves. Use the speed graph for custom curves when a preset is not enough. [L0343.038]

**DaVinci Resolve**

- DaVinci Resolve by Blackmagic Design is free and includes most of the functions of Premiere Pro, making it a budget option for video editing. [L0341.029, L0343.029]
- It covers most functions of Premiere Pro for basic editing work and is a budget-friendly option for beginners. [L0343.029]
- It is a free desktop editor with a layout similar to Premiere Pro. It has a folder section for organising media and drags clips onto a timeline, but its shortcuts differ. [L1427.017]
- The preset "balanced background music" applies a finished music mix once you place a music track. [L1427.032]

### Splice, InShot, Edits, iMovie and TikTok's Editor

**Splice**

- Splice is a phone editing app used for cutting, voiceovers, speed changes, slow motion and extracting sounds. It is preferred over TikTok's editor for product videos. [L1380.024]
- Cost: about $12 per month. One statement in the lesson says $12 per year, so verify the price in the app store. [L1380.024]
- Splice is used to edit TikTok ads: it matches on-screen text to speech and adds non-copyrighted sounds before upload. [L1381.032]
- Splice is a mobile and desktop editing app. The speaker recommended editing on a phone and sending the file to a computer. [L1383.049]
- Splice works better on iPhone because TikTok allows higher-quality uploads from iOS, while Android uploads are limited. Android phones are still viable. [L1380.025]

**InShot**

- InShot is a mobile video editor used to edit creator content before uploading it to social platforms. [L0784.023]
- InShot is not tied to any single platform, so its exports are easier to reuse elsewhere. Use it for content that goes to several platforms. [L1453.002]
- Platform-native text tools in Instagram, TikTok and YouTube Shorts add overlay text in each platform's own fonts and style when the video is uploaded. [L0784.024]

**Edits (Instagram)**

- Edits is a separate mobile video editing app made by Instagram, positioned as Instagram's counterpart to CapCut. It is a standalone download, not a feature inside the regular Instagram app. [L1453.001]
- Edits offers more editing options than Instagram's built-in reel editor, including more fonts and animations, and removes many of its limitations. The built-in reel editor is still more restricted. [L1453.004]
- The Edits app has five main tabs: Ideas, Reels, Edits, Record and Inspiration. Inspiration is opened with the plus icon at the bottom of the screen. [L1453.006]
- The Reels tab shows videos similar to your own or videos the app recommends you produce. [L1453.007]
- The Edits tab is the project gallery and holds current projects and drafts. Projects can be sorted by date created or alphabetically. [L1453.008]
- Recording inside the Edits app allows up to 10 minutes per clip. [L1453.009]
- How Edits' export metadata behaves after re-uploading to other platforms is unknown. Use InShot for content that goes to several platforms. [L1453.002]

**iMovie**

- iMovie covers basic cuts and transitions for simple video edits. [L0644.029]
- iMovie is enough for basic cuts, cropping and simple transitions on UGC or repurposed footage. Use a separate online editor when a clip must be converted to vertical format for social placements. [L1426.047]
- Online editors are used to convert formats, for example making a video vertical for ads. [L0644.029]

**TikTok's built-in editor**

- TikTok's built-in editor is fast for quick trims and small fixes without re-rendering a full project. [L1077.047]
- If TikTok's text-to-speech timing jumps around and will not stay in place, restarting the phone did not fix it, but uninstalling and reinstalling the app did. Try reinstalling before you change the content. [L1444.071]

### Voiceover and Text-to-Speech

**ElevenLabs**

- ElevenLabs converts a script into speech in a chosen voice and supports multilingual output. Non-English languages need the multilingual option. Several text-to-speech tools were compared, and ElevenLabs sounded the most natural to the creator. [L0888.007]
- ElevenLabs generates AI voiceovers that can be added to short video creatives. Use it when you need narration without recording your own voice. [L1219.017]
- ElevenLabs is used to make the voice in ads AI-generated. [L1131.052]
- Setup: [L0888.020]
  1. Choose a voice. [L0888.020]
  2. Optionally change the voice settings. [L0888.020]
  3. Check the languages offered under the multilingual option. [L0888.020]
  4. Paste the prepared text. [L0888.020]
  5. Click Generate. [L0888.020]
  6. Download the audio as an MP3. [L0888.020]
- Voiceover swaps: change a proven video's narration into another language while keeping the video itself. [L1195.010]
- Use a multilingual voice model and check the supported languages for any non-English video. The voice should sound familiar to the target audience, such as a documentary-style voice. [L0888.031]
- Before producing audiobooks or other long audio for a store, check how many characters or words the text-to-speech plan includes. Choose the subscription tier so the full text can be converted without running out of allowance. [L0888.032]
- The $5 plan is limited to 50,000 characters per month. [L1131.052]
- Opening several accounts instead of buying a higher plan to stay within a character limit can break the tool's terms of service. Check the rules before doing this. [L1131.053]

**Script source**

- Google Docs holds the script. The creator checks fluency, simplifies the language for listening and keeps the approved final text. It is the single source for the text used in voice generation. [L0888.030]

**Other voice tools**

- CapCut's built-in text-to-speech is described under CapCut above. [L1427.048]

### AI Video, Avatar and Script Tools

**Scripts and images**

- ChatGPT writes the video script from a prompt that specifies length, target age group, style reference and tone. Have a knowledgeable reviewer check the script for accuracy before use. [L0888.006]
- Midjourney, used through Discord, generates portrait images of the character and offers variations of a chosen image. It is used to create the avatar that will later be animated. [L0888.005]
- AI tools are used to produce faceless UGC and viral short content for the store's ads and social posts. [L0914.015]

**Avatar animation**

- D-ID animates a single portrait image with an uploaded audio file, moving the lips, blinking and adding facial expressions automatically. The portrait does not need teeth or manual animation setup. [L0888.008]
- D-ID setup: [L0888.021]
  1. Upload the portrait image and the MP3 audio file. [L0888.021]
  2. Generate the talking video. [L0888.021]
  3. The output carries a watermark in the bottom left corner. Cover it before publishing. [L0888.021]

**Video generators**

- Visla creates videos from text scripts and can produce stock-style footage. It struggles with colloquial language and was not yet reliable enough for finished ads at the time of the lesson, but it can produce usable stock footage. [L0353.035]
- Visla asks setup questions before it generates a video: aspect ratio (16:9, 9:16 or 1:1), the platform the video will be published on, the tone, the type of video (for example entertainment) and the medium. Answering these first gives a more targeted result. [L0353.057]
- Runway is an AI video editing tool that can be used to edit product and marketing videos. [L1416.004]
- Pre-built AI platforms such as Arcads, Higgs Field and Motion come with built-in instructions for specific tasks. They can create ads from a brand website, analyse ads or research ads in public ad libraries. Some allow limited customisation. [L0485.022]
- Pre-built platforms can replace whole traditional roles, such as a designer or manual static ad production, without custom instructions. Choosing which platform to use, or whether to use one, requires knowing which are best and how to prompt them. [L0485.023]
- Pre-built platforms work fairly similarly. Trying one for a month is a reasonable test before committing. [L0485.024]

### Captions and On-Screen Text

- Vide.io generates subtitles automatically from an uploaded video and lets you choose subtitle style, size and font. Its output is only a first draft and needs correction. [L0888.009]
- Vide.io (written "vid.io" in one unit) adds captions to videos, including bold, social-style caption formats. Use it to make short ad videos easier to follow with the sound off. [L1219.018]
- Auto captioning in CapCut and Premiere Pro generates captions from the spoken audio, which saves time on manual text entry. [L1427.036]
- Style auto captions after generating them, as step 5 of the CapCut ad order. [L1423.004]
- Platform-native text tools in Instagram, TikTok and YouTube Shorts add overlay text when you upload. [L0784.024]
- Premiere Pro's TikTok-style text overlay is described under Premiere Pro above. [L0343.032]

### Graphics, Canva and Static Assets

- Canva is used for the landscape image layout, the simple logo, the text space and the thumbnail, using its thumbnail templates. It serves all static graphics in the video. [L0888.010]
- Canva can be used to produce promotion ad creatives. Fiverr or similar freelance marketplaces can be used to outsource them. Google Sheets works for the 12-month offer calendar and for storing survey responses. [L1204.057]
- Canva Pro costs 11.99 USD per month and includes a stock video library. It is a cheaper option when budget is tight. [L1427.058]
- Re-download AI-generated ads before uploading to Meta:
  - Upload AI-generated ads from ChatGPT or Claude to Canva and re-download them before uploading to Meta. [L0316.010]
  - This removes the metadata that Meta reads to detect AI-generated content, which prevents potential CPM penalties. [L0316.010]
  - It takes 2 minutes and has no downside. [L0316.010]
- Use Photoshop or Illustrator with an Envato Elements library when you need graphics and effects beyond Canva. [L0754.008]

### Export, Compression and Format Conversion

**Format conversion**

- Aspect ratios for ads: 16:9 (landscape), 9:16 (vertical) and 1:1 (square). Visla asks for the ratio before generating. [L0353.057]
- Use a separate online editor to convert a clip to vertical format for social placements. [L1426.047, L0644.029]

**Converting video to GIF with ezgif**

- ezgif.com or any search result for "video to GIF converter" converts video clips to GIF format. Upload the video, select the time range, adjust quality and size, and download. [L0321.020]
- Procedure: [L0321.021]
  1. Go to ezgif.com. [L0321.021]
  2. Click Upload Video. [L0321.021]
  3. Select start and end time (in seconds or milliseconds). [L0321.021]
  4. Click Convert to GIF. [L0321.021]
  5. If the file size is too large, click Optimize and reduce quality (for example from quality 100 to 120). [L0321.021]
  6. Check that quality is acceptable and not pixelated. [L0321.021]
  7. Click Save or Download to save the GIF file. [L0321.021]
  - Typical file reduction: 3.49 MB reduced to 1.27 MB by quality adjustment. [L0321.021]

**Compression**

- Clipchamp was suggested as a free or low-cost video compression tool. [L0857.061]
- Paying a freelancer around 5 USD on Fiverr to compress videos is much cheaper than buying a professional editing tool that costs around 300 USD. [L0857.062]

## Email, SMS and Customer Lists

### Choosing the Email and SMS Platform

- Klaviyo is the email platform most sources in this chapter use. It has the deepest Shopify integration, handles automation, segmentation, SMS, and templates, and reports revenue from email [L0274.030, L0604.010, L0605.007, L0607.001, L0854.077, L1076.010].
- Install Klaviyo before the store receives traffic so that automated sequences are running from the start [L0040.002].
- Klaviyo covers all email: campaigns, abandoned cart emails, and messages to vendors [L0337.030].
- Klaviyo sends abandoned cart emails and promotions directly, without pushing every contact into Shopify first [L0359.055, L0532.012].
- Klaviyo is the platform for lead flows and campaigns, and its dashboard shows the revenue those emails generate, so you can check whether a lead flow works [L1076.010].
- Klaviyo is used for email and SMS, including sign-up forms, flows, campaigns, and segments [L0604.010, L0605.007, L1092.092, L1114.035].
- Klaviyo stores the addresses captured by exit-intent overlays and shows how many visitors signed up [L0963.055].
- Klaviyo is a strong choice for stores of this size. It is used instead of a heavy CRM [L0468.076].
- Klaviyo is the preferred email platform for Shopify because of its deep integration. Omnisend was not evaluated because Klaviyo met all needs (older advice, 2022) [L0665.052].
- Klaviyo is the recommended email tool, and it supports exit-intent overlays and embeddable opt-in forms for sign-up pages (older advice, 2022) [L1281.090].
- Klaviyo is recommended for abandoned cart flows and other automated messages [L1251.040].

#### Klaviyo Pricing and Free Tier

Sources give different free-tier sizes. Check the current pricing page before you commit to a plan.

| Source | Free tier stated | Applies to | IDs |
|---|---|---|---|
| Free for the first 250 or 500 contacts; exact limit to be checked on the pricing page | 250 or 500 | Current pricing page | L0417.016 |
| Free for the first 500 contacts; billed by contact count above that | 500 | Current pricing (source) | L1076.019 |
| Free for up to about 500 contacts; steps up beyond that (older advice, 2021) | About 500 | 2021 pricing | L0247.076 |
| Free plan limited; paid when limits are exceeded | Not a fixed number | Current pricing | L0274.030 |

- Charges grow with the monthly contact count [L0417.016].
- Plan contact growth against the plan price, because charges begin once the list passes a set size (older advice, 2023 source) [L0934.055].
- The example used the basic plan at about $50 per month. Plan prices changed around September 1 according to the lesson, so check current pricing before you buy [L0337.030].
- The lead flow and the post-purchase flow both start in the free tier [L1076.019].

#### Shopify Email, Shopify Messaging, and Omnisend

- Shopify Email is a free, native email tool. It includes 10,000 emails per month free, then charges fractions of a penny per email. It lacks Klaviyo's template library but integrates natively and needs no extra app [L0274.031].
- Shopify Email is included with a Shopify account, sends up to 10,000 emails a month at no extra cost, and is recommended over Klaviyo for cost reasons. Klaviyo is more advanced but becomes expensive as the list grows (2024 source) [L1285.041].
- Shopify Email covers most of what Klaviyo does for new stores, and the first 10,000 emails every month are free. It is not necessarily better than Klaviyo, and switching is not required (2023 source) [L1114.036].
- Shopify Email is free for up to 10,000 emails per month and can replace Klaviyo for most stores. Klaviyo remains a strong option, but Shopify Email now covers most of its functions at no cost (2023 source) [L1283.085].
- Shopify Email is a free email marketing app built by Shopify. It sends automated follow-up emails and promotional campaigns to the store's list. It does not handle day-to-day one-to-one emails, which go through the regular mailbox [L1307.001].
- Shopify Email includes Abandoned Checkout and Discount Reminder automation templates and a built-in AI tool that improves and customizes template messaging [L0156.003].
- Shopify Messaging is a free app from Shopify for email and SMS, used mainly for marketing and follow-up messages [L0147.001].
- Shopify Messaging is the primary app for abandoned checkout automation, accessed through Shopify Admin. Shopify Flow provides a visual editor to modify and customize the workflow [L0149.003].
- The Shopify Messaging dashboard shows emails sent, open rate, conversion rate, and revenue, with a date range selector for reporting [L0147.004].
- Omnisend Email & SMS is an email and SMS platform that is installed at a later stage, alongside other tools such as Klaviyo, not at the foundation stage [L1327.040].

#### When to Keep or Switch Email Tools

Sources disagree on whether Shopify Email should replace Klaviyo. Use the rule that fits your stage.

- **New store, no existing list:** Shopify Email can be tested from the first day as a comparison (older advice, 2020). It can replace Klaviyo for most stores at no cost (2023) [L0967.055, L1283.085].
- **Automation depth:** At the time of the call, Klaviyo still offered deeper email automation than Shopify's native tool. Switch to Shopify Email only if it matches Klaviyo's automation depth [L0941.046].
- **Large or growing list:** Shopify Email is recommended for cost. Klaviyo is more advanced but becomes expensive as the list grows (2024) [L1285.041].
- **Existing Klaviyo user:** There is no reason to switch to another email tool [L1114.035]. Switching large email flows is costly and results are uncertain, so keep Klaviyo (older advice, 2020) [L0967.055].
- **Any existing working tool:** If an existing email or opt-in tool already works and you are comfortable with it, there is no reason to switch. Migration usually creates a lot of unnecessary work. Switching makes sense for a new business or when you are looking for something new (older advice, 2021-22) [L0608.002].
- **Other alternatives:** Drip is a comparable alternative to Klaviyo, with a different interface for building flows and moving contacts between lists (older advice, 2021-22) [L0608.003].
- **Deciding factor:** Email automation platforms such as Klaviyo and Drip do similar things with similar Shopify integration depth. The main deciding factor is usability, especially how flows are built and how contacts move between lists. A faster tool lets you produce more automations (older advice, 2021-22) [L0608.004].
- **Lock-in:** Only the email automation platform is costly to change later. Flows and lists cannot be imported into another platform and must be rebuilt by hand (older advice, 2021-22) [L0608.013].
- **Klaviyo at the time of the source call:** Klaviyo was still the platform used for welcome, upsell, and other flows, with triggers set per product or event [L0970.082].
- **Do not pay twice:** Do not pay for both Klaviyo and Privy. Switch your opt-in forms to Klaviyo instead (older advice, 2020) [L1277.067].

### Installing the Tools

#### Klaviyo for Shopify

1. In Shopify, open Apps, then Apps and sales channels, then open the Shopify App Store [L0607.003].
2. Search for Klaviyo, click Install, then click Install apps [L0607.003].
3. Register with the same email address used for the Shopify account. The Klaviyo dashboard opens [L0607.003].
4. Connect Klaviyo to Shopify if it is not connected yet [L0607.003].
5. Turn on website tracking and enable it for the store [L0607.003].
6. Under import, upload contacts (email contacts recommended) and newsletter subscribers, or skip this if none exist yet [L0607.003].
7. Open the brand library and add the logo and brand colors. Klaviyo applies them to all email templates [L0607.003].
8. Invite team members, or skip this step [L0607.003].

#### Shopify Email

1. Log in to the Shopify admin panel of the store [L1307.006].
2. Click Apps in the left navigation [L1307.006].
3. Scroll to the bottom of the installed apps list and click All recommended apps [L1307.006].
4. Under Made by Shopify, find Shopify email [L1307.006].
5. If it is not listed, click Find more apps in the Shopify app store, search for "email", and select Shopify email [L1307.006].
6. Click Install, then click Install app to grant the requested permissions [L1307.006].
7. Confirm that "Email" now appears under Apps [L1307.006].

- The Shopify Email app dashboard has Templates and Settings sections. Automations and campaigns are also created from this app, after its basic configuration is complete [L1307.007].
- Open Apps in the Shopify admin and click the Email app. Click Settings to reach the sender email, double opt-in, open tracking, and template branding options. Template branding is at the bottom of the page [L1307.015].
- The first email may ask the recipient to display images from the sender. After clicking this, images show in that email, and future emails display images by default [L1094.061].
- Besides a browser color picker, look up the brand's six-digit hex color code in the store's own Shopify account settings. Paste that hex value into the Template Branding color field, such as the button fill, so emails match the site [L1317.015].

#### Other Store Apps Installed Alongside Email

- Klaviyo and SMSBump are both installed. Klaviyo handles email automation and SMSBump handles SMS [L0693.028, L1360.013, L1432.010].
- An assistant installed and implemented Klaviyo and SMS Bump in the store for the speaker [L1432.009, L1432.010].
- Clavio and SMS Bump are recommended for installation. Tell the team which apps to install and how to set them up [L1358.012].

### Abandoned Checkout and Cart Flows

- Email and SMS apps are needed to win back visitors who added to cart or started checkout without buying. This improves customer retention [L0635.004].
- Clavio's flows matter most when running paid or TikTok traffic. They send reminder emails every few hours to visitors who added to cart but did not buy [L0634.001].
- Clavio can handle email remarketing and SMS for abandoned carts once the store has traffic. The presenter suggested a second phone number for SMS signups, using the text-free app, to keep personal numbers private [L0820.037].
- Klaviyo is installed to send automated emails, including for bounced visits and abandoned carts [L0532.012].
- Klaviyo builds the abandoned checkout flow, the email templates, and the A/B tests. Use Preview & test to send test emails, and Manage template to edit the shared layout. Save before going live [L0567.032].
- ChatGPT drafts the abandoned cart copy from a brand primer. Keep the working prompts in one document and update them when a version performs better [L0567.035].

#### Checking Abandoned Cart Flows on Single-Page Checkout (2023 source)

1. Opt in with a test account on the checkout form [L1114.038].
2. Place a test order [L1114.038].
3. Confirm the abandoned cart emails and Zapier triggers both arrive [L1114.038].

#### Flow Settings Before Going Live

- The Klaviyo flow Settings panel lists Smart Sending, UTM Tracking, and Additional Filters as separate toggles. Check each one before the flow goes live [L0567.050].
- Additional Filters was switched off in the lesson's flow. Add filters only when a specific audience rule is needed [L0567.050].

### Welcome, Lead, and Post-Purchase Flows

- Klaviyo flows are automations that fire after a customer action, such as an order or a delivery [L0417.015].
- Klaviyo flows are used for many automatic messages, not only review requests. Common examples are birthday emails and shipping confirmations, all triggered by a customer action [L0417.027].
