- Set the flow trigger in Klaviyo to "When someone subscribes to" the sign-up pop-up's list. In the example, the pop-up was an OptiMonk offer of 10% and 15%, so the flow starts at signup [L1470.003].
- Klaviyo can automate quote follow-up emails with flows and filters for separate brands. Building these requires a lot of setup for each brand's templates and images. Automate follow-ups only after the manual template system works, so the templates are already tested (older advice, 2021-22) [L1396.036].
- Feedback Genius is an autoresponder that integrates with the Amazon account. It sends email sequences 5, 6, and 10+ days after purchase. It enables upselling, distributing e-book links, and requesting reviews without manual emails. At about $19 per month, it becomes essential once you reach 30-50 sales per day, as manual follow-up becomes impractical [L0228.029].

### Sign-Up Forms, Pop-Ups, and Lead Capture

#### Choosing a Pop-Up Tool

- Klaviyo is used for sign-up forms, including the exit-intent pop-up that collects phone numbers [L0363.030].
- Klaviyo now includes its own sign-up forms and exit-intent pop-ups, so one tool can handle email capture and the flows. This replaces older standalone pop-up apps such as Privy [L1204.022].
- Klaviyo's built-in sign-up form builder creates the welcome pop-up [L0605.007].
- Klaviyo's embed code connects forms to a Shopify page [L1114.035].
- Klaviyo handles exit-intent overlays in this setup, replacing Privy, which was dropped because it was no longer needed (older advice, 2021). Exit-intent overlays can be hidden on specific pages where discounts are restricted [L0247.078].
- Klaviyo now covers both email marketing and coupon or pop-up offers for newer stores. Privy is no longer needed alongside Klaviyo, and an existing Privy paid plan can be dropped once Klaviyo is in use (older advice, 2021) [L1090.060].
- Klaviyo now handles the email functions previously done by Privy, while After Ship remains part of the setup (older advice, 2021) [L0968.047].
- Privy is a Shopify app used to capture emails from site visitors with pop-ups and offers. The brand reported an email engagement rate of about 20% from this approach [L0790.020].
- Optimonk is a pop-up builder for multi-step pop-ups with email and SMS signups. It is chosen over Klaviyo pop-ups for stronger visual design and faster load speed [L0418.015].
- OptinMonster builds content opt-ins: sidebar forms, inline forms inside blog posts or collection pages, and exit-intent pop-ups. The same tool can place a related download in the middle of an article [L0571.028].
- Email opt-in apps such as OptinMonster and email campaign tools such as Drip can be turned on before a store drives paid traffic. This captures visitors who are not ready to buy (older advice, 2021-22) [L1456.028].

#### Building an Optimonk Campaign

Procedure:

1. Ask the client to install Optimonk from the Shopify app store on their store [L0418.030].
2. Have the client send you an Optimonk team invite, so you do not need Shopify admin access [L0418.030].
3. Open app.optimonk.com with the invite and confirm you can see the client's account [L0418.030].
4. Create a new campaign from scratch, select the relevant domain, and click Next to open the blank canvas [L0418.030].

- The paintbrush tool copies the style of one element, such as a button, onto another. Use it to make a new button match an existing one, then change the label or color only where needed [L0418.031].
- Known issue: Optimonk's hover effect on some buttons does not stick after the cursor moves away. After styling, move the cursor in and out of every button on desktop and mobile to confirm that the hover state behaves as intended [L0418.029].

#### Connecting Optimonk to Klaviyo

Note: the source spells the tool "Optimunk" in this integration procedure. Other units in this chapter name it Optimonk [L1138.006, L0418.015].

1. In Klaviyo, click Settings in the left menu and open the API section [L1138.006].
2. Copy the public API key and enter it in Optimunk under Integrations > Add new integration > Klaviyo [L1138.006].
3. Back in Klaviyo, click Create Private API Key [L1138.006].
4. Name the key Optimunk and set Select Access Level to Full Access. The options are Custom Key, Read-Only Key, and Full Access. Full Access gives access to every API scope [L1138.006].
5. Click Create and copy the private key into Optimunk [L1138.006].
6. Click Setup integration in Optimunk and continue [L1138.006].
7. In Optimunk, open the Klaviyo integration, click Select a list, and choose the list you just created in Klaviyo. Then continue to field mapping [L1138.010].

#### Facebook Lead Ads

Setup in Facebook Creator Studio:

- Navigate to Creator Studio → Tools → Lead Ads Forms. Create a new form, upload a brand image, add a headline, and set fields (Name and Email at minimum). Add a privacy statement, then integrate with Klaviyo via Integrations → Facebook integration [L0308.004].

Connecting the lead form to Klaviyo:

1. Go to the Klaviyo dashboard [L0308.006].
2. Click Brand (bottom left) → Integrations [L0308.006].
3. Add the Facebook integration and log in [L0308.006].
4. Attach the lead form created in Facebook Creator Studio [L0308.006].
5. Anyone who submits the form automatically enters a custom Klaviyo list [L0308.006].
6. Create lead generation campaigns targeting that list [L0308.006].

#### Other Capture Tools

- Shop Message is a Shopify app for Messenger marketing. After installation, it adds an automatic opt-in to the product detail page and sends abandoned cart messages to the customer's Messenger inbox [L0790.016].
- A notify-me-when-back-in-stock app at about $5 per month captures email sign-ups from visitors who cannot buy yet. This works as proof of demand for pre-orders or stock-outs [L1016.052].

### Quizzes and Contests

- Typeform is used to build the quizzes on the website, and the quiz results trigger automatic follow-up emails [L0337.035].
- Typeform, Quizzer, and WriteMessage are tools for running online quizzes and surveys that collect email addresses. Their prices differ, so cost is the deciding factor when the results are otherwise comparable. Choose based on the price fit for your budget (older advice, 2021-22) [L0608.010, L0608.011].
- Quiz tools such as Typeform, WriteMessage, and Quizzer capture emails before showing results. The quiz is embedded on a dedicated page of the Shopify store (older advice, 2021-22) [L0922.019].
- For quiz-based newsletter sign-ups, search the Shopify App Store for a freemium or inexpensive quiz app. Typeform is expensive and is not a Shopify app (older advice, 2022) [L1092.093].
- A contest tool grows the list. It is one of the separate tools used for each email marketing job (older advice, 2021-22) [L0608.013].

### SMS Marketing

- SMSBump is a dedicated SMS marketing app that includes GDPR compliance and quiet-hour sending rules [L0363.030].
- SMSBump is an SMS tool that also includes abandoned cart recovery. It can be used as an alternative to Klaviyo for SMS-based cart recovery [L1251.041].
- Klaviyo is used for email automation, and SMSBump is used for SMS marketing. Both are needed to set up automated flows for abandoned carts, abandoned checkouts, and welcome messages [L0693.028].
- Recart is an SMS tool that also lets you create email-style templates for SMS. SMSBump is a similar alternative. Klaviyo is used for email and pop-up rules [L1213.059].
- Klaviyo is an email and SMS platform, and SMS Bump is installed alongside it [L1360.013].

### Messaging Channels and Personal Outreach

- Minichat is a Facebook Messenger bot that sequences messages the way an email campaign does. Once set up, it sends automatically. Each message in a sequence is scheduled, so it does not need manual sending [L1243.017].
- Bonjoro can send personal thank-you messages to survey respondents. Create a separate list in Bonjoro for survey responses, distinct from the new-customer welcome thank-you flow. Send one pre-recorded message per response [L1052.013].

### Building Emails

#### Klaviyo Email Builder

- The Content panel offers blocks such as Text, Button, Horizontal rule, Product, Layout, Columns, Image, Header bar, Social links, Table, Section, and Spacer. The Product block shows the abandoned items [L0567.033].
- Set the Section Styles fields in Klaviyo: Background, Content color, Section color, Border, Padding, Columns, and Stack on mobile. Check each section on both Desktop and Mobile previews [L0567.034].
- Klaviyo product block placeholders, as they appear in the builder. Check the exact Liquid in your account. These show each abandoned item with its quantity and line price [L0567.036]:

```
Title: {{ item.product.title }}; Quantity: {{ item.quantity }}; Total: {% currency_format item.line_price|floatformat:2 %}
```

- Klaviyo is used to send the promotional emails for each holiday [L1113.036].
- Klaviyo is the email platform used for lead flows and campaigns. Use a saved template for each new blog post, with the featured image, title, short description, and a button to the blog (older advice, 2021). Send a manual broadcast each time a new blog post goes live [L0834.050].

#### Design Assets and AI Drafting

- Canva can build image elements for emails, such as a collage of customer testimonial screenshots. Place the finished image in an image block inside the email [L0607.002].
- Claude and Claude Design can generate a new email module for a use case the existing library does not cover. Upload the new module to the library for reuse [L0610.006].
- Use ChatGPT to draft abandoned cart copy from a brand primer, and keep the working prompts in one document [L0567.035].

### Campaigns, Segments, and Audiences

- Klaviyo can export lists of opted-in non-buyers for Facebook custom audiences (older advice, 2022) [L1113.036].
- Klaviyo can segment by product purchase [L0274.030].
- Klaviyo stores the email list and shows how much revenue the list generates (older advice, 2022) [L0854.077].

### List Hygiene and Deliverability

#### Cleaning the List

- Upload the email list as a CSV to a verification website. It shows counts of verified, spam trap, complainer, bot, and undeliverable addresses. Remove the bad ones. Do this about every six months (2024 source) [L0836.054].
- Third-party validators check email lists for invalid and unreachable addresses before import. They are used during list cleaning, and the cleaned list is what gets imported into the new provider [L0633.017].

#### Warming Up the Domain

- Warmup Inbox (warmupinbox.com) is a domain warm-up tool. It connects to a sending inbox, sends emails automatically, simulates opens, replies, and clicks, and moves emails out of spam. Run it continuously for a couple of months alongside the provider's own warm-up [L0633.015].
- Warm-up tools that show inbox versus spam placement per mailbox provider give a second opinion on deliverability. This matters because the email platform's own metrics are not always fully accurate [L0633.016].

### Platform Architecture

- Each email marketing job is best handled by a separate tool. An email automation platform sends and segments. An opt-in form tool collects addresses through pop-ups and inline forms. A contest tool grows the list. A quiz or survey tool collects addresses through questions (older advice, 2021-22) [L0608.013].
- Quiz and survey tools differ in price. When results are otherwise comparable, cost decides [L0608.011].
- Automate only after the manual process works. Build flows once templates are tested [L1396.036].

## Customer Support, Live Chat and Helpdesk

### Choosing a live chat tool

- Live chat lets visitors message you during the shopping session, so you can answer objections and give immediate support, which supports conversion. [L0040.007]
- For a beginner on Shopify, Shopify Inbox is preferred over Tidio because it is simpler to set up. Tidio offers more features and automations but is more complicated to configure. [L0661.048]
- Shopify Inbox's main advantage over Tidio is phone push notifications, which let the store owner answer customer questions quickly. [L1220.046]
- One 2024 setup uses Shopify Inbox for live chat with customers instead of Tidio. [L0360.076]
- A live chat app can be used from a phone like a text conversation, so the owner can answer pre-sale questions such as whether an item is in stock. Put a live chat link on the product page. [L1346.013]
- Shopify live chat is the support channel for store-level problems, such as theme or code settings that affect how the store appears in search results. (Older advice, 2021.) [L1090.064]

### Shopify Inbox

#### What it is and what it costs

- Shopify Inbox is Shopify's built-in live chat sales channel. It is free of charge ($0) and included with the store, so no paid third-party live chat app is needed. It is not active by default and must be added as a sales channel. [L0948.003]
- Shopify Inbox is a free live chat app from Shopify that adds a chat widget to the storefront. It is included with the Shopify plan at no extra cost. [L1311.001]
- Shopify Inbox is included with all Shopify plans and lets customers message you directly from your website in real time. [L0151.001]
- The Shopify Inbox app is free. Install it early so that replies can be sent quickly from a phone. [L0661.047]
- Shopify Inbox works like a live chat and sends push notifications to a phone whenever a customer asks a question. It is installed in the store and in the Shopify mobile app. [L1220.028]
- Shopify Inbox replaces the need for Tidio for Shopify stores. [L1432.008]
- Shopify Inbox asks for an email address before the chat begins. [L0856.074]
- The chat widget appears on every store page, so visitors can start a conversation at any time. Messages appear in Shopify Admin and are pushed to your phone through the mobile app. [L0151.003]
- Shopify Inbox can replace third-party live chat apps. [L0971.099]

#### Install: route A (Apps menu, short version)

1. Log in to Shopify Admin.
2. Click Apps.
3. Search for "Shopify Inbox".
4. Select the app (free plan).
5. Click Install. [L0151.004]

#### Install: route B (Apps menu, detailed version)

1. Log into Shopify Admin.
2. Click Apps.
3. Search for "Inbox"; the listing shows the label "Free plan available".
4. Click Install; the app is added to the store.
5. Optionally pin the "Inbox" entry in the sidebar navigation so it is easy to find.
6. Click Inbox in the sidebar and click "Turn on chat".
7. Customize the widget in the theme customizer (see the App embeds settings).
8. Click Save.
9. Reload the storefront to confirm the chat icon is visible. [L1311.005]

#### Install: route C (Sales channels)

1. Log into the Shopify admin.
2. In the left navigation, open Sales channels.
3. Click the plus icon, search for "inbox" and select Shopify Inbox.
4. Confirm with yes to grant the app access to store data.
5. Shopify Inbox then appears under Sales channels. [L0948.004]

Install in the Shopify App Store by searching "Shopify Inbox" and adding it to the store. Then install the Shopify Inbox app on the Shopify mobile app. [L1220.045]

#### Turning chat on

- Under Sales channels > Shopify Inbox > Online store chat, chat is off by default. Click Manage settings and turn it on to activate the chat on the storefront. Setup takes about five minutes. [L0948.005]
- In Shopify Admin, Inbox > "Turn on chat" opens the theme customizer, where the chat widget is placed on the storefront. The widget does not appear until chat is turned on. [L1311.006]

#### Verify on the live storefront

To verify Shopify Inbox chat on the storefront after saving the settings:

1. Open a private (incognito) browser window.
2. Load the live store URL.
3. Check that the chat icon appears in the bottom right corner.
4. If it is missing, wait a few minutes, since setting changes can take time to show on the store. [L0948.020]

To test the full chain:

1. Open the storefront, click the chat icon and send a test question.
2. Enter a name and email address when the form appears and click "Start chat".
3. Open Shopify Inbox in the admin and confirm the conversation is listed.
4. Confirm the message arrives as a notification in the mobile app.
5. Reply from the admin and check that the reply shows in the mobile app as well. [L1311.016]

#### Common mistakes

- If chat is not switched on in the Inbox app, no chat icon appears on the storefront, so visitors cannot reach you. After switching it on, always check the live storefront to confirm the icon is visible. [L1311.014]
- The admin bar at the bottom of the storefront is visible only to logged-in admin users. Testing while logged in shows a view that visitors do not see. Hide the bar or test in a logged-out browser window to see the real visitor experience. [L1311.015]

#### Mobile app

- The Shopify Inbox mobile app (iOS and Android) sends push notifications when customers message, and lets you respond from anywhere by logging in with your Shopify credentials. [L0151.010]
- Shopify Inbox has a separate mobile app for iPhone (Apple App Store) and Android. It is signed in with the same Shopify username and password used for the admin. [L1311.002]

To install the mobile app:

1. Install the Shopify Inbox app on the phone from the Apple App Store (iPhone) or Google Play (Android).
2. Log in with the Shopify username and password.
3. Confirm that incoming chats arrive as phone notifications and appear in the app. [L1311.020]

The mobile app lets the owner answer messages from a phone, and the chat box can be turned off in its settings when the owner is unavailable. [L0971.099]

#### Responses, AI and availability

- Shopify Inbox responses can include text replies, links, discount codes, images, and emojis. [L0151.007]
- Enable instant answers by turning on "Track my order", "shipping details responses", and "return policy responses" so the AI pulls answers automatically from your store pages. [L0151.008]
- Shopify Magic is Shopify's AI tool integrated into Shopify Inbox. It can offer automatic instant answers, for example to contact-info questions (sourced from the contact page) and return-policy questions (sourced from the return policy page). [L0948.009]
- Set chat availability to 24/7 so visitors can message at any time. They can still leave messages outside business hours. The automatic first reply is optional, and marketing opt-in can remain enabled. [L0151.009]

### Tidio

Sources disagree on the preferred tool. Recommendations from 2019 to 2021 favour Tidio. The beginner preference for Shopify Inbox appears in a 2024 source and in the Source C course. Tidio has more features and automations but is harder to configure, so use Shopify Inbox for simple setup and Tidio when you need its extra features. [L0661.048, L0360.076, L1002.030, L0663.043]

- Tidio is a live chat option for smaller stores. The presenter did not recommend the live chat tool his company uses, since it is built for large volume. (Older advice, 2019.) [L0663.043]
- Tidio is the live chat software recommended for the store. Live chats and inquiries are an early sign that visitors are engaging with the site. (Older advice, 2019.) [L1002.030]
- Tidio is a live chat app for Shopify stores. Avoid the native Facebook Messenger chat integration on Shopify. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0852.077]
- Tidio has a free plan. Start with it and upgrade only when needed, without using the chatbot. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0834.053]
- Tidio's free live chat can run alongside Klaviyo for email. Do not place discount codes in the chatbot greeting, because discounts already appear across the site. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0247.077]
- Tidio is a live chat app that notifies you of incoming chats on mobile and can capture email addresses to import into Shopify. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0962.053]
- Tidio can route live chat messages to the owner's phone so chats are answered as they arrive, which allows chat support outside office hours. (Older advice, 2022.) [L1113.034]
- Tidio Live Chat adds a live chat widget to a Shopify store so visitors can contact you directly while browsing. [L1399.004]
- ChatGPT can generate automatic replies for a live chat widget such as Tidio. This lets a store answer common visitor questions without a human agent being online. [L1416.002]

### talk.to and tawk.to

- talk.to is a Shopify live chat app that lets visitors ask questions on a product page, and other Shopify live chat apps offer similar functions. Chat messages can reach the store owner's phone as a text or Facebook message, so quick replies from a phone are possible. (Older advice, 2019.) [L1110.052]
- Talk.to (tawk.to) is a live chat app for Shopify that is low-cost and performs well on both chat functionality and page speed. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1298.005]
- The tawk.to dashboard shows live visitors, page views, chats (answered and missed), engagement, availability and positive sentiment, with reporting for today or the last 7 days. Active chats open in a window in the dashboard for day-to-day handling. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1298.037]
- tawk.to offers paid add-ons for removing branding, screen sharing, video and audio calls, or hiring agents to answer chats for $1 per hour. These are optional and not needed to launch. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1298.006]

### Gorgias chat and helpdesk

#### Set up the Gorgias chat widget

1. In Settings, open Channels and click Chat.
2. Click Add chat.
3. Enter a chat title.
4. Select the platform type.
5. Connect a store.
6. Choose whether to allow live chat messages or only offline messages.
7. Click Create and customize. [L0752.002]

#### Appearance

Settings > Chat > Appearance > Installation. Fields:

- Chat title.
- Language (English (US)).
- Intro message for business hours (for example "How can we help?") and outside business hours (for example "We'll be back tomorrow").
- Colors (main color and conversation color), font, company logo, and widget position on the website.

Labels, automated messages and offline capture subjects can be customized to match the brand's tone of voice. [L0752.004]

#### Preferences

Settings > Chat > Preferences. Choose when live chat is available, for example only when agents are online. Outside those hours the widget shows the built quick responses and lets the visitor leave an email address for later contact. Also configure email capture, an autoresponder, and forwarding of chat replies to the customer's email. [L0752.005]

#### Installation on Shopify

- 1-click installation for Shopify adds the widget to the store in one step and automatically enables Automation Add-on features if available. [L0752.006]
- Placement options are Show on every page, Show on specific pages, or Hide on specific pages. [L0752.006]
- Manual installation via embed code is used for other platforms or specific pages. [L0752.006]
- A store connection is required for Automation Add-on features in chat and for 1-click installation. [L0752.006]
- A single chat widget is enough for most brands. Separate chats can be created for different purposes if needed. [L0752.007]

#### Campaigns

Gorgias chat campaigns prompt a message based on the page URL and can add conditions such as time spent on page. The example campaign shows a greeting when a visitor arrives on the site. The campaign name, audience (current URL), conditions and message text are set in the campaign editor, and the sender is a random agent's first name. [L0752.003]

#### Offer

With a three-month plan, the second and third month of the Automation Add-on are free. For new Gorgias users, an annual plan gives two extra months of the regular platform free. [L0752.032]

#### Gorgias helpdesk

- Gorgias is the helpdesk used to find customer tickets and emails, to hold saved macros such as `CHARGEBACK - No Ticket`, and to print or save tickets as PDF evidence. Any email platform works if it can do the same. [L0350.016]
- Gorgias connects to Shopify so that the customer profile shows customer type (for example VIP), email, phone number, total spent, and the order list with payment status (for example PAID) and fulfillment status (for example FULFILLED). The Shopify integration allows creating orders from within the helpdesk. [L0753.007]
- Gorgias centralizes all customer conversations and customer data in one platform. Integrations with e-commerce apps keep workflows connected, so support and sales run from a single tool. [L0753.009]
- The Gorgias AI agent gives instant, personalized replies across the whole customer journey and chooses the right channel for each shopper at the right moment. It is used for both pre-purchase questions and post-purchase support. [L0753.003]
- The Satisfaction view under Statistics shows a "Latest Surveys" table with the columns Customer, Assignee, Date, Score and Comment, plus a "Go to ticket" link on each row. Use the link to open the original conversation behind a low score. [L0401.017]

### Shared inboxes and helpdesks

- Reamaze is a customer communication platform that centralizes all messages in one inbox. It houses an FAQ library, stores response templates, provides live chat, and integrates abandoned cart recovery. [L0230.003]
- Reamaze is a customer service software that handles emails, chats and social media messages in one list, showing who is working on each case. It should be adopted once the team has one to three people, since a shared inbox in Gmail becomes inefficient and loses track of who sent what. [L0337.031]
- Reamaze (spelled "Remaze" in the source) is a help desk inbox connected to Shopify that gathers email, social media messages and online chat in one place. Agents can see order status, change or cancel orders from the inbox, add internal notes and set follow-up reminders. It also stores reply templates that insert fields such as the customer name or tracking number automatically. [L1473.001]
- Zendesk is used for internal customer communication. Zendesk Chat is used on many of the stores, but it is described as overpriced and outdated for most sellers. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0852.076]
- Zendesk is used as the customer email interface for support. Customer replies and outgoing support emails can land in spam, so check the spam folder for missing replies. (2024.) [L1285.042]
- Zendesk charges per seat, and a 20-person team made it expensive. Switching to Help Scout cut the monthly support software cost by about $1,000. (2025.) [L0807.038]

### Answering support requests and saved macros

- Shipment progress can be shown to customers through a multi-carrier tracking page such as 17track, where the tracking number is entered in the link. Agents attach a screenshot of the current status to the reply so the customer sees the same information. [L0405.034]
- Reply templates in Reamaze insert fields such as the customer name or tracking number automatically. [L1473.001]
- Gorgias holds saved macros such as `CHARGEBACK - No Ticket`. [L0350.016]

### Chargeback documentation

- In Gorgias, find the customer's tickets and emails, apply the saved macro `CHARGEBACK - No Ticket` where it fits, and print or save the tickets as PDF evidence. Any email platform works if it can do the same. [L0350.016]

### Call tracking and phone support

- Mighty Call (mightycall.com) provides call tracking and chat monitoring. It shows call logs, how many calls were answered and how many chats came in, which lets the owner check a phone or chat team's performance. (Older advice, 2019.) [L0467.049]
- MightyCall records phone calls and tracks how many calls are answered. This helps verify that a virtual assistant doing customer service actually answers calls. (Older advice, 2022.) [L0854.076]

### Offline and after-hours messages

- Set up your online chat tool to take a message when no agent is available and forward it to your email. The forwarded message should include the visitor's question, their email address and any phone number they gave. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0909.014]
- Gorgias shows the built quick responses outside chat hours and lets the visitor leave an email address for later contact. [L0752.005]
- Gorgias intro messages can differ for business hours and outside business hours. [L0752.004]
- Shopify Inbox chat availability can be set to 24/7, and visitors can still leave messages outside business hours. [L0151.009]
- Turn off the Shopify Inbox chat box in its settings when the owner is unavailable. [L0971.099]

### CRM and when one is warranted

#### Current practice for small stores

- Keep customer data in Shopify and Klaviyo rather than a separate enterprise CRM such as Salesforce, which is not designed for drop-ship stores of this type. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0468.077]
- A small store can manage customer profiles with Shopify's built-in customer records and send all customer email from a business Gmail account, without a separate CRM. [L0666.057]

#### Named CRM options (2021-22 source, older advice)

- Copper is a CRM that integrates with Gmail and suits a small team that wants a light, easy system. It is one option among several for organizing customer contacts. [L0949.018]
- Gorgeous is a customer-journey tool built for Shopify that offers more than the default Shopify features. It suits larger stores better than new ones, and users' opinions of it vary widely. [L0949.019]
- ActiveCampaign is a light CRM with strong email automation at a reasonable price and some CRM functions. At the time of the lesson it lacked the direct Shopify integration that tools such as Klaviyo and Drip offered. [L0949.020]
- Trello boards are a simple way to organize customer follow-ups and tasks in a small business. They are useful as a stopgap before a full CRM is justified. [L0949.021]

#### When a CRM is warranted

- A CRM is only needed when the Shopify-plus-Gmail setup stops covering the workflow. [L0666.057]
- Use Trello as a stopgap before a full CRM is justified. [L0949.021]
- Introducing a complex CRM before you have consistent sales adds work without a return, because good CRM tools are hard to set up and need data to be useful. Start with simpler tracking until sales are steady. [L0949.022]
- High-ticket stores have far fewer orders than typical e-commerce stores, so their customer records grow slowly. A simple tracking system is often enough for a long time, even at high revenue. [L0949.023]

## Reviews, Social Proof and Surveys

### Why reviews and social proof come first

- Product reviews build trust with new visitors, which helps them decide to buy. Collect both product reviews and store reviews. [L0589.001]
- Shoppers look for social proof and trust before buying, so a review app is treated as a must. [L0635.003]
- Install a Shopify product review system on every product page to show social proof, even a basic one. The author calls this well worth the effort. [L1346.012]
- A review section does not need to be built by hand on a Shopify store. Use an app for the reviews and a page builder or theme section for the layout around them. [L0387.034]
- Review apps and urgency apps are installed on Shopify to add urgency elements and product reviews to store pages. [L0532.013]
- A Questions & Answers app shows customer questions and answers on product pages. This creates user-created content for SEO and builds social proof to increase conversions. [L0176.005]

### Ethics and rules for collecting reviews

- Ask only approved suppliers for reviews, and only if they have product reviews available. Request their permission before using any reviews, and do not copy reviews without explicit supplier approval. [L0155.004]
- Supplier reviews can be entered with permission, so they appear on product pages in the same style as future customer reviews. [L0855.110]
- Older advice (2021): reviews provided by a supplier can be uploaded to Judge.me only with written permission from that supplier. [L1090.058]
- Editing or inventing reviews misleads buyers and can violate consumer-protection rules, for example fake-review rules in the US. Use review tools only for real customer feedback. [L0502.040]
- **Conflict on seeding.** The seeding units say that initial reviews can come from suppliers, family or friends to establish credibility before paid traffic begins [L0040.003]. Review apps such as Ali Reviews or Judge.me can seed initial reviews on a new store, to be replaced later with real customer reviews [L0513.030]. The ethics units require real customer feedback and supplier permission [L0502.040, L0155.004].
- **Operator reading:** the seeding units and the ethics units apply to different situations. A seeded review must not be presented as a real customer review, and supplier reviews need permission before they are shown. [L0040.003, L0513.030, L0502.040, L0155.004]
- Review imports from supplier listings can show weak or irrelevant feedback. Screen each review before import and reject any that do not fit the product. Limit imports to four stars and up with photos to keep the store credible. [L0634.032]

### Judge.me: profile and plans

- Judge.me Product Reviews collects, displays and manages customer product reviews. Find it by searching "judge.me" in the Shopify app store. [L1245.001, L0851.001]
- Judge.me is the recommended reviews app for dropshipping stores. It has been used successfully for 7-8 years and is the best fit for high-ticket dropshipping operations. [L0155.002]
- The free plan is enough to collect and display reviews. The paid plan is not required, and no billing setup is needed to start. [L0155.005]
- The paid "awesome plan" is optional and can be added later. Installing the free plan requires no agreement to a billing policy. [L1245.002]
- On the free plan, widget texts such as "Awesome" cannot be edited. Editing them requires the paid plan. [L0908.017]
- **Conflict on Google star ratings and the free plan:**
  - One unit says the Judge.me free plan can automatically email review requests and shows star ratings in Google and Google Shopping. [L0908.009]
  - A 2025 unit says the Google Shopping star integration costs $15 per month and the free plan does not include it. [L0972.026]
  - Use the 2025 value as the more recent one, and check the plan in the app before relying on Google Shopping stars. [L0908.009, L0972.026]
- Judge.me was chosen over the previous review app because it displays star ratings in Google and Google Shopping, which few low-cost review apps offer. Showing stars in search and shopping results can improve click-through. [L0908.010]
- Judge.me reviews can appear in Google Shopping ads through its Google Shopping feature. It is worth paying for once a store has a good number of reviews (2024). [L1115.057]
- Older advice (2021): Judge.me integrates more easily with Google Shopping star ratings than Stamped.io. [L0834.051]
- Older advice (2020): the paid Judge.me plan sends star ratings to Google Shopping. Stamped.io is an alternative with a similar function. [L1111.077]

### Judge.me: Google, search stars and trust signals

- Judge.me collects reviews and integrates with Google, so star ratings can appear in Google Shopping results. It is used as the review aggregator in the store setup (older advice, 2022). [L0803.080]
- Judge.me star reviews add schema markup to organic results, which can make star ratings appear in search and increase click-through rate (2023). [L1283.084]
- Judge.me displays reviews across the site and on a dedicated review page. This serves as the store's trust signal in place of paid security seals (2023). [L0934.168]
- Judge.me stars can appear in Google Shopping results and replace SEO Rich Snippets for reviews on new stores (2023). [L0804.049]
- SEO Rich Snippets and Minifier were removed from the Shopify app store. Replace them as follows: [L0804.050]
  1. Open the Shopify app store.
  2. Search for rich snippets and minifier.
  3. Choose a 5-star app that does the same job.

  Existing stores keep the old apps they already had. [L0804.050]
- Judge.me badges look similar to Trustpilot and give a more authoritative look on the homepage. Check that the badge links down to the reviews section on the page. Most review apps that work with PageFly support this. If not, contact the review app or PageFly support. [L1363.019]
- Judge.me needs the license number from the theme vendor entered in its setup. Without that license entry, the review block may not work correctly on the product page. [L1188.021]

### Installing Judge.me

Install steps in Shopify:

1. Open the Shopify App Store and click Visit the app store.
2. Search for Judge.me and open the first result, which shows the free plan.
3. Click Add app.
4. Agree to give the app access to the store so it works properly.
5. Click Install app.
6. Choose who installs it: yourself via the theme editor, or Judge.me's team.
7. Click Start installation.
8. Select where reviews should appear: the reviews widget on the product page, stars on the product page, and stars on the collection page.
9. Click Choose theme and install on the current live theme, unless another theme is installed but not yet published and should be used.
10. Click Install judge.me and wait for the installation to finish.

[L0908.012]

- Search for Judge.me in the Shopify App Store, open the app, click Add app, grant access to the store, and click Install app. Choose between installing it yourself through the theme editor or having the Judge.me team install it for you. [L0908.011]
- The Judge.me support team installs widgets manually and sends an email within three hours after the request. Installation may take about two hours in practice. [L0908.013]
- The Judge.me widget install (2024): after installing, add the review widget to product pages from the app's top-left box. If the automatic install does not show it, use the app's contact-developer box, and the developer can install it. [L0836.053]

**Widgets (procedure):**

1. Go to Judge.me → Widgets.
2. Locate the Review Widget.
3. Click Install.
4. The widget will be added to your Shopify theme automatically.

[L0155.008]

- The product page review widget is found under Settings > Review display > Widgets. The first time, click Install; it adds the widget automatically to the Shopify theme. A separate star ratings badge widget is also available there, and several widgets can be used at once. [L1245.004]
- In Settings, the Other widgets section shows where each widget is toggled on or off on the site. Under the preview badge settings you can set badge colors and choose Hide badge texts. Leave it off so both stars and text are shown. [L0908.018]
- Branding: open Settings and scroll to the Branding section at the bottom left. Set the primary color to one of the colors used in the store's layout and color scheme. This primary color also styles the review request emails, including the stars. [L1245.003]

### Judge.me: review requests and emails

- Judge.me automatically sends a review request email 14 days after purchase. This timing ensures customers have received the product before being asked to write a review. [L0155.010]
- Judge.me Settings groups requests under Review requests and Other emails. These control the automatic review request emails sent to customers. [L0908.019]
- Customers can send their review by email or leave it through the Shopify Product Review app, which should already be installed on the store. [L0417.013]
- Judge.me is a product review app that stores reviews for each product and also a general store review area. Use it to add reviews collected by email (2024). [L0965.059]
- Judge.me publishes product reviews on product pages (2023). [L0970.088]

**Email templates (procedure):**

1. Go to Judge.me → Email Templates.
2. Edit subject lines or review request copy if desired.
3. Leave default settings enabled, which is recommended.

[L0155.011]

### Judge.me: moderation

- Judge.me automatically approves and publishes reviews by default. In the dashboard you can view all reviews, mark spam or unrelated reviews as hidden, and control what appears on product pages. [L0155.012]
- New reviews appear in the review section and are published by default. Each review can be marked as Fake, Spam or Unrelated to the product, which hides it from the store. When hiding a review, choose the reason for hiding it. [L1245.016]
- Moderation steps:
  1. Open Judge.me and go to the review section.
  2. Check the new reviews that appear, which are published by default.
  3. For any spam, fake or unrelated review, mark it as Fake, Spam or Unrelated to the product.
  4. Select the reason for hiding the review; it is then hidden from the store.
  5. Edit any review if needed. You receive a notification each time someone leaves a review.

  [L1245.017]
- Mistake: leaving spam or fake reviews visible on the store. Marking them as Fake, Spam or Unrelated hides them from the product page and keeps unrelated content off the store. [L1245.018]

### Judge.me: entering reviews, imports and migration

**Entering supplier reviews directly (procedure, with permission only):**

1. Confirm the supplier gives permission to use their reviews.
2. Enter reviews directly on your product pages.
3. Submit them the same way a customer would submit a review.
4. They will publish like normal customer reviews.

[L0155.013]

- Supplier reviews can be added one by one or imported with a spreadsheet upload (2025). [L0972.025]
- Judge.me can import reviews that were originally posted on eBay. Export the eBay reviews as a file first, then upload that file in Judge.me. This only works if you already have eBay feedback you want to show on your store. [L0941.105]
- The Judge.me importer is very important: install it so that existing reviews can be brought into the store (Source D 2026). [L1327.036]

**Migrating from an old review app (procedure):**

1. In Judge.me, go to the import function and click Import reviews to bring in existing reviews from the old app. Confirm the import succeeded.
2. Wait until Judge.me confirms by email that the app is installed.
3. In Shopify, remove the old review app(s), such as product reviews apps, after the import is done.
4. Also remove the old app Stamped.io once it is no longer needed.
5. If no reviews are currently being collected, the removal steps can be skipped.

[L0908.014]

- Do not remove an old review app before its reviews are imported into the new app. Once the import is complete, exporting settings from the old app is not needed for Judge.me. [L0908.015]
- Moving existing reviews from Stamped.io to Judge.me: open the import function in Judge.me, click Import reviews, and confirm that the import succeeded. Remove Stamped.io from the Shopify store only after the import is confirmed. [L0908.022]
- Older advice (2021): running two product review apps on the same store can show each review twice on the product page. Keep one review app, remove the other, and check the product pages. [L0801.186]

### Luke's Reviews

- Luke's Reviews collects, imports and shows product reviews and photos. It sends automatic review request emails after fulfillment and can run a referral and photo-review incentive. [L0476.037]
- The import-to-looks bookmark is a Chrome bookmark that copies review data from an AliExpress product page into Luke's Reviews for import. [L0476.038]

**Setup (procedure):**

1. Go to Apps, open Luke's product reviews and photos, click Install, and scroll down to install the app.
2. Click Continue as free.
3. Follow the setup: in the theme editor turn on the app embed and click save.
4. Add the review widget below the product description and click save.
5. Add the rating widget below the product title and click save.
6. Choose the store language and icon color, then add your branding colors.
7. Set the review request email: choose how many days after fulfillment the email is sent. Pick a number based on your supplier's delivery time (the lesson used 21 days).
8. Set the photo and video incentive: a discount reward of 15 for photo reviews and 20 for video reviews, given automatically.
9. Optionally set up the referral program: the referred friend gets a discount (example: 10 off), the referrer gets a reward (example: $5), and you can set a minimum spend and choose whether to show it on the store.
10. Click continue through the remaining screens.

[L0476.034]

- Parallel setting summary: install Luke's Product Reviews and Photos from the Shopify app store (free plan), turn on the app embed in the theme editor, then add the review widget below the description and the rating widget below the product title. Set the review request email to be sent 21 days after fulfillment (adjust for the supplier's shipping time). Offer a discount for reviews of 15 for photos and 20 for videos. [L0634.029]
- Referral program settings: set the discount the referred customer receives (example: 10 off), the reward for the referrer (example: 5 dollars), and the minimum spend required. Choose whether to show this on the store. [L0634.030]
- **Email timing across apps:** Judge.me sends its review request 14 days after purchase by default [L0155.010]. In Luke's Reviews, the request is sent a number of days after fulfillment, set to the supplier's delivery time (21 days in the lesson) [L0476.034, L0634.029]. Use the app's own timing setting for each store.

**Import from AliExpress (procedure, version A):**

1. In Google Chrome, drag the import to looks button from the Luke's Reviews setup into the bookmarks bar.
2. Open the product page on AliExpress.
3. Click the import to looks bookmark, choose your store, and choose the product that the reviews belong to.
4. Set the rating filter to four stars and up.
5. Choose the countries the reviews should come from.
6. Turn on the option to import only reviews that include photos, if wanted, and set fetch most recent reviews to yes.
7. Click preview and import.
8. Accept each review you want with import, and reject the others.
9. Click Finish importing.

[L0476.035]

**Import from AliExpress (procedure, version B):**

1. Open Google Chrome and drag the Import to Looks button to the bookmarks bar.
2. Open the matching AliExpress product page.
3. Click the Import to Looks bookmark, choose your store and the product.
4. Set the rating filter to four stars and up.
5. Choose the countries the reviews should come from.
6. Enable only reviews with photos and fetch the most recent reviews.
7. Click Preview and import.
8. For each review, click Import to keep it or Reject to skip it.
9. Click Finish importing.

[L0634.031]

- The free Looks plan can import only 20 reviews per import. More reviews require a higher paid plan. [L0635.019]

### Ali Reviews, Loox and other review apps

- Ali Reviews is a paid review app. It lets you edit review text, names, pictures, product links and target country. It costs about $19 per month at lower volume and about $39.90 per month at high sales volume. The lesson notes that the high-volume price is more than Shopify's 29 USD monthly plan. [L0502.039]
- Judge.me and Loox are other review apps besides Ali Reviews. Ali Reviews allows editing of review text, names, pictures and target country. Compare these tools only on real customer reviews and conversion results. [L0502.045]
- Ali Reviews can add review images. Use it to publish reviews that answer specific buyer doubts, such as fit or size concerns. [L0773.063]
- The same lesson uses Ali Reviews images to show that a product does not have a concern. Judge.me and Ali Reviews are both review apps for Shopify. [L0774.025]
- Judge.me also adds review images, which can strengthen trust on product pages. [L0773.023]
- Riview imports reviews from AliExpress and Amazon, with more customization options than Loox. Both apps can add review sections to product pages, but Riview gives more layout flexibility. [L0321.019]
- Rightview can fetch 600 or more Amazon reviews for a product. Amazon Review Export takes only about 100 at a time. It exports a CSV that you can edit and re-import into a review app, and set to update automatically when new reviews appear. Most Shopify review apps do not allow editing. [L0335.050]
- Rivo was suggested as a cheaper alternative to Loox for early-stage stores. It has a 7 to 14-day free trial and a plan around $6 per month, and it offers review editing that other apps lack. [L1410.012]
- Right View is a product review app with review editing, presented as a cheaper alternative to Loox. It offers a free trial of 7 to 14 days and a plan around 6 USD, and it suits a store on a tight budget. [L1412.026]
- **Naming note:** the sources name this tool Riview, Rightview, Right View and Rivo. The exact name is not confirmed by the sources, so check the Shopify App Store before installing. [L0321.019, L0335.050, L1412.026, L1410.012]
- Ali Reviews, Judge.me and similar review apps can seed initial product reviews on a new store. Later, replace them with real customer reviews. [L0513.030]
- Older advice (2021): Stamped.io is a review platform. Its custom forms, menu and plans are as follows:
  - In the Reviews & UGC platform, go to Customize and then Add Custom Forms to add extra review questions, such as how the fit was, how the quality is, or the customer's age group. These questions collect buyer insight beyond the star rating. The platform recommends adding them once reviews start coming in. [L1298.035]
  - The main menu includes Home, Queue, Customers, Products, Analytics and Marketing. Under Moderation it has Reviews, Questions, Net Promoter Score, Checkout Comments, Visual UGC and Instagram. Loyalty & Rewards is a separate section. The Home page summarises the last 30 days of widget interactions, revenue and advocate traffic. [L1298.036]
  - The free-forever plan supports only standard product reviews, covering up to 50 orders. Photo reviews and other richer review types require a paid plan, starting with Basic at $23 per month for up to 200 orders. [L1298.038]
- Older advice (2021): Judge.me and Stamp.io are the most common review apps on Shopify. Ask other review tools, such as Yotpo, whether they can load the review title with the product name. [L1191.020]
- Older advice (2020): Stamped.io is an alternative review app with a similar function to Judge.me. [L1111.077]

### Junip

- Junip is a Shopify review app for collecting, managing and displaying product and store reviews. It runs review request flows and campaigns to start gathering reviews right away. [L0589.002]
- Junip sends customers an email asking them to leave a review. The email links to a mobile-first submission form. Review request flows and campaigns are set up inside Junip. [L0589.003]
- The submission form accepts a star rating and a text review by default. Customers are encouraged to add photos and videos. Custom questions can be added using product traits and customer attributes. [L0589.004]
- Photo and video reviews add more evidence than text alone. The vendor gives no figure for how much they raise conversion, so test them on your own store. [L0589.005]
- Junip review displays adapt automatically to the look and feel of the Shopify theme. No custom code is needed to match the brand. [L0589.006]
- Junip integrates with Klaviyo, OmniSend and PostScript, and offers API access for custom needs. These integrations connect review collection with existing email and SMS tools. [L0589.010]
- Junip offers a free 14-day trial that can be installed directly. Use the trial to test review requests and displays before paying. [L0589.011]

**Junip incentives:**

- Junip has a two-tier incentive system. The standard incentive covers all reviews. The immediate incentive covers only reviews that include a photo or video. [L1244.002]
- The standard incentive is given for any submitted review, regardless of its content. The reward does not depend on how good or detailed the review is. [L1244.003]
- The immediate incentive is given only when a review includes a photo or video. Text-only reviews do not receive this second-tier reward. [L1244.004]
- Junip generates a unique, single-use discount code for each eligible review that is submitted. The customer receives this code after submitting the review. [L1244.008]
- Path: Incentives > Add incentive > Add standard incentive. Under Incentive type, pick Free shipping, Percentage discount or Fixed discount, then set the amount. [L1244.005]
- Under Reward this to, choose "Verified buyers only (Recommended)" or "Anyone who leaves a review, including unverified buyers." The verified-only option is marked as the recommended choice. [L1244.006]

**Junip incentive setup (procedure):**

1. Go to Incentives and click Add incentive.
2. Click Add standard incentive.
3. Under Incentive type, select Free shipping, Percentage discount or Fixed discount.
4. Enter the amount for the chosen incentive type.
5. Under Reward this to, select Verified buyers only (Recommended), or Anyone who leaves a review, including unverified buyers.

[L1244.007]

### Video reviews

- VideoPeel lets customers record video reviews on their phone with very little effort. It is recommended because older methods of getting customers to record and upload video were more cumbersome (older advice, 2021-22). [L1011.010]
- VideoPeel records and uploads the video automatically, so customers can leave a video review from a phone in a few clicks (older advice, 2021-22). [L1012.018]
- **Conflict on dedicated tools:** review apps such as Judge.me or Stamp.io can also collect video reviews, depending on plan and features. A dedicated video tool is therefore optional if the review app already supports it (older advice, 2021-22). [L1011.011]

### Testimonial and UGC forms

- Typeform can host a testimonial request form. The form should collect first name, last name, a video upload, and a checkbox confirming that the customer agrees the content can be used in ads. [L0789.059]
- Jotform is used to build a customer review and UGC submission form with file upload, email field and category choice. A ready template can be cloned from the link provided and adapted with the store name. [L0605.009]
- Testimonial collection feeds social proof on product and store pages. Pair it with the review apps above, so testimonials and reviews appear in one consistent place. [L0040.003, L0589.001]

### Announcement bars

- A Quick Announcement Bar is a thin banner at the top of a store used to show short messages such as shipping offers or promotions (older advice, 2021). [L1089.005]
- Quick Announcement Bar by Hextom is a Shopify app with a free plan. It displays a sitewide bar at the top of the store, and the free plan covers this setup, so premium options are not required. [L0531.010]
- Older advice (2020): the app supports promotional messages such as coupon codes or floating bar alerts. It supports dynamic text that changes based on product collections. [L0245.031]
- Quick Announcement Bar by Hextom is an expiring coupon banner that can be excluded from specific pages or collections. Use it when some suppliers forbid discount codes, so the banner does not appear on their product pages (older advice, 2021). [L1090.055]
- **Conflict on advanced targeting:**
  - Quick Announcement Bar is the fallback when a store's theme has no built-in announcement bar section. Its free plan covers a sitewide code bar. The paid tier adds advanced page targeting and custom code options that this setup does not need. [L0531.020]
  - Older advice (2021): in the app, use the Advanced targeting option to exclude specific vendors or collections so the bar does not show on their pages. The app's conditional logic is free to use. [L0671.053, L0671.192]
  - Use the 2021 value only if the free advanced targeting is still available in the app. Otherwise use the paid tier that the other unit describes. [L0531.020, L0671.053, L0671.192]

**Install (procedure):**

1. In the Shopify admin, open Apps and click Visit app store.
2. Search for quick announcement bar and select the app by Hextom.
3. Choose the free plan and click Add app.
4. Grant the requested access and click Install app.
5. To return later, open Apps in the admin and click the app.

[L0531.014]

**Create and style the bar (procedure):**

1. In the app's dashboard, click create new bar.
2. Choose one of the basic templates. The lesson used a blue template.
3. Enter an internal name for the bar, for example save 10, which is not shown to visitors.
4. Paste the message text into the message field. The preview appears immediately.
5. Replace the old code and date in the message with the current code and the next expiry date.
6. Set "Is the bar clickable?" to no.
7. Set the close button to no.
8. Keep the display position at the top, which makes the bar sticky.
9. Set the background and text colors with the color picker to match the store theme, or enter specific color codes.
10. Keep the font consistent with the store, and leave font size and bar padding at their defaults unless a change is needed.
11. Click Save.

[L0531.015]

- The bar's target configuration is at its default of showing on all pages. Keep it set to every page for a sitewide code, and make sure the bar is shown on both desktop and mobile. [L0531.016]
- If different suppliers need different coupon codes, exclude the pages of the brands that should not show the current code. Paste their URLs or a keyword from their URLs into the app's exclusion field. This is optional and depends on the store's profit margins. [L0531.017]

### Surveys: post-purchase feedback

- A post-purchase survey asks buyers why they bought, what their pain points are, and what they expect. It is useful for stores that do not yet have enough own customer data. Re-convert runs such surveys. [L0902.007]
- Post-purchase surveys ask customers questions after the purchase flow. Their results show which messages and angles customers respond to. [L1141.029]
- A post-purchase survey app can ask buyers why they bought and how they use the product. The exact app name was not clear in the recording. [L1213.032]
- Post-purchase surveys reach people who have already paid, not random visitors. Use them to learn why customers bought, what problem they had, and what result they want. [L0906.001]
- A single on-site survey can run through an online chat widget or Hotjar. Visitors answer simple questions on the site to give feedback on their experience (older advice, 2021-22). [L0399.016]
- Feedback forms do not need polished design. A simple option such as Google Forms is enough. Spend the effort on writing specific questions, not on the look of the survey (older advice, 2021-22). [L0682.025]

### Surveys: Zigpoll

- Zigpoll is a post-purchase survey tool. It asks customers about their pain points, wants and objections. The responses help decide what to say in ads. [L0351.042]
- Zigpoll is a survey app that asks customers questions after they have bought, so answers come from people who already paid. Responses can be exported as a CSV file for analysis. [L1140.001]
- Zigpoll connects to third-party tools such as Slack, so survey responses can be passed on to other business apps. Check the integration options in the app before building a separate export process. [L1140.032]
- A Zigpoll post-purchase survey appears on its own for buyers right after they buy. No separate invitation link is needed. Set it up as a post-purchase survey so responses come from people who have already paid. [L0906.024]
- Zigpoll setup rules:
  - Set at most five questions per survey, each with four to five answer options. [L0906.002]
  - Reorder questions with drag and drop under Slide Management, and check the Survey Preview before publishing. [L0906.002]
  - Set open-ended questions to the Long Answer question type, and keep closed questions as multiple-choice with four to five options. Use Long Answer where you want the customer's exact words about their problem, trigger or result. [L0906.025, L1140.010]
  - Reorder the slides with Slide Management by dragging and dropping them into place. [L1140.010]
- Rewards: a Zigpoll survey can show a promo code on its completion screen as the reward for finishing. Alternatively, offer a monthly cash prize draw for one respondent, for example $1,000, if a discount is not suitable for your margin. [L0906.026]
- Export: open the survey's responses in the Zigpoll dashboard and use the export button to download a CSV file. If the button is hard to find, Zigpoll support can point it out. [L1140.012]
- Analysis setup: create a Claude project for the product, add the exported survey CSV to the project's knowledge, and start all analysis chats inside that project so the file stays available. [L1140.013]

**Analysing responses (procedure):**

1. Wait until you have a usable number of survey responses, around 50, 100, or 200 or more.
2. Download the responses from Zigpoll.
3. Paste the responses into Claude.
4. Add the responses to your Claude project knowledge.
5. Ask Claude to find common pain points, common desires, objections, frequently used phrases, emotional patterns, new ad angles and new hooks.

[L0906.006]

### Referral and loyalty

- Smile.io is the recommended loyalty app for Shopify stores. It is simple to set up, highly customizable, and lets the store owner set the reward rate for their products. Other loyalty apps exist in the Shopify App Store if Smile.io does not fit (older advice, 2021-22). [L0956.003]
- Luke's Reviews includes a referral program, set up as described in the Luke's Reviews section above. [L0634.030]
- Older advice (2021-22): a referral app gives each customer who signs up the tools needed to refer others, such as a link or code and ready-made shares. The app tracks every referral made through that link or code. [L1234.006]
- Referral tracking works like affiliate marketing. Each participating customer uses a unique link or code, and the system attributes purchases to that customer. This is how a referral is credited to the person who made it (older advice, 2021-22). [L1234.008]
- Setup of a referral app is largely automatic. Install it, then configure it for the business and the chosen reward model. Once configured, the app generates the materials customers need to refer others (older advice, 2021-22). [L1234.009]
- Refersion is a referral tool that can also run affiliate arrangements. This is useful when working with bloggers or forum owners. It is one of the recommended tools for referral programs (older advice, 2021-22). [L1234.004]
- Referral Candy is another recommended referral app. Further referral apps are available in the Shopify app store (older advice, 2021-22). [L1234.005]
- Talkable is a referral and rewards app that costs about $2,000 and includes fraud protection. It is useful when referral volume becomes too high to approve each referral manually. [L0790.017]
- Friendbuy is an affordable referral app for running a friend-referral program in your own store. It is an alternative to Talkable for a similar purpose. [L0790.018]
- Klaviyo can automate review request emails once manual follow-up volume becomes too high. Use it after the first reviews are collected manually (older advice, 2020). [L1111.078]

### Trust badges

- Trustpilot Reviews adds external review ratings to the store. It is a download-only install at the foundation stage. [L1327.037]
- Judge.me badges can give the homepage a more authoritative look, as described in the Judge.me section above. [L1363.019]

#### Further details

- Judge.me is used for product reviews on Shopify stores. [L0857.063]

- Judge.me is a Shopify app for collecting, managing and displaying product reviews on a store. It is the review tool used when setting up customer reviews on a Shopify shop. [L1008.002]

## Upsells, Bundles, Cart and Post-Purchase Offers

### Choosing the offer stack

- Bundles, quantity breaks, upsells and cross-sells are usually built with dedicated apps, and several apps exist for each job, so compare them before choosing one [L0435.018].
- Bundle and upsell apps are an option once the store has more than one product [L0774.019]. Complementary-bundle apps work best when the store has a strong product that can be sold as an upsell [L0650.070].
- Older advice (Source A, 2021): add post-purchase and cross-sell apps only once the store grows and average order value matters, because they are not needed on day one [L0968.046].
- Older advice (Source A, 2021): do not use urgency timers or urgency apps unless there is a specific real deadline, such as a delivery date before Christmas [L0968.048].
- Timer values in the sources differ by context. Kaching Cart and OCU setups use a 3-minute timer [L0560.012, L1141.016]. A 20-minute deadline appears as an example in post-purchase SMS and email text [L0789.033]. Use any timer only with a real deadline, as the older rule states [L0968.048].
- For free-gift content, use ChatGPT to brainstorm bundle names, offer copy and persuasive framing before you build offers in the store [L0232.020].
- For free-gift content, use ChatGPT to create instructional eBooks, email sequences, guides and digital resources. They cost nothing to produce but add perceived value to higher-tier bundles [L0232.026].
- A native Shopify discount system can apply a discount automatically when a customer adds 2 or 3 units. The discount appears without codes or third-party apps, which reduces friction in multi-buy decisions [L0233.015].
- Planning helper: the BSGR Product Upsell Planner (Google Sheets) can be exported via File > Download as.xlsx,.ods,.pdf,.html (zipped),.csv (current sheet only) or.tsv (current sheet only) [L0448.024].

### Bundle Bear

#### What it does
- Bundle Bear creates product bundles, volume discounts and free-gift offers in Shopify [L0634.025].
- Offer types, as described by the app: volume discount (more items, bigger discount), mix and match (discount for different products bought together) and cart goal (discount when the cart reaches a set value, e.g. $50) [L0477.028].
- Bundle and volume discount are the two most-used types [L0476.029].
- Volume discount example: buy 2 and save 10%, buy 3 and save 15% [L1220.025].
- Bundle option for two complementary products: save 10 dollars when buying both, or save 20 dollars when buying two items [L0476.029, L0634.025].
- Free-gift bundle: set the second product as a free gift and select which product is the gift [L0476.029, L1220.026].
- Bundle link with no discount: shows that both items can be bought together [L0476.029, L1220.026].
- Example offer from one store: buy two, get 20% off plus a free gift [L0549.027].
- Discount formats differ by source. Fixed-dollar amounts (10 or 20 dollars) are used for the two-item bundle, and percentages (10% and 15%) for volume tiers. Use the format the offer type requires [L0634.025, L1220.025].
- The lesson used the Bundle and Volume discount options. Advanced settings include a must-choose-option rule, country limits, start and end dates and rounded prices [L0634.025].
- Economics reported in the lesson: a 40 to 50 percent bundle take rate and roughly $40 extra profit per order. Treat these as reported results, not guarantees [L0650.049, L0650.070].

#### Setup procedure
1. Install Bundle Bear from the Shopify App Store and click Install app.
2. Click Create offer.
3. Choose the offer type. Bundle and volume discount are the two most-used types.
4. For a bundle, select the first product and the second product. Choose the discount: save 10 when buying both items, or save 20 when buying two items. Alternatively, set the second product as a free gift and select which product is the gift. Or choose no discount to show that both items can be bought together.
5. Open advanced settings. Choose whether the customer must choose an option, limit the offer to a country, set a start date and end date, and enable Round discounted prices with.99 as the ending.
6. Save the offer.
7. Open Design to change alignment, colors, and the button color to match your store. Change the text if the ads target a country with a different language.
8. Click save.
9. Open Embeds in the theme editor, check the installed apps, and switch Bundle Bear on.
10. Open the product section, click Add block, and choose Bundle Bear. Drag it below the buy buttons.
11. Check the layout on a mobile phone, then save. [L0476.029]

- Offer options: click Add option to add tiers. Advanced settings include custom badges, start and end time for urgency, and rounding of discounted prices (e.g. to 50 cents or per dollar). Click save and activate [L0477.028].
- Placement by source (sources differ, so test them):

| Source | Placement | When it applies |
|---|---|---|
| L0477.028 | Below the quantity selector on the product page | Offer block placement in the app's setup |
| L0476.029 (step 10), L0634.027 | Below the buy buttons, not below the discount | Theme block placement via Embeds and Add block |
| L0549.027 | Below product image and title, above the regular Buy Now button | One store reported this position drove most of its sales and raised average order value |

- Mobile is the most important view, so check the layout on a phone before publishing [L0634.027, L0476.029].

### Kaching Bundles

- Kaching Bundles is a Shopify app for building product bundles with quantity breaks, Buy X Get Y deals, subscriptions and free gifts [L0861.001, L0862.016].
- It creates quantity-break tiers, such as 2-pack and 3-pack offers with percentage discounts, directly on the product page. It is the tool used to build bundle tiers [L0860.019].
- Use it for all bundle offers, starting with a basic setup [L1299.001]. It is also listed as the bundle tool in the build [L1149.012].
- Find it by searching the Shopify App Store, install it and approve the billing permissions [L0861.001].
- Billing: the app bills from the first customer checkout that uses a bundle, with a fee of about $15 in the setup shown [L0861.002]. Reported as $15 from the first checkout made through the app [L0862.016]. Verify the current price and billing terms inside the app before publishing live bundles [L0861.002, L0862.016].
- The dashboard shows Added revenue, Orders with bundles, visitors and AOV for each bundle deal. Filters for 24 hours, 7 days, 30 days and All time show deal performance. The Store widget status must read enabled [L0861.021].
- The optional store widget displays the bundle offers in the store [L0862.016].
- Kaching suite: Kaching Cart, Kaching Bundles, Kaching Upsell, Kaching Popup and Kaching Subscriptions are separate apps. Each adds its own blocks to the product page or cart drawer in the theme editor [L0560.010, L1188.020].

### Rebuy dynamic bundles

#### Creating the bundle widget
1. In Rebuy, go to Widgets > Product Page > New Widget.
2. Scroll to Dynamic Bundle, click Create new, name it Dynamic Bundle Offer and confirm with Create it! [L0557.001]
- The default data source rule for a new dynamic bundle is "if anything, return recommended AI". It compares the main product with products other customers bought together with it [L0557.002].
- A purchase-based recommendation rule works well as a starting point for bundles because it needs no manual product picking. It relies on what customers actually buy alongside the main product [L0557.003].
- To create or edit a dynamic bundle, go to the product page tile in the Rebuy app and select the widget. If none exists, create the dynamic bundle widget there first, then open its Data Sources page from the widget [L0559.025].

#### Widget ID
- The widget ID appears in the widget header (e.g. Dynamic Bundle Offer (ID: 117326)) and as the number in the edit page address, /widgets/id/<ID>/edit. Copy this number for the Shopify app block field or the manual div [L0557.021, L0557.005].

#### Installing with Shopify App Blocks
1. Copy the widget ID shown in the widget header (e.g. Dynamic Bundle Offer (ID: 117326)).
2. Click Install (top right) in the Rebuy widget editor, then click add block to my online store.
3. In Shopify's theme customizer, open the product page template.
4. Add a block (new section or existing section) and choose Rebuy Widget under APP BLOCKS.
5. Paste the widget ID into the Widget ID field.
6. Click Save.
7. Open a product page to confirm the bundle shows. [L0557.005]
- The Rebuy Widget app block has these fields: Enable Widget (on), Widget ID, Enable Product ID (lets the widget use the product ID to return products based on the data source) and Enable Collection ID (lets the widget use the collection ID for the same purpose) [L0557.006].
- App blocks only appear in themes built for Shopify Online Store 2.0. Use the manual code method if the app block cannot be installed [L0557.007].

#### Installing manually in theme files
1. In the widget editor, open Placement, expand Manual installation and copy the widget div.
2. Open the theme's three-dot menu and click Edit Code.
3. Open layout/theme.liquid.
4. Add the line RenderRebuyExtensions to theme.liquid, checking the exact Liquid call against Rebuy's install guide.
5. Create a new file snippets/rebuyExtensions.liquid.
6. In that file, write an "if product" block that contains the copied widget div and close it with "end if".
7. Save, open a product page and scroll down to check the widget. [L0557.009]

#### Editor layout
- Widget editor tabs: General, Language, Data Source, Discount, Timer, Layout, Placement and Images. Preview toggles between Desktop and Mobile, Live Mode is available, and Install and Save sit at the top right [L0557.008].

### Rebuy data sources for bundles

#### Setting up a data source
1. Open the Widgets section in Rebuy and select the dynamic bundle widget (create one first if none exists).
2. Open the widget's Data Sources page.
3. Click Add Rule and choose the rule type: Product Tags, Collection or Specific Products.
4. Set the condition: for tags use Contains Any and enter the tag; for a collection select the collection; for specific products select the products.
5. Enter the rule title that shoppers will see.
6. If the bundle should show a discount, set Widget Discount to Percentage and enter the value.
7. Turn on Exit if Matched if rules below this one should be ignored when it matches.
8. Click Save and confirm the message that the data source was saved.
9. Create the matching automatic discount in Shopify (see the Shopify procedure) and test the bundle on the storefront. [L0559.002]

| Rule type | Use it to | Example | Condition used |
|---|---|---|---|
| Product Tags | Pull in every product that carries a shared tag | Tea products | Contains Any + tag name (tea) |
| Collection | Pull in all items of one collection | Jewelry collection | Collection selected |
| Specific Products | Build a fixed set around one product | Supercharge drink plus accessory products | Individually selected products |
[L0559.003]

- Tag rule example: Product Tags, Contains Any, value tea makes every tea product appear as bundle items on a tea product page. The shopper picks which teas to include before adding the bundle to the cart [L0559.004].
- Collection rule example: a jewelry collection fills the bundle with options from that collection. Adding the bundle puts all selected items in the cart at once, which reduces shopper fatigue on large ranges [L0559.005].
- Specific-products rule example: a hand-picked set, such as a main product plus complementary accessories, suits a single high-margin product that should always be sold with the same add-ons [L0559.006].
- The rule editor shows the rule type, a condition field with operators such as Contains Any, a title field, a sort option (Smart Sort or Recommended (AI)) and the Exit if Matched toggle. Further buttons are Add Before, Add After, Duplicate, Remove and Collapse. Save the data source with Save [L0559.007].
- Rules are evaluated in order. When Exit if Matched is enabled on a rule, every rule after it is ignored if that rule matches. Put narrow or specific rules above broad ones so the broad rule does not swallow them [L0559.008].
- Each rule's conditions can be joined with AND or OR. Add rules at a position with Add Before or Add After. Use connectors to combine several tags or products in one rule instead of building many separate rules [L0559.009].
- Under the data source's filter settings, Filter out of stock products and Filter out of stock variants keep unavailable items out of the bundle. An Add Exclusion filter option removes specific items from the input [L0559.010].
- The data source name is only visible internally, so use a name that makes the bundle purpose clear to your team. A Clear Cache button on the data source page helps if the widget does not update after changes [L0559.011].
- Mistake: testing before the Shopify automatic discount is active shows the widget discount but no discount in the cart, which looks like a bug. Create and activate the Shopify discount first, then test in a cleared cart [L0559.021].
- Bundle items are added to the cart as separate products, not as one bundle SKU, so there is no separate bundle inventory. Stock is managed per item as usual [L0559.023].
- Shopify automatic discounts apply without a code. They are the method used to make a Rebuy widget discount real, and they can combine with other active discounts if that option is enabled [L0559.024].
- Rebuy on a product page lets shoppers tick several products and variants and bundle them together. When this builder adds too many clicks, replace it with ready-made bundles that carry a fixed extra discount [L1225.089].

### Rebuy product-page widgets

| Widget type | What it does |
|---|---|
| AI Recommendations | Shows items customers also bought, based on the product being viewed |
| Recently Viewed | Shows cross-device browsing history so customers can buy previously viewed products |
| Add to Cart Upsell (Popup) | Popup at add-to-cart offering a higher-value alternative |
| Add to Cart Subscription Upsell (Popup) | Popup at add-to-cart offering a subscription instead of a one-time purchase |
| Add to Cart Cross-Sell (Popup) | Popup at add-to-cart asking if extra items should be added |
| Product Add-Ons | Lets customers add accessories to the cart when the main product is added |
| Featured Items | Shows specific products or collections, e.g. new products, top sellers or sale items |
| Buy It Again | Shows previously purchased items, falling back to top-selling items |
[L0557.004]

### ReBuy as a platform

- ReBuy is a Shopify app that uses AI to personalize product recommendations across the shopping journey. Stated goals: higher average order value, conversion rate and customer lifetime value [L0593.001].
- According to the vendor, ReBuy can be set up on a Shopify store in minutes without coding or developer skills. Treat this as a vendor claim and verify it on your own store [L0593.002].
- Custom rules can be stacked on top of each other to control which products appear where and how often. This supports a deliberate merchandising strategy instead of default recommendations [L0593.003].
- ReBuy connects directly to other tools in the store's tech stack and works with several third-party integrations. Check the integration list against your own apps before installing [L0593.009].

### Product-page upsells and cross-sells (Bold and related apps)

#### Bold apps
- Bold Upsell, sold by Bold under the name Product Upsell, adds upsell products to product pages to present complementary items at the point of sale [L0247.080].
- Bold Product Upsell is a paid Shopify app that shows related accessories to shoppers. It offers a 14-day free trial, then costs just under $10 per month [L0469.056] (Source A, 2021, older).
- Bold Upsell costs 10 to 90 dollars per month depending on how many people see the upsells. A store with low traffic typically pays 10 to 20 dollars per month. Each upsell takes about 15 to 30 minutes to configure [L1076.043].
