- The feed app's Sync Settings page has product submission options: Variant Submission Preference (First Variant Only or All Variants), Append Variant Name to Product Title (Yes or No), Inventory Policy (Follow or Ignore), Product Title Preference (Default Product Title or SEO Title), Product Description Preference (Default Product Description or SEO Description), Product Image Option, Submit Additional Images, and Submit a Rich Product Description with HTML tags. These options control what the Google Shopping feed sends to Merchant Center for each product. [L0402.014]

- The product feed is submitted in the same currency as the Shopify store's default currency. To change the feed currency, change the currency in the Shopify store settings. [L0402.015]

- Product feed apps work between the Shopify store and Google Merchant Center to optimize product feeds. Options named in the lesson are Flexify (a free plugin with a Facebook feed focus), Data Feed Watch, Feed Optimize, Feedonomics and Go Data Feed, and one popular app costs about $5 per month. Choose by pricing model and features. [L0454.026]

- Feed tools flag issues that Google Merchant Center does not, such as HTML in product descriptions or special characters like the ampersand, which can export as broken characters. Use a feed grade check to catch these problems automatically. [L0454.029]

- Feed tools can export one product feed to Google Shopping, Facebook Shop, Instagram product posts, Pinterest buyable pins, eBay, Bing, Amazon and affiliate networks such as ShareASale. They also handle currency conversion and multi-country export. Amazon is only an option where the supplier allows dropshipping there. [L0454.030]

- Feed tools can scan competitor product pages and search results to detect MAP pricing violations and alert you. They can also help manage product ratings and seller ratings. The manual alternative is to have an assistant check for violations. [L0454.032]

- Software prices for product feed tools can often be negotiated, so ask the vendor before accepting list pricing. Compare tools on pricing model and features rather than picking one only by reputation. [L0454.044]

- In the Google & YouTube app on Shopify, connect only your Google Analytics 4 property. If the feed app already manages the Merchant Center connection, the Merchant Center connection should not be linked inside the Google & YouTube app. [L0470.049]

- To reconnect a product feed through a feed app such as Simprosys, use the feed app's connect option for Google Merchant Center and select the Content API as the input method, rather than Google Sheets or scheduled fetch. [L0470.050]

- A product feed management app connected to Shopify is used to submit products to Google Merchant Center and to manage the tracking tags. [L0472.040]

- Shopping feed management tools such as Feed Optimize, Data Feed Watch and GoDataFeed let you change product data before sending it to Google and Facebook. They can also pool data from Google Ads and Bing Ads to show a combined CTR. [L0510.031]

Shopping feed management process:
1) Take product data from Shopify.
2) Modify the fields that influence CTR, such as images, titles, descriptions and review data.
3) Send the optimized feed to Google Shopping.
4) Send the same feed to Facebook for dynamic product ads.
[L0510.032]

- Google Shopping (formerly Product Listing Ads) is described as the highest-quality, cheapest traffic for e-commerce, and every store should run it. Its main threats are the product feed going offline, prices being disapproved, or poor data quality. [L0575.004]

- Google Merchant Center's default feed is free; Go Data Feed is a paid feed-management tool (around $60-70 per month for three feeds, more above that). It distributes the feed to hundreds of channels such as Amazon, eBay and price comparison sites, and lets you set rules to optimize and enrich feed data. [L0575.007]

- The Shopify Google Shopping app publishes products to Google Merchant Center when the product availability box is checked. Use one Google Shopping app and avoid mixing duplicates. [L0664.075]

- A product feed app should fill in as many columns as possible so Google can match products to searches better. [L0665.050]

- Connect the product feed to Google Merchant Center through an app that refreshes it every 24 hours. Do not rely on manual Excel exports, since a price change that is not updated in the feed causes mismatches. [L0671.084]

- A built-in TikTok shop feed was offered as a theme feature to replace a paid tool. The speaker claimed that the paid tool costs $10,000 a year, a figure that was not verified during the lesson. [L0709.042]

- The app 'Feeds for Google Shopping' by Simprosys InfoMedia formats Shopify product data to Google Merchant Center's required format and sends it automatically. Manual spreadsheet feeds are error-prone and time-consuming, so an app is used instead. [L0731.020]

- To install the feed app, open the Shopify App Store from the Apps section of the Shopify menu, search for 'feeds for google shopping', select Feeds For Google Shopping by Simprosys InfoMedia, then click Install app. [L0731.021]

- Connect the feed app to Merchant Center: 1. Log in with the Google account used to create the Merchant Center account. 2. Allow the app access to the account. 3. Select the Merchant Center ID, checking it matches the ID shown in the top right corner. 4. Confirm the account. 5. In Merchant Center, go to Growth > Manage programs and click Get started under Shopping ads. 6. Return to the app and confirm that programs have been enabled. [L0731.022]

- In the feed app's Quick Sync Settings, choose All Products for which products are submitted, Default Product Title and Default Product Description, and All Variants for variant submission. Set tax to 'Yes, prices include tax' if the shown prices include tax, and enter a notification email. [L0731.024]

- Product ID format options in the feed app are Global Format (e.g. shopify_US_123456_987654, the default), SKU as Product ID (used when Judge.me reviews are installed), and Variant ID as Product ID (needed for the Facebook product catalog feed). Choose the global format unless one of the exceptions applies. [L0731.025]

- The feed app has a free trial of 22 days, after which it costs about $5 per month (price at time of recording in 2021). The app must be activated before the trial ends, or it stops sending product data and the ads stop running. [L0731.030]

- After the Merchant Center connection succeeds, the Feeds for Google Shopping app shows Quick Sync Settings for product submission. A SWITCH TO ADVANCE INTEGRATION button leads to the more detailed feed optimization settings, which are only needed for a non-standard setup. [L0731.038]

- The feed app needs permission to manage Google Ads campaigns, product listings and Google Shopping accounts. Access must be allowed during sign-in, or the app cannot link Google Ads or insert the remarketing and conversion tags. [L0731.041]

- Avoid the old native Shopify Google feed plugin, which had poor reviews. It only worked for US currency, failed for customers in Canada, submitted the highest shipping cost instead of the lowest, and was poorly maintained. [L0732.006]

- Symprosis is a paid Shopify app with a small monthly fee that syncs products to Google Merchant Center. It is recommended for keeping feed data current and reliable. [L0732.007]

Connect a feed app such as Symprosis to Merchant Center:
1. Install the app and choose to sync your data.
2. Enter your Merchant Center ID (a numeric ID shown in Merchant Center) and confirm the account.
3. Check the warnings the app shows, especially a missing default shipping rate for the country.
4. Wait a few minutes for the data to be processed and check that products appear in Merchant Center.
[L0732.008]

- Feed management apps for Merchant Center typically cost a few dollars per month. The fee is considered worth it for reliable, automatically updated feeds. [L0732.014]

- A feed management app that communicates with Merchant Center can find more errors than Google's diagnostics alone, and it tells you exactly which field to fix. Using such an app speeds up error correction and improves product visibility in Google Shopping. [L0732.022]

- Feeds for Google Shopping is the Shopify app used to submit product feeds. After approval, it hosts the switch that enables Google Shopping Actions and where the additional Shopping Actions feed data is submitted. [L0738.023]

- Order Sync for Google Shopping, made by Simprosys, brings Shopping Actions orders from Merchant Center into Shopify. Without it, orders are visible only inside Merchant Center and no notification reaches the merchant, so install it only after approval. [L0738.024]

- Custom labels can be added either in the Feed for Google Shopping app or through the Shopify CSV bulk upload. The CSV method is the quicker option for many products, because the Shopify CSV template already includes the Google custom label fields. [L0747.016]

- The Feed for Google Shopping app does not automatically pick up changes to certain Shopify meta fields. Shopify does not notify the app when SEO title, SEO description, custom labels, age group, gender or similar fields change, so these must be re-synced manually in the app. [L0747.017]

- Feed for Google Shopping > Settings options: Which Products Needs to be Submitted in Feed (All Products or Products from a Collection); Product Title Preference (Default Product Title or SEO Title); Product Description Preference (Default Product Description or SEO Description); Variant Submission Preference (First Variant Only or All Variants); Append Variant Name to Product Title (Yes or No); Inventory Policy (Follow or Ignore); Product Image Option (use second image for a product with no variant); Submit Additional Images (checkbox); Submit a Rich Product Description (checkbox, uses HTML tags). The feed currency follows the Shopify store default currency and is changed in the Shopify store settings. [L0747.018]

- The Feed for Google Shopping app offers a free sign-up for additional attributes to submit rich product data, including to Facebook and Instagram Shops. [L0747.019]

- The Feed for Google Shopping product list has tabs for Submitted, Submitted with Warnings and Excluded. Above them is a panel titled Errors from Merchant Center with a Refresh Product Errors button, and it shows when the error data was last refreshed, so refresh it before acting on an older error list. [L0747.021]

- The Feed for Google Shopping app's Settings menu holds Sync Settings From Shopify, Google Shopping Settings, Google Shopping Actions, Bing Shopping Settings, Facebook Catalog Settings, XML Feed Settings and Google Local Inventory. Sync Settings From Shopify is the page that pulls Shopify field changes into the feed. [L0747.022]

- The Feed for Google Shopping Default Settings section sets fallback values for Default Google Product Category, Default Age Group, Default Gender, Default Product Condition and Default Product Identifier Management. These defaults apply to products that do not have their own value in Shopify, so they should be checked before the first feed submission. [L0747.023]

- Google Product Category values in the feed must be the full Google taxonomy path with ' > ' between each level, for example Health & Beauty > Personal Care > Hair Care. A category that is only the last word, or a path from a different taxonomy version, does not match the Google category list. [L0747.024]

- Product feed apps automate sending your product data to advertising platforms. Separate apps exist for Google, Bing and Facebook because each platform has slightly different feed requirements. [L0762.010]

- Feed apps update the feed about once a day with stock status, price changes, new images and new titles. Optimizations made to products on the store, such as better titles, then reach Google Ads automatically. [L0762.011]

- The Google Shopping Feed app checks each product manually and submits it to Google Merchant Center, helping avoid bans and fix suspensions. It is recommended before starting Google Ads and costs around $3 to $4 per month or is free. [L0788.025]

- Google Merchant Center is used to receive the product feed for Google Shopping. The store feed is connected through the API so product data stays current. [L0801.108]

- Feed for Google Shopping is the Shopify app that sends product data to Google Merchant Center and offers more control than the built-in Google channel. [L0801.109]

- Google Merchant Center holds product data for Google Shopping; product details must be complete so Google can match your products with search queries and generate impressions. [L0834.056]

- The feed app for Google Shopping is Simprosys, which connects the store to Google Merchant Center and submits the product feed. Use the feed app's submission path, not the Google sales channel's Merchant Center listing option. [L0835.133]

- Feed for Google Shopping is a Shopify App Store app that handles the Google product feed and provides conversion tracking. It is the recommended tool for connecting Shopify to Google. [L0852.074]

- Google Shopping feed app is used to submit product identifiers, with settings for brand, MPN, SKU and GTIN handling. [L0855.112]

- Symprosis is a manual control tool for Google Shopping feed management used alongside the Google & YouTube app. [L0856.073]

- Shopify apps can generate and sync a Bing shopping feed from inside the store. The workflow matches the Google feed setup, so the same app and steps can be reused. [L0866.008]

- Feed for Google Shopping connects the Shopify product feed to Google Merchant Center via the API. It updates stock status, price, title, description, SKU, photos and promotions within about 20 minutes. It is preferred over the Google channel app. [L0962.004]

- A product CSV uploaded directly to Google Merchant Center does not update automatically and only holds the data from that file. An API-based feed pushes every store change to Merchant Center in near real time, which is why price and stock changes need it. [L0962.005]

- The Feed for Google Shopping promotion setting applies only to Google Shopping campaigns. It does not carry promotions over to Facebook or other connected accounts, so those must be set up separately. [L0962.151]

- Feed for Google Shopping lets you modify the product data sent to Google and Facebook. Use it to send only part of a long description or to adjust fields instead of editing product pages. [L0963.048]

- If a Facebook feed rejects a product for exceeding character limits, shorten what is submitted through the feed app. Do not delete the content from the product page to make it fit. [L0963.049]

- Google Merchant Center holds the product feed for Google Shopping. Custom labels in the feed can be used to group products so that a campaign can be bulk edited. [L0966.069]

- Shopify apps that connect the store to Google Merchant Center pull product data automatically. Depending on the settings, the feed can be pushed to campaigns manually or imported automatically, for example once every 24 hours. [L0967.054]

- Google Merchant Center holds the product feed used for free product listings and paid Google Shopping ads. Products that are approved there can appear in free listings, which start giving data within days. [L0968.050]

- To disconnect Merchant Center from the Google & YouTube app: open the app in Shopify, find Merchant Center under Connected services, click the three dots, click Manage settings, and disconnect it. Keep the Google Analytics connection active, since it is used for sales tracking. [L0972.027]

- The Google & YouTube app should be used only to connect the Shopify store to Google Analytics. Product feed management is handled separately through a dedicated feed app. [L0972.028]

- A dedicated product feed app gives more control over the feed, such as including or excluding certain products, than the default Google feed. Check its settings for automatic daily sync. [L0972.029]

## Advertising Platforms, Tracking and Attribution

### Ad Accounts, Ownership and Risk

- A personal ad account has no dedicated support, and once it is banned it is gone. An agency account is the safer choice for scaling, because the agency handles support and replacement accounts. [L0351.033]
- The Kuro Network client portal menu lists: Ad Accounts, Request Top-Up, Funds Transfer, Request BM Transfer, Request Unban, Request BM Verification and Support Chat. [L0351.055]
- Meta Business Manager (business.facebook.com) is free and holds the Facebook page, Instagram page, pixel and audiences. Audiences are created there under the menu Audiences. [L0649.056]
- A Meta Ads account and a Google Ads account, each with remarketing audiences built, are required before a holiday promotion starts. [L1221.009]
- Older advice (2020): to link Instagram to a Facebook Business account without mixing personal profiles, log in to Facebook Business Manager > Settings > Assets > Add Instagram Account, and select only your business Instagram account. [L0245.073]
- Microsoft Advertising is Bing's paid advertising platform and needs its own account. It is separate from Bing Webmaster Tools, which is free and only reports on organic search performance. [L0303.015]
- Older advice (2020): Bing offered a start credit of about 75 to 100 USD for new advertisers. Search for the Bing credit offer and sign up through the Microsoft link to claim it. [L1277.068]

### Google Ads Tools and Setup

- Google Ads management tools: Keyword Planner for keyword research, Google Ads Settings for campaign configuration, Google Shopping for product listing ads, and AdWords Express for simplified ad creation. [L0020.005]
- The native Google Ads dashboard is free and needs no add-on. Supermetrics is an add-on that should be treated as paid, so check current pricing before budgeting for it. Start with the native dashboard and add Supermetrics when a combined multi-platform sheet is needed. [L1236.025]
- To create a native Google Ads dashboard:
  1. Open the native Dashboards feature in Google Ads and create a new dashboard, which starts blank. [L1236.027]
  2. Add a widget, for example a chart of conversions by device. [L1236.027]
  3. Resize the widget as needed. [L1236.027]
  4. Add further widgets, such as conversion rate by device, geographic performance by state or a campaign view. [L1236.027]
  5. Click Save. [L1236.027]
  6. Return to the saved dashboard at any time to check metrics and bid adjustments without navigating through campaigns. [L1236.027]
- Google Shopping competitor check: open the Google Analytics Content report (left sidebar) to see the store pages that get the most traffic. Search those top product pages in Google Shopping to check what competitors charge. [L1090.061]
- Older advice (2021): Microsoft Advertising (Bing) can import Google Ads campaigns. Bing does not group multiple sellers of the same product in one listing, so the price comparison that Google shows does not happen there. [L1090.063]

### Google Analytics, GA4 and Tracking Foundations

- Google Analytics is a tracking tool that monitors which pages visitors view, which pages they exit from, and how long they stay on the website. [L0293.006]
- Google Analytics 4 (GA4) tracks website traffic, conversions, user behavior, and creates remarketing audiences. It replaced Universal Analytics. [L0274.032]
- Universal Analytics has been phased out in many accounts, so stores should use GA4. Migration from Universal Analytics to GA4 is done through the GA4 migration lesson in the analytics module, and analytics setups should be updated to GA4. [L1114.040] (2023)
- Shopify requires GA4 to be connected through the Google channel app. Google removes the old Universal Analytics connection on July 1. Do not rely on the old Universal Analytics entry for tracking. [L0723.014]
- Conversion Pixel App: a Shopify app that manages the conversion tracking pixel required to link customer purchases to Google Ads campaigns for attribution. [L0032.005]
- Google and YouTube app: a free Shopify app for connecting Google Analytics to the store. Its free plan is enough for this purpose, and it is now owned by Google LLC. [L0722.008]
- To link Google Analytics to a Shopify store:
  1. Enter the store URL in the Google Analytics setup. If Google recognizes a Shopify store, it offers Install with Shopify. Click Next to see the instructions. [L0722.002]
  2. In the Shopify admin, search the Shopify App Store for the Google and YouTube app and choose the free plan. [L0722.002]
  3. Click Install. [L0722.002]
  4. After installation, the app appears under Sales Channels in the Shopify admin. [L0722.002]
  5. In Google Analytics, copy the Tag ID of the data stream. [L0722.002]
  6. In the Google and YouTube app, follow the Google Analytics connection option one, which uses the guided Shopify video. Do not use option two, because it is more complex. [L0722.002]
  7. Paste the Tag ID where the setup asks for it. [L0722.002]
  8. Open the app's Settings and confirm that only one Google Analytics property is connected. [L0722.002]
- Connect neither Google Ads nor Google Merchant Center through the Google and YouTube app. That app is meant solely for linking Google Analytics. If either Google Ads or Merchant Center appears as connected under that app's Settings, remove the connection. [L0722.009]
- The person who links Google Analytics to a Shopify store through the Google and YouTube app needs administrator rights on that Google Analytics account. Without them, the guided connection cannot be completed. [L0722.013]
- The Google channel setup offers to turn on Google emails and to manage Merchant Center from there. Do not activate these during the Google Analytics connection. Set up Merchant Center in its own later step. [L0723.018]
- Use the feed app's integration for the Google tracking tag. If tracking fails after moving from Universal Analytics to GA4, contact that app's support through its help button with a screenshot. [L1094.058] (2023)
- Keep GA4's default user engagement event settings when integrated directly with Shopify, since changing them could affect Shopify analytics. Default user engagement events count visitors after about 10 seconds on the site. [L1094.059] (2023)
- Google Analytics real-time view shows how many people are on the site at a given moment. Open it during a large influencer post to see the traffic it generates. [L0790.023]
- Check traffic platform by platform in Google Analytics (for example Pinterest traffic under Traffic sources) to see whether traffic and sales come from that platform before judging its cost per click. [L0665.045] (2022)
- The Facebook Pixel should be installed on the store before running paid traffic, so that analytics data accumulates in advance. Business Manager at business.facebook.com is free to set up and is where the pixel and analytics are managed. [L1243.015]
- Meta Pixel Helper is a browser extension used to check whether the Meta pixel is installed and firing correctly on a store's pages. [L0984.001]
- Facebook Pixel Helper is a free Chrome extension that stays in the top-right area of the browser. It shows which Facebook Pixel events fire on any website you open, which lets you examine competitor tracking. [L1243.006]
- Before enabling Microsoft UET tracking through the feed app, check the Shopify store for any existing conversion tracking or UET scripts. If you are unsure about an existing script or tag, contact the app's support first. The support link is at the bottom of the setup page under "Get support". [L1036.015]

### Meta Ads: Creation, Bulk Building and Testing

- Facebook Power Editor is a tool for building and editing many ads in bulk. The lesson places it after the audience and message work, which should be finished first. [L0643.063]
- AdEspresso or Qwaya (also called Choir) generates split test variations of text, image and color, and can set up 240 or more variations in about two minutes. Use them for split testing, especially when the monthly ad spend is around $2,000 or more. [L0643.051]

### Ad Manage.ai: Media Buying Automation

- Ad Manage.ai automates media buying through batch ad launching, integrated Meta Ads Manager, AI-assisted creative generation, and cross-platform automation rules. It integrates with Dropbox, Google Drive, Frame.io, SharePoint, AIR, Focal, Shade, Iconik, Brandfolder, Canva, Figma, YouTube, and TikTok asset libraries. The platform saves 20-40 hours per month on media management tasks. [L0207.001]
- Pricing: a 40% discount on the first month is available through partnership. The Essential plan is recommended for starting. The In-House Plus plan offers advanced automation features including cross-platform scaling. [L0207.012]
- Launch procedure:
  1. Navigate to the Launcher section and select your ad account. [L0207.003]
  2. Click the blue launch button and select "Select media to use". [L0207.003]
  3. Choose a content source: Dropbox, Google Drive, Frame.io, YouTube, TikTok asset library, or other integration. [L0207.003]
  4. Paste a shareable link or browse folders, then select specific files or videos to import. [L0207.003]
  5. Either add creatives to an existing ad set (if 20 or fewer new ads) or duplicate an existing ad set and rename it. [L0207.003]
  6. Select launch status: Active (starts spending immediately) or Paused (delays launch). [L0207.003]
  7. Load a saved copy template or create new copy with primary text, headlines, description, call-to-action, and URL parameters including UTM tracking. [L0207.003]
  8. Review all settings and click "Launch Ads". [L0207.003]
  9. Wait approximately one minute for the launch to complete. [L0207.003]
- Ads launch in approximately one minute on average in Ad Manage.ai, compared to longer sequential steps and upload times in Meta's native Ads Manager. [L0207.005]
- Click 'Edit in Table Mode' to assign different settings to individual ads within the same batch launch. Card view applies identical settings to all ads. Table Mode lets you customize targeting, budget, or other fields per ad. [L0207.014]
- The Existing Ads tab in Media Library displays all ads currently in your connected Meta ad account, whether active, paused, or inactive. View their total spend, ad count, and launch status to prevent duplicate creatives and track performance. [L0207.015]
- When duplicating an ad set, a "Copy ads to new ad set" checkbox appears. Leave it unchecked if you only want to replicate the ad set structure and settings. Check it only if you also want to copy the original ads into the new ad set. [L0207.017]
- After launching ads in Ad Manage.ai, click "View in Meta" or "View Launch" to open that ad directly in Meta's native Ads Manager. [L0207.018]
- Ad Manage.ai includes a free Google Sheets plugin for bulk ad launches. Connect your spreadsheet to trigger automatic ad launches directly from Sheets without using the platform interface. [L0207.019]
- The Inspiration tab's Clone Ad feature operates on a 1,000-credit monthly cycle, resetting every 3 days. Each cloned ad consumes credits. Once depleted, the feature is unavailable until the next cycle resets. [L0207.020]
- Ad Manage.ai's API reports take 20-30 minutes to refresh with current data from Meta. While loading, the platform displays the most recent available data for Top Creatives, Fatigued Ads, Landing Pages, and custom reports. [L0207.013]

### TikTok Ads

- TikTok pixel setup: in TikTok Ads Manager, create the pixel under Tools > Events > Manage Web Events, then Create Pixel > Partner Setup > Next > Shopify. Set Data Sharing for the pixel to Maximum. [L1373.002]
- TikTok Pixel Helper is a Shopify app used to connect the TikTok pixel to a Shopify store. [L1376.025]
- A new TikTok Ads account is under review after setup and can take up to 24 hours to be activated before campaigns can run. The account is active once TikTok Marketing shows as Active. [L1373.005]
- TikTok Ads Manager is used to run Spark Ads through a generated authorization code or to upload traditional ads. A separate Ads Manager with its own email is recommended for paid work. [L1381.033]
- TikTok UTM procedure, to track ads after iOS tracking limits:
  1. Open a UTM builder for TikTok, for example the one offered by PP Ads. [L1377.033]
  2. Paste the product page URL into the builder. [L1377.033]
  3. Copy the generated URL. [L1377.033]
  4. Paste the generated URL into the website URL field of the TikTok ad. The UTM parameters pass campaign, ad group and ad names to Shopify so you can track ads after iOS tracking limits. [L1377.033]

### Pinterest Ads: Account, Profile and Verification

- Pinterest traffic: Pinterest performs better in some niches than others. It is worth connecting when the store already gets Pinterest traffic or it fits the marketing plan. Connecting it through Shopify is simple and carries little cost, so there is rarely a reason not to try. [L0666.056] (2024)
- Older advice (2021): Pinterest can be connected to a store, and small-budget Pinterest ads can be tested. [L0671.097]
- Pinterest business account setup, option A (convert an existing personal account via settings, or create one directly at ads.pinterest.com):
  1. Either convert a normal Pinterest account to a business account via Settings, or go directly to ads.pinterest.com. [L1133.003]
  2. Fill in the basic business details. [L1133.003]
  3. Wait until the account shows as ready. [L1133.003]
- Pinterest business account setup, option B (full checklist):
  1. Either convert your existing personal account to a business account in the account settings, or create a new account at ads.pinterest.com. [L1135.017]
  2. Enter the profile name as your store name. For a general store, use the general store name. [L1135.017]
  3. Enter your website link. [L1135.017]
  4. Select a country: your home country or the US, since Pinterest allows targeting of any country. [L1135.017]
  5. Select the brand focus. If unsure, choose Other or Home. [L1135.017]
  6. Select up to three options from the focus list, such as sell more products. [L1135.017]
  7. Describe the business as online retail or marketplace and select Shopify as the platform. [L1135.017]
  8. Answer yes to being interested in advertising. [L1135.017]
  9. Enter your phone number if you want to use Pinterest support, or skip it. [L1135.017]
- Account setup details: fill in store name, website, country (your own country is fine, there is no need to choose United States only), brand focus (top three options, e.g. "sell more products"), business type "Online retail or marketplace" with Shopify selected, and answer yes to being interested in advertising. [L1134.003]
- Check the business account: the profile should show the label "Business", and the menu should show an "Ads" section. Profile > Settings is where these details are verified. [L1135.018]
- The business profile checklist: a business account shows "Business" on the profile and an "Ads" section. [L1134.004]
- Profile preparation procedure:
  1. Change the username to match the brand or product. [L1133.004]
  2. Change the profile image. [L1133.004]
  3. Add a short About Us section. [L1133.004]
  4. Add a cover image. [L1133.004]
  5. Claim the website by adding a TXT record to the domain DNS or by adding the HTML tag. [L1133.004]
- Profile detail: set the username to the store name (or product name), upload the store or brand logo as profile image, write a short "about" section of about five to six lines (this can be generated with ChatGPT), and add a cover image such as a store banner. [L1134.004]
- Entering a phone number is optional and is not used for verification. Add it only if you want access to Pinterest support for banned accounts or ad insights. Do not rely on their guidance, because their agents are not focused on direct response marketing. [L1135.019]
- Website verification (HTML tag option):
  1. In Pinterest settings, go to Claimed account, then Claim website, and choose the HTML tag option. [L1134.005]
  2. Copy the meta tag. [L1134.005]
  3. Paste it into the Shopify theme file theme.liquid just below the canonical URL line, then save. [L1134.005]
  4. Return to Pinterest, click Continue, enter the store name and click Verify. [L1134.005]

### Pinterest Ads: Shopify Connection, Payment and Conversions

- Install the Pinterest app in Shopify:
  1. In Shopify, go to Apps, then App settings, then the Shopify App Store. [L1135.022]
  2. Search for Pinterest and install the Pinterest app. [L1135.022]
  3. Connect your Pinterest account through the sales channel. [L1135.022]
  4. Go to the Marketing section and confirm that the Pinterest tag is installed. This is the key step for tracking. [L1135.022]
  5. Continue the setup in the app and proceed to billing. [L1135.022]
- Pinterest setup in Shopify, in order:
  1. Install the Pinterest app from the Shopify App Store and connect the Pinterest account. [L1134.002]
  2. Add the Sales channel. [L1134.002]
  3. Go to the Marketing section and confirm the Pinterest tag is installed. This is the key step for tracking. [L1134.002]
  4. Click continuous setup. [L1134.002]
  5. Go to ads, then billing, and enter business details. [L1134.002]
  6. Add a payment method: a credit or debit card or a virtual card. PayPal is not accepted. [L1134.002]
- Pinterest does not allow listing a product without merchant approval. Merchant approval is a non-essential extra. Rejections often come from weak product descriptions, so a rejected shopping merchant account should not stop the ad setup. [L1134.001]
- Shopping merchant approval can stay pending or be rejected, often because of product description issues. This blocks product listings but is not required to run Pinterest ads, so it is not a reason to stop. [L1135.023]
- Enabling conversion campaigns when the error "increase your conversion events" appears:
  1. Open a product page of the store. [L1134.006]
  2. Open the browser developer tools (right-click > Inspect, or More tools > Developer tools if inspect is disabled). [L1134.006]
  3. Go to the Console. [L1134.006]
  4. Paste the provided conversion-trigger code (the code is not reproduced in this unit) and run it 10 to 20 times. [L1134.006]
  5. Wait 5 to 10 minutes until the conversion event is active and the conversion campaign option appears. [L1134.006]

### Microsoft Ads (Bing)

- Import from Google Ads: Microsoft Ads can import Google Ads campaigns. Make changes in Google Ads first, where most data and sales are, and sync to Microsoft once a batch of changes is approved. Optionally sync only new updates. [L0968.049] (2021)
- Import procedure (2025):
  1. Import your Google Ads campaigns into Microsoft Ads. [L0837.022]
  2. Immediately lower the budgets of the imported campaigns, since imports carry over the Google budgets. For example, a $20 per day Google budget should be reduced to $5 per day in Microsoft. [L0837.022]
  3. Scale the Microsoft budgets gradually over time. [L0837.022]

### Retargeting: ROI Hunter

- ROI Hunter is a Shopify app that sets up dynamic retargeting without coding. Search for the exact name "ROI Hunter" in the Shopify App Store, because a generic search for Shopify dynamic retargeting returns other apps. [L0558.003]
- ROI Hunter is a free Shopify app, so dynamic retargeting on Google Display Network can be set up without paying for a separate developer or code work. Installing it and syncing it with the store is all that is needed to create the audiences and campaigns. [L0558.012]
- Do not manage ROI Hunter campaigns from inside Shopify. Make all campaign and budget changes directly in Google Ads, since the app's Shopify side is only for setup and sync. [L0558.005]

### Break-Even and Pre-Launch Checks

- Dropship Toolkit is a website with a break-even ROAS calculator for dropshipping stores. Enter the selling price, the cost of goods and fulfillment to get the break-even ROAS before setting scaling thresholds. Use it before running ads to check whether a product can be profitable at a given price. [L0642.050, L0661.046]

### Attribution and Profit Tracking

Conflict note: sources disagree on when to adopt Triple Whale. One source says to use it for early-stage testing when accuracy matters for budget decisions. Two others say to consider it only once the store is consistently profitable and to compare it with a cheaper option first. Apply the first when verifying metrics before scaling. Apply the second when deciding whether to pay for a tool.

| Tool | Cost | Accuracy (as stated) | Use when | Source ids |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triple Whale | $300/month | 80% for sales, ROAS and ad performance | Early-stage testing and verifying metrics before scaling; or, once the store is consistently profitable | L0309.032, L0309.034, L0694.008, L0694.044 |
| Northbeam | $900/month | 100% | Only if the business generates enough profit margin to justify the cost | L0309.034 |
| Cometly | Not stated | Not reliable | Do not use | L0309.033 |
| Converge | Possibly lower than Triple Whale | Not stated | Compare with Triple Whale before paying | L0694.044 |
| Free UTM tracking app | Free option | Not stated; UTM data is not perfect either | Cross-check Facebook data against actual sales | L0694.008 |

- Triple Whale is attribution software used to capture precise ad performance metrics when analyzing and iterating creatives. [L0258.024]
- Triple Whale is a tracking and analytics platform costing $300/month. It is 80% accurate in measuring sales, ROAS, and ad performance. Use it to verify your metrics before scaling ads, as inaccurate tracking wastes budget. [L0309.032]
- Triple Whale is used to track sales and ROAS for winning products. It replaces manual tracking of other ad metrics once a product is clearly profitable. [L1173.016]
- Cometly is a tracking platform that does not work reliably and wastes money. Do not use it. Choose Triple Whale or Northbeam instead. [L0309.033]
- Northbeam is a tracking platform costing $900/month and providing 100% accuracy. Use it only if your business generates sufficient profit margin to justify the cost. Otherwise, use Triple Whale ($300/month, 80% accuracy) for early-stage testing. [L0309.034]
- Triple Whale is a paid attribution tool that reconciles Facebook-reported sales with real orders. Attribution alternatives such as Converge exist at possibly lower prices. Because the tool costs money, consider it only once the store is consistently profitable, and compare it with a cheaper option first. [L0694.044]
- A UTM tracking app with a free option can be used to cross-check Facebook data against actual sales, because UTM data is not perfect either but is a useful second source. A paid attribution tool such as Triple Whale is worth considering once the store is consistently profitable, and cheaper alternatives exist. [L0694.008]
- True Profit shows profit per campaign and can be used on its highest plan, reported at about $150 per month. It helps make more calculated scaling decisions but is not essential at the beginning. [L1476.044]
- Conversios GA4 app connects Google Analytics 4 and supports Google SEO tracking. It is needed when scaling, not at the foundation stage. Microsoft Clarity is an additional analytics tool shown in Customer events. [L1327.041]
- Unique coupons, dedicated phone numbers or separate addresses for each ad allow every response to be traced to its source. Use them to compare headlines, publications and offers on a cost-per-customer basis. [L1261.048]
- Telephone response lines are more convenient for many people than mailing a coupon, and they can be measured as a separate response channel. Include a phone option in offers and track it separately. [L1261.051]

### UTM Tracking for Partners and Banners

- Add a referral or banner partner link with UTM tracking:
  1. Choose the destination URL. [L0971.024]
  2. Open a UTM builder. [L0971.024]
  3. Set utm_source to banner ad, utm_medium to banner, and utm_campaign to the partner URL. [L0971.024]
  4. Leave campaign ID empty. [L0971.024]
  5. Copy the generated link and send it to the partner. [L0971.024]
  6. Check the traffic, country and sales of that source in Google Analytics. [L0971.024]

### Reporting and AI Reporting Limits

- Supermetrics is a Google Sheets add-on that connects to ad accounts such as Google Ads, Bing Ads and Facebook Ads and pulls their data into a sheet. Because the data lands in a regular spreadsheet, Google Sheets formulas and conditional formatting can be applied to it directly. [L1236.024]
- Manus is an AI agent that can connect to a Meta ad account through Meta marketing tools, read the ad-level names and build a report. It processes the exported data with Python code to produce the final file. [L0885.014]
- Manus is an AI agent that can connect to a Meta ad account and load a Meta Ads analysis skill before pulling data. At the time of recording, it had just been acquired by Meta (Facebook), so its roadmap and features may change. [L0885.025]
- Setting up an AI creative-count report in Manus:
  1. Go to Manus and integrate (connect) the Meta ad account. [L0885.015]
  2. Send a prompt that names the ad account, describes the report and attaches an example report. [L0885.015]
  3. State what to exclude (iterations and new concepts) and which field to group by (product type from the ad-level naming convention). [L0885.015]
  4. Review the delivered report, which can be produced as a spreadsheet. [L0885.015]
- Prompt for the creative-count report (use as written, replacing the bracketed placeholders):

```
I need you to look at the ad account [AD ACCOUNT LINK]. I need a report like the attached example that tracks the total number of creatives launched per month per product. Now note that we do not track iterations or new concepts. What I need is the report by product type, which is part of our naming convention at the ad level (for example: Mixed, Oodie, Weighted Blanket, [OTHER PRODUCT TYPES]). Count each ad once in the month it launched, show the monthly totals per product type, and deliver the final report as a spreadsheet.
```
[L0885.016]

- When asking an AI agent for an ad account report, you can attach a screenshot or recording of an example report, or describe the output in exact words. In the words, state what must be counted, what must be excluded (for example iterations or new concepts), and which naming field to group by. [L0885.026]
- Conflict note on AI reporting: Manus can connect to and build reports from a Meta account [L0885.014, L0885.015], but current AI reporting tools such as Manus are not yet reliable for interpreting ad account results. Use the AI for data gathering and file production. Use human analysis from mentors, group calls or one-on-one coaches to interpret the data. [L0885.017]
- In Excel, the status bar shows Average, Count and Sum for a selected range of cells. Example: selecting 5 values that sum to 273 gives an average of 54.6. [L0885.018]

### Automation Limits

- Conflict note on automation: Ad Manage.ai offers cross-platform automation rules [L0207.001]. Older advice (2020) states that automating ads with tools like Zapier is not recommended. Facebook's built-in automated rules are occasionally useful but rarely worth the effort. [L1088.069]
- Preflect AI is an ad automation tool that runs campaigns across Facebook, Google and TikTok for about $500 per month, with no minimum ad spend. It suits owners who want a hands-off setup, but someone still has to read the data and decide the next test. [L1184.065]

### Click Fraud Protection

- Older advice (2019): a separate click fraud app is not necessary. Monitor all ad campaigns and exclude Google Display Network sites that bring in little value or show poor results. [L1068.050]
- Older advice (2022): a click-fraud protection app was tested for one month by one operator and did not save any ad money, so it was not recommended. Compare any savings against the monthly fee within the first month before keeping such a tool. [L1092.108]

## Conversion Testing, Site Speed and Monitoring

### Tool Map and Testing Stack

| Job | Tool | Units |
|---|---|---|
| A/B testing on Shopify, no code | Visually | L0197.001, L0389.017, L0389.025 |
| Controlled A/B testing after site changes | Lively | L1225.086 |
| Price, image and description tests on one product | Lomio | L0524.042 |
| Session recording and heatmaps | Lucky Orange, Hotjar, Smartlook, FullStory, Microsoft Clarity | L0293.007, L0390.020, L0837.029 |
| User testing with real people | User testing services, Usertesting.com | L0390.019, L0423.018 |
| Speed testing | GTmetrix, Google PageSpeed Insights, Pingdom | L0851.002, L0390.057, L0575.023, L1522.037 |
| Image optimization and SEO app | Smart SEO | L1190.005 |
| Broken-link checks | Check My Links (Page Modified) | L0332.003, L0332.025 |
| Uptime monitoring | Pingdom | L0575.023 |
| Test tracking | Trello, spreadsheet, Google Sheet, Asana | L0399.002 |

### Conversion Testing: Prerequisites

- Testing tools named in the sources include Optimizely, VWO, Convert and Google Optimize. [L0423.011]
- Tools for finding conversion problems include Google Analytics with Shopify Analytics, Usertesting.com for user tests, and session recording tools such as Hotjar, Smartlook and FullStory. [L0423.018]
- VWO and Google Optimize-style platforms are for running controlled tests. They are only worth the investment once traffic and basic best-practice fixes are in place. [L0886.023]
- Track conversion rate tests and planned future tests in a Trello board or a spreadsheet, Google Sheet, Asana or similar project management tool. A downloadable Trello template can be used as a starting point. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0399.002]

### Conversion Testing: Tools

**Visually (Shopify A/B testing)**

- Visually is a Shopify A/B testing platform available risk-free through Source E: no charges until reaching 1.5x to 3x return on investment, which creates zero upfront app cost. [L0197.001]
- Its recommendation engine uses Shopify purchase data, customer order history and on-site browsing behavior. [L0197.001]
- Most tests are set up in 3-4 clicks, with toggles for magnifying glass, reviews and design elements. Custom options are available through the Visually team. [L0197.001]
- Visually runs no-code A/B tests and on-site experiences: promotion bars, social proof, product recommendations, product upsells, custom HTML/CSS/JS components, redirects, Fake Click, and global CSS or JavaScript files. [L0389.017]
- Results are sent to Google Analytics when the event box is ticked. [L0389.017]
- The Redirect feature sends visitors to a different URL. This lets you compare two pages, such as two landing pages, and optimize conversions without code. It sits alongside Fake Click, Global CSS and Global JavaScript in the element list. [L0389.025]

**Lively**

- Lively is an app for A/B split testing on a Shopify store. After major website changes, run the new version against the old one in a controlled test, so each version gets enough visitors to show a clear result. [L1225.086]

**Lomio**

- Lomio is used for price and image testing, and for description testing, rather than full page tests. It is a quick way to shift prices and visuals on one product. [L0524.042]

**Neat**

- The Neat A/B testing tool is an option for Shopify stores to run conversion tests. It can be used alongside another testing tool to compare results. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0399.004]
- Neat AB Testing is a split-testing app from the Shopify App Store. It pulls conversion data from the Shopify checkout and links it to each page version, so results are collected inside the app. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1364.033]

**Google Optimize (discontinued)**

- Google discontinued Google Optimize in September 2023. It can no longer be used, and current alternatives must be checked before setup. [L0399.003, L0423.011, L1259.068, L1364.032]
- Older sources (2021-22) describe Google Optimize as a free split-testing tool linked to Google Analytics for on-page tests. That method no longer works as described, so a current alternative is needed. (Older advice.) [L0399.003, L1364.032]
- For landing page split tests in 2026, use a current A/B testing app that works with Shopify instead. [L1259.068]

### Session Recording: Lucky Orange

- Lucky Orange records your visitors' mouse movements and clicks on store pages in real time. It creates heatmaps showing where visitors interact. [L0293.007]
- You can watch individual visitor sessions to see scrolling patterns, cursor movements and interaction points. [L0293.007]
- Lucky Orange records visitor sessions and shows country and location. It can be used to measure average time on site. [L0363.029]
- Lucky Orange offers a 7-day free trial. If you continue after the trial ends, the subscription costs $10 per month. [L0293.008]
- Use Lucky Orange to show why people bounce. A free trial or free account is enough to start. (Older advice, 2022.) [L0665.047]
- Use it to spot frustrating elements, such as things visitors try to click that are not clickable, which increase bounce rate. (Advice from 2023.) [L0804.048]
- It shows where visitors click, where they stop and whether something on the site looks broken. (Older advice, 2018.) [L1085.044]
- A free trial is available for testing. (Older advice, 2019.) [L0663.064]
- Lucky Orange shows how visitors behave on a store, so you can make optimizations based on what they actually do. [L0837.029]
- Microsoft Clarity is a free alternative that offers similar behavior tracking. [L0837.029]
- It shows how long a visitor has looked at a product before leaving. You can use that information to personalize abandoned cart messages. [L1472.055]

**Bot traffic and IP addresses (Lucky Orange)**

- Lucky Orange shows visitor IP addresses. This lets you block bot traffic that inflates visitor counts. [L0941.043]
- A seven-day free trial is enough to collect this data. You can cancel afterwards. [L0941.043]
- Repeated visits from one IP address with no purchases are most likely a bot. [L1114.037]
- To check, search the IP address on Google. Others often identify it as a Google, Microsoft or Meta bot. [L1114.037]

### Session Recording: Other Tools

- Hotjar offers heatmaps, scroll maps, user recordings and exit-intent feedback polls. [L0390.020]
- Smartlook is a similar session recording tool. [L0390.020]
- FullStory is the most expensive option of this group. [L0390.020]

**Daily recording review routine (Hotjar, Smartlook or similar)**

1. Install the tool on the store. [L0390.021]
2. Once a day, spend about 10 to 15 minutes watching about 5 recordings. [L0390.021]
3. Prioritize visitors who added products to cart but did not complete the purchase. [L0390.021]
4. Look for buttons that are hard to find, checkout buttons hidden by scrolling, elements visitors click that are not clickable, and rage clicking. [L0390.021]
5. Record each flaw and fix the most frequent ones first. [L0390.021]

### User Testing

- User testing services let you buy recorded sessions with real users matching a chosen audience, such as age range or interests. [L0390.019]
- Assign them a task, such as buying a specific product. You receive recordings with spoken thoughts and notes on why tasks were easy or hard. [L0390.019]
- Usertesting.com is named as the tool for user tests when looking for conversion problems. [L0423.018]

### Speed Measurement: Which Numbers to Trust

- Shopify's built-in speed test is not a reliable measure. Use Google PageSpeed Insights and GTmetrix for speed checks. (Older advice, 2022.) [L0665.051]
- A Shopify speed score is less useful than the GTmetrix breakdown for finding the cause. (Advice from 2023.) [L1283.080]
- GTmetrics and Shopify can show different load times because the tests run from different locations. [L0807.037]
- The slowest item in the GTmetrics waterfall is usually the same slow element that shows up in Shopify. [L0807.037]

### Speed Measurement: GTmetrix

- GTmetrix is a free speed test that reports page load performance and a waterfall of every page element with its load time. Use it to find the element slowing a product page. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0801.110, L0851.002]
- GTmetrix reports a performance score and a waterfall chart. The waterfall shows how each element of the page loads, from fastest to slowest. [L1446.018]
- It shows the scores, the elements slowing the page, and the code loaded on the site. (Advice from 2023.) [L0970.089]
- A free account with no credit card is enough for these tests. [L0857.060]
- The free GT Metrics account needs no credit card and allows about 3 speed tests per day. Enter the product or store URL and run the test to get a starting score. [L1190.006]
- The free version also shows which installed apps or tracking scripts slow the page. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0551.029]

**Benchmarks**

| Metric | Clean store (no code changes, no heavy apps) | Lower results mean |
|---|---|---|
| Performance | About 99% | Room to optimize theme, apps or images |
| Structure | About 95% | Room to optimize theme, apps or images |
| LCP (largest contentful paint) | Under 1 second | Room to optimize theme, apps or images |
| Grade | A |, |

[L1190.007]

**Reading the waterfall**

- The GT Metrics Waterfall view lists every loaded resource from fastest to slowest. [L1190.008]
- Scroll down to the lines that stretch furthest to the right on the timeline. These are the slowest items. [L1190.008, L0272.015]
- Optimize or remove the apps or resources that cause them. [L1190.008]
- Check the waterfall to find slow images or app code, then remove or reduce them. (Older advice, 2021.) [L0962.046]

**Speed check procedure (GTmetrix)**

1. Run the store URL through GTmetrics. [L1283.081]
2. Open the waterfall view. [L1283.081]
3. Find the item loading slowest, such as a large image or an app script. [L1283.081]
4. Fix it or send the code to the developer for identification. [L1283.081]

**Finding a slow app (GTmetrix)**

1. Create a free GTmetrics account. [L0807.036]
2. Enter your store URL and run the test. [L0807.036]
3. Open the waterfall view. [L0807.036]
4. Scroll to the bottom of the waterfall to see the slowest-loading items. [L0807.036]
5. Note any app or developer names you do not need. [L0807.036]
6. Remove the unneeded app from the store. [L0807.036]
7. Retest the store speed. [L0807.036]
8. Contact the app developer to ask them to remove any leftover code. [L0807.036]

### Speed Measurement: PageSpeed Insights

- Google PageSpeed Insights often returns generic speed recommendations that are impractical for a Shopify store. [L0390.057]
- Filter its results against your actual app list and image sizes. Start with the heaviest elements, such as apps and unoptimized images. [L0390.057]
- The "Remove unused JavaScript" entry lists each script with its host domain, such as a chat widget, pop-up, analytics or finance vendor. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1266.036]
- Use those domains to match each slow script to the installed app that injects it before deciding to remove or replace it. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1266.036]
- Use Google PageSpeed Insights, GTmetrix and Pingdom to test page speed regularly. They list suggested fixes, though many of those fixes are technical. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1522.037]

**WebPageTest waterfall**

- In a WebPageTest waterfall, one slow third-party request, such as a chat widget that stalls on connection, can hold back every request that follows it. This can push the Largest Contentful Paint out by tens of seconds. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1266.046]
- Locate the stalled item by its position in the waterfall. Removing that one script can change the result more than any other fix. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1266.046]

### Apps, Scripts and What You Control

- Installed apps add code that slows a store. The impact depends on how each app is built, not only on how many are installed. [L1190.003]
- Remove apps that are not needed so their code is removed as well. [L1190.003]
- Shopify's hosting, CDN and base platform scripts cannot be changed. Focus optimization effort on images, apps, custom code, CSS, HTML and JavaScript that the store controls. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1266.020]
- The standard Shopify plan at about 29 dollars per month serves assets from cdn.shopify.com. The store does not need a separate CDN for normal speed. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0551.029]
- Check hosting-level items in PageSpeed Insights, including Google Tag Manager and tracking tools. Hotjar, Privy, Zotabox, Klaviyo, Facebook and Google Analytics scripts add JavaScript cost. Check whether each is still needed, and move tags to Google Tag Manager where possible. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1266.027]
- Financing widgets such as Bread load their scripts on every page, including the homepage. Shoppers only need them on product or checkout pages. For high-ticket stores, check whether the financing app's scripts can be limited to those pages. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1266.037]

### Image Optimization and Alt Text

- Add image alt text either manually or with a free Shopify app from the Shopify App Store. Compress product images as well, since large images slow the store down. (Older advice, 2020.) [L0468.087]
- Minifier adds metatags (alt text) to product photos automatically. It resets once per month. (Older advice, 2022.) [L0272.057]
- If only some photos get tagged when you expect all to be tagged, contact Minifier's support for a reset, then run it again. (Older advice, 2022.) [L0272.057]
- Crush Picks is an alternative app if Minifier issues persist. (Older advice, 2022.) [L0272.057]

**Smart SEO: optimize images by collection**

1. Open the Collections tab. [L0368.002]
2. Select all collections. [L0368.002]
3. Deselect those without images. Only collections that actually have images need this step. [L0368.002]
4. Click Optimize collection images. [L0368.002]

Path: Smart SEO app > Configure > Image optimization. [L0368.002]

**Smart SEO: optimize product images**

1. Open the Shopify admin and open the Smart SEO app. [L1190.009]
2. Find the Image optimization section and click Configure. [L1190.009]
3. Open the Products tab. The app pulls in all store products and shows whether each image is optimized. [L1190.009]
4. Tick the products to optimize, for example all of them. [L1190.009]
5. Click Optimize product images and wait for the job to finish. [L1190.009]
6. Check the savings shown afterwards for the combined image size before and after. [L1190.009]

**Expected results**

- In one test with 10 products, the combined image size dropped from 60.2 MB to 18.1 MB, which saved about 42 MB. [L1190.010]
- A store with hundreds or thousands of products may need around 30 minutes or longer. The job can run while you work on other tasks. [L1190.010]

**Smart SEO: add alt text to product images**

1. Open Smart SEO, go to the Alt tags section, and click Configure. [L1190.013]
2. Select all products with the checkbox at the top and click Edit products. [L1190.013]
3. Set the alt text to the product title as a dynamic value pulled from the store, then click Save. [L1190.013]
4. Open each product image and rewrite its alt text to describe what the image shows, such as the view angle (for example, front view, front view with model, back view with model). [L1190.013]
5. Save each change. [L1190.013]

### Smart SEO: Features, Plans and Settings

- Smart SEO covers image compression, Instant Page preloading, alt text editing, AI-generated meta titles and descriptions, product SEO scores (0 to 100), and structured data (JSON-LD). [L1190.005]
- The JSON-LD feature requires a paid plan. [L1190.005]

**AI meta tag limits by plan**

| Plan | AI meta descriptions per month |
|---|---|
| $10 per month | 100 |
| $20 per month | 200 |
| $30 per month | 300 |

[L0965.057]

- Meta tags generated by an AI app stay on the product after you cancel the subscription. You can subscribe for a month or two to generate them, then cancel. [L0965.058]
- Structured data (JSON-LD) setting covers product pages. Before saving, upload the logo, add social media URLs, set the price range, enable the phone number, and connect Judge.me reviews to the store. [L1190.030]
- The 0 to 100 optimization score for each product updates after you edit its title or meta description and refresh the product list. Use the low scores to choose which products to improve first. [L1190.050]
- Smart SEO > Page speed > Instant page is disabled until it is set up. Click Enable to preload each page just before a user opens it. This makes page loads feel almost instant and improves user experience. Google's Core Web Vitals test is expected to reflect this over time. [L1190.011]

### Broken Links: Check My Links

- Check My Links is a free Chrome extension offered by pagemodified.com. It checks every link on the page you are viewing and marks broken ones. [L0332.003]
- It finds 404 links on other sites without clicking each link manually, which is too slow on pages with dozens of outgoing links. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0332.003]
- Use Check My Links for broken link building. Searching the Chrome Web Store for "check my links" returns similar extensions, such as Link Redirect Trace from linkresearchtools.com and Redirect Link Checker. Use the extension offered by pagemodified.com. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0332.026]
- Page Modified also offers a crawl of an entire website instead of only the open page. Use the full-site crawl when you need to audit every page of a site for broken links. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0332.025]

**Installation**

1. Install Check My Links from the Chrome Web Store. [L0332.004]
2. Chrome asks to add it with the permission to read and change all data on the websites you visit. Accept this to add the extension. [L0332.004]
3. The default options can be left unchanged. [L0332.004]

**Options**

| Option | Setting | Units |
|---|---|---|
| Method of Request | Keep GET as the default. HEAD is faster but can misreport the true HTTP status of a page. Switch to GET if results look odd. GET is also required to parse hashtag targets by element ID. | L0332.005 |
| Autocheck | When enabled, the extension checks links automatically whenever the URL of the current tab changes and the page has loaded. If you enable it, also enable Valid Link Caching. | L0332.006 |
| Warnings | Warnings appear in the browser console (F12, then Console tab) and are highlighted yellow. Optional checks: Parse DOM after hashtag (flags a hashtag link whose target element does not exist), Trailing # (flags links ending in #), Empty Link (flags empty href), and Anchor tag without href. | L0332.007 |
| Valid Link Caching | Enable it so the extension stops re-checking good links that return HTTP 200. It only checks new links or links that were previously broken. This greatly reduces the time needed to recheck a page. The Clear Cache button resets it. | L0332.008 |
| Nofollow Links | By default the extension skips links with the nofollow attribute, so only dofollow links are checked. Leave the option Check Links with rel=nofollow unchecked, because dofollow links are the ones worth pursuing for backlinks. | L0332.009 |
| Exclude links from these domains | Enter one domain or URL part per line to skip it. The default list already excludes ad network domains such as googleadservices and doubleclick, and appliedsemantics.com, because these are not sites you would contact about a link. | L0332.010 |

- On the options page, change the settings you need, then click the "Save my preferences" button at the bottom so the changes are stored. [L0332.027]

**Results**

- After running Check My Links on a page, the results panel shows the following counts. [L0332.011]

| Result | Meaning | Units |
|---|---|---|
| Links | Total on the page | L0332.011 |
| Valid links | HTTP 200 | L0332.011 |
| Valid redirecting links | Redirects | L0332.011 |
| Warnings | Optional warning checks | L0332.011 |
| Invalid links | Act on these; highlighted red on the page | L0332.011 |

- The counter shows how many links are still in the queue. [L0332.011]

### Uptime Monitoring: Pingdom

- Pingdom monitors the website every minute and sends SMS or email alerts on downtime. [L0575.023]
- The base plan costs about $10 per month or $100 per year and covers up to five sites. [L0575.023]
- A common alert threshold is 10 or 20 minutes of downtime, to avoid noise from brief hiccups. [L0575.023]

## Orders, Automation, Fraud and Operations

### Automation principles and sequencing

- Automate only after you have processed orders by hand for a few days to a few weeks and understand each step. Automating steps you do not yet understand produces the wrong logic. [L0934.056, L0934.171]
- Use Zapier for simple automated flows. Hand tasks too complex to automate, such as detailed product uploads, to virtual assistants. Use human assistants for multi-step tasks. [L1441.019]
- Automation can recover abandoned sales through follow-up and retargeting, monitor order status and trigger customer reviews, distribute content to social channels, instantly complete repetitive tasks, maintain customer records, transcribe media files, and generate new content. [L0236.008]
- In practice, automation can recover abandoned carts, track all order statuses, automatically request and collect product reviews, post new content to social media without manual work, and execute any repetitive business task in seconds without additional instruction. [L0285.005]
- Zapier is the central automation engine. It connects Shopify, Facebook, LinkedIn, Google Sheets, Trello and Slack to manage orders, communications, content distribution and customer tracking without manual intervention. [L0236.007]
- Zapier connects apps and automates steps. One example used it to send new orders to Trello and to send automatic emails based on quiz results on the website. It looks intimidating at first but can be learned. [L0337.034]
- Shopify is the core platform of a dropshipping store and the base all automations connect to. Its data (orders, customers, products) is the starting point for most automation work. [L0437.001]

### Platforms, costs and choice of tool

**Shopify Flow**
- Shopify Flow is a free app that simplifies building and editing automations compared with the Messaging app interface. Install it via Marketing > Automations > Install. [L0282.005]

**Zapier basics**
- Zapier (zapier.com) connects applications with automated workflows called "Zaps". [L0283.003]
- Each automation is a Zap made of one trigger and one or more actions or steps. [L0737.005]
- Zapier supports hundreds of pre-built integrations. Connect each platform once (for example, enter the Shopify subdomain); the connection stays active for all future Zaps that use that platform. [L0286.032]
- Authentication with each platform is required once, after which the connection is maintained permanently. [L0286.005]
- Zapier's screens and terms change over time. Check each step against the current Zapier and Shopify interface before following older setup instructions. *Older (2022).* [L1281.127]
- Zapier's Premium plan costs $20 per month. [L0286.005]
- The free plan does not include the Shopify integration. The plan that includes it costs $20 per month and is the only paid tool in this basic automation stack. [L0437.007, L0437.008]
- The 2023 comparison cites Zapier at $50 per month. Use $50 as the 2023 price point for that comparison, and the $20 Premium figure for the plan with the Shopify integration. Check the current price before budgeting, because the sources differ. [L0251.061, L0286.005, L0437.008]
- The 2023 lesson's comparison table lists IntegralMat at $19.99 per month and IFTTT as alternatives. Both provide similar capabilities for connecting applications, automating email, data transfers and notifications. Compare pricing and the specific integrations you need before choosing. [L0251.061]
- The Source E comparison describes Zapier per-automation cost as "a couple dollars per automation per month". [L0287.003]

**Zapier Gmail triggers and Slack actions**
- Zapier offers four Gmail-based triggers:
  - **New Email & Polling:** activates when messages arrive in your specified mailbox.
  - **New Email Matching Search Polling:** restricts triggering to emails that meet search criteria you define, such as a specific sender domain.
  - **New Label & Polling:** fires when you apply a label to an email.
  - **New Labeled Conversation & Polling:** triggers once when a label is first added to an email thread. It does not re-trigger if more messages appear in that thread. [L0287.007]
- When you test a Gmail trigger, Zapier automatically loads your previous emails from that mailbox as sample data. You do not need to trigger events manually; Zapier retrieves recent historical emails so you can check the logic before publishing. [L0287.017]
- Slack actions available in Zapier workflows include:
  - **Retrieve Thread Message:** extracts all messages from a specific conversation.
  - **Send Channel Message:** posts to public channels, with optional delivery scheduling.
  - **Send Direct Message:** routes private messages to a user or yourself through Slackbot, and supports scheduled sending.
  - **Send Private Channel Message:** targets restricted channels.
  - The integration also supports message deletion, channel creation, reminder scheduling and channel lookup. [L0287.008]
- Zapier's Slack integration sends real-time private messages containing lead details when forms are submitted, giving immediate visibility on mobile or desktop. [L0284.007]
- Zapier's **Attachments setting:** when connecting to services that send file attachments, you choose whether attachments auto-open for processing or remain clickable links for manual team review. [L0286.039]

**AI steps in Zapier**
- In Zapier's AI by Zapier step, models marked with a Lightning icon (such as GPT-4-1 mini and GPT-4-1 nano) are free to use. You do not need a paid OpenAI API account or your own ChatGPT credentials for them. [L0287.005]

**Why an API matters**
- An API is the connection tool that lets one application command another. Zapier uses APIs to connect Gmail, ChatGPT and Slack. Each integration works because the API gives the automation platform permission to trigger actions in that app, such as creating a task in ClickUp, sending a message in ChatGPT or posting a Slack message. [L0287.002]

**Platform comparison**

| Platform | Best for | Cost | Learning curve | Native integrations | Practical for simple tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Zapier | Beginners | Most expensive (couple dollars per automation per month) | Easiest | Most app connections | Yes, very intuitive |
| Make.com | Experienced users | Substantially cheaper than Zapier | Still accessible but slower to build | Fewer connections | No, more annoying setup for basic tasks like text splitting or time conversion |
| N8N | Developers only | Free or very cheap if self-hosted | Very steep (needs API, webhook, JSON, MCP knowledge) | Flexible | No, designed for low-code development, not business users |

[L0287.003]

- If you are brand new to automation, start with Zapier because it is the most intuitive. After gaining experience with several automations, move to Make.com to reduce costs. Switch to N8N only when you have many dozens of automations running at once and want to self-host to eliminate platform fees. [L0287.004]
- Plan to switch from Zapier to Make.com when you have dozens of automations running at once. At that scale, Zapier's per-automation monthly costs add up significantly, while Make.com's pricing becomes more economical. Move to N8N only at very large numbers of automations or if you focus on AI agents. [L0287.015]
- Make.com handles basic data transformations more awkwardly than Zapier. Splitting or reformatting text, converting code between formats, and transforming timestamps between time zones each require extra steps. These small friction points add up in complex automations, so Make.com feels slower despite its lower cost. [L0287.016]
- N8N is the only one of these platforms that supports self-hosting. You run the whole platform on your own computer or laptop instead of N8N's cloud servers, so all processing stays on your device. Self-hosting is possible because N8N is open source. It removes monthly subscription costs, making it free or very inexpensive once you run many automations, unlike Zapier and Make.com, which charge per automation. [L0287.013]
- N8N has the best AI agent functionality of the three main platforms. If you focus heavily on AI agents, N8N is the superior choice despite its steep learning curve, which requires knowledge of APIs, webhooks, JSON and MCP. [L0287.014]
- Make.com is the mid-tier option and N8N the developer option; the platform guidance above applies to IFTTT as well, which the 2023 comparison lists as a Zapier alternative. [L0251.061, L0287.004]

**Cost and tool stack (reference table)**

| Tool | Cost | Purpose in Workflow |
|---|---|---|
| Zapier Premium | $20/month | Connects Shopify to Slack, Trello, Google Sheets, Rev.com, WordPress, and social platforms via automated Zaps |
| Slack Free | Free | Receives Zapier alerts and team messaging |
| Trello Free | Free | Order status board and tracking with five lists |
| Google Sheets | Free | Automated customer data logging and measurement |
| Rev.com | $1/minute | Audio and video transcription |
| Grasshopper | Varies | 1-800 business phone number with SMS and calling app |
| BuzzSumo | Varies | Content research and trend identification |
| Buffer | Varies | Social media scheduling for non-automated posts |

[L0286.029]

- The basic automation stack is Shopify, Zapier, Slack, Trello and Google Sheets. Zapier is the only paid tool in it. [L0437.007, L0437.008, L0286.005, L0286.006, L0286.007, L0286.008]

### Abandoned cart alerts to Slack

**Purpose and timing**
- Slack sends real-time notifications to desktop and smartphone apps. Use it to alert the shop owner or a team member the moment a cart is abandoned, so the phone follow-up can happen within minutes. [L0281.004]
- A real-time Slack notification each time a customer abandons checkout lets the store owner follow up immediately. Slack works like Discord for team alerts. [L0471.002]
- Zapier checks Shopify every 5 minutes for new abandoned carts and forwards customer data directly to Slack. [L0281.005]
- Maximum latency between cart abandonment and the Slack notification is 5 minutes. [L0281.006]
- Set "Abandoned cart age in hours" to 0 in Zapier's Shopify trigger. This sends the notification immediately on abandonment, with no delay. [L0281.009, L0286.009]
- The Zapier wait setting for cart data should also be 0 hours, so the data arrives immediately. [L0737.006]

**Who receives the alert**
- The Slack recipient can be anyone on the support team: the shop owner, a virtual assistant, an employee or a family member helping with phone follow-up. Choose who receives the Slack message when you configure the automation. [L0281.008]
- The Slack free plan receives real-time Zapier notifications, including abandoned cart alerts with customer name, phone number, email, order value and checkout link. [L0286.006]

**Slack message settings**
- Auto expand links: no.
- Link username and channel names: yes. [L0281.010]

**Message template**

```
Hi [Customer First Name], [Customer Last Name] just abandoned a cart with the value of [Cart Value]. [Customer First Name] can be reached at [Customer Phone Number]. The order was for [Product Name], and it would be shipping to [Shipping State]. Here is their abandoned checkout URL: [Abandoned Checkout URL]. Good luck on the call.
```

[L0281.012]

**Example message**
- The Slack bot can be named Steve, with a tuxedo emoji as its profile picture. It alerts the team when customers abandon checkouts. Each message should include customer name, cart total, phone number, product details, shipping destination and the checkout link. Example output: "Hi, [name], John Smith just abandoned a cart with the value of $49. John can be reached at 55555. The order was for an official Source A hoodie, and it would be shipping to New York. Here is their abandoned checkout URL. Good luck on the call." [L0281.014]

**Setup procedure**
1. Create a new Zap in Zapier.
2. Select Shopify as the trigger source.
3. Choose "New abandoned cart" from Shopify trigger options.
4. Authenticate with your Shopify store login credentials.
5. Set "Abandoned cart age in hours" to 0.
6. Click "Test this step" to verify connection with an existing abandoned cart from your store; if none exist, manually create and abandon a test order in Shopify.
7. Add an action by clicking the plus symbol to create a new step.
8. Select Slack as the action platform.
9. Choose "Send a channel message" as the Slack action.
10. Authenticate with your Slack login and select your Slack workspace.
11. Create a new Slack channel for these notifications (e.g., "Steve") or select an existing channel.
12. Click in the message text area to customize the notification.
13. Use the plus button next to the text field to insert dynamic data fields from the abandoned cart: customer first name, customer last name, cart value, customer phone number, product name, shipping state, and abandoned checkout URL.
14. Write the message: Hi [First Name], [Last Name] just abandoned a cart with the value of [Amount]. [First Name] can be reached at [Phone]. The order was for [Product], and it would be shipping to [State]. Here is their abandoned checkout URL: [URL]. Good luck on the call.
15. Set "Bot name" to a custom name (e.g., "Steve").
16. Select a bot icon to represent the automation in Slack.
17. Verify that "Auto expand links" is set to no and "Link username and channel names" is set to yes.
18. Click "Continue" to preview the message based on your test abandoned cart data.
19. Click "Send test to Slack" to verify the message displays correctly in your Slack channel.
20. Once verified, turn the Zap on by clicking the toggle switch at the top (default is off).
21. The automation now runs continuously: Zapier checks for new abandoned carts every 5 minutes and immediately sends a Slack message with all customer data.

[L0281.011]

**Related follow-up options**
- Zapier can be used with Shopify abandoned checkout events to trigger notifications alongside email tools such as Klaviyo or Shopify Email. Before relying on it, opt in with a test customer and place a test order to confirm each Zap fires. [L1114.073]
- Zapier can connect Shopify so that an abandoned cart automatically places the customer into a Minichat Messenger sequence. The connection is set up once and then runs automatically. [L1243.018]
- Zapier automates follow-ups for abandoned checkouts. Keep the subscription only if it brings sales. If it does not pay for itself after a while, cancel it and follow up manually. [L0806.047]
- Zapier's Shopify and Slack connection for abandoned carts is covered in the course's automation module. [L0964.077]
- Shopify's abandoned carts report, under Orders, lists customers who started checkout without buying. It is useful for calling those customers to learn why they left. *Older (2021).* [L0801.115]

### Abandoned cart logging in Google Sheets

**Overview**
- Zapier connects Shopify to Google Sheets without code. [L0737.005]
- Google Sheets (free) automatically logs customer information from Zapier when carts are abandoned or orders are placed. It captures email, name, phone, creation date, contact status and recovery status for team visibility. [L0286.008]

**First Zap: new abandoned cart creates a sheet row**
