1. Log into Zapier and create a new zap.
2. Set the trigger to Shopify and choose 'new abandoned cart'.
3. Connect the Shopify store and select the store to link.
4. In the options, set the wait time before data is passed through to 0 hours so the data arrives immediately.
5. Add an action: click the plus, click action, and choose 'create spreadsheet row' in Google Sheets.
6. Log into your Google account to connect it to the zap.
7. Select the spreadsheet 'abandoned cart tracking sheet' and the worksheet 'abandoned carts'.
8. Map every field (date, name, phone number, email, cart value, products, shipping, link to cart) to the matching data from the test order.
9. Click continue, then click test this step.
10. A successful test pushes the data into the sheet, and the phone team receives a Slack notification at the same time.

[L0737.006]

**Rules for the sheet**
- Zapier matches each mapped field to a sheet header by name. Headers must be typed exactly as the fields are named in the mapping. Mismatched headers stop the correct data from landing in the right column. [L0737.007]
- Leave the Recovered and Notes fields unmapped in the create-row step. Team members fill these in by hand, and mapping them would overwrite or pre-fill fields that need human input. [L0737.008]
- Run the test step with a test order before activating a zap, so you can confirm each field lands in the correct column. Fix any mismatch before the automation runs on real carts. [L0737.009]
- Choosing the wrong spreadsheet or worksheet in Zapier sends rows to the wrong place. A worksheet can have any name, as long as the correct one is selected in the zap. [L0737.010]

**Second Zap: paid order updates the sheet**
1. Create a new zap and name it along the lines of 'paid order to update abandoned cart Google tracking sheet'.
2. Set the trigger to Shopify and choose 'paid order' instead of 'new abandoned cart', then select the store.
3. Add a step called Search in Google Sheets to look for an existing row.
4. Select the spreadsheet 'abandoned cart tracking sheet' and the worksheet 'abandoned carts'.
5. Set the search column to Email and match it against the email of the latest paid order.
6. If no matching row is found, nothing happens and the zap ends without changing the sheet.
7. If a matching row is found, add an update action for that row and fill the Recovered column with the date of the purchase.
8. Keep calls and notes manual; only the Recovered date is automated.

[L0737.011]

- The paid-order Zap matches the sheet only by exact email address. A customer who places the paid order with a different email will not have the Recovered column updated. Enter the recovery date by hand in that case. [L0737.020]

### Order flow: Shopify to suppliers

**Trigger rules**
- Use the Shopify trigger "New Paid Orders" so the automation runs only after an order is marked paid. Rejected or unpaid orders do not trigger it. Refunds require cancelling the order separately. *Older (2021).* [L0469.062]
- Zap 1 trigger conditions: payment status marked as paid; order status any; fulfillment status any. This fires once, when payment is first marked as paid, not repeatedly. [L0292.005]

**Vendor filtering**
- The vendor filter Zap uses Shopify as the order source, Filter by Zapier for vendor matching, and Gmail for sending email. You already have Shopify and Gmail; you only need a Zapier account. [L0283.003]
- When you set up the vendor filter, Zapier pulls sample orders from your Shopify store and displays all available data fields (vendor name, product name, SKU, customer details and more). Use these samples to build and verify the filter condition, for example "line item vendor text exactly matches Source A merch", before the Zap processes live orders. [L0283.015]

**Multi-product orders**
- Zapier order processing can handle orders with multiple products. The lesson demo used a single-product order, so adjust the workflow to process each line item in a multi-product order. *Older (2020).* [L0961.079]

**Fulfilment handoff**
- Zapier is used for most store automations, including order forwarding to suppliers. Shopify's Mark as fulfilled action and Klaviyo tracking emails complete the process from order to customer notification. *Older (2020).* [L0468.078]
- Zapier can automate order processing to suppliers. Choose whether it automatically captures payment or only pre-authorizes, based on how often you run into inventory issues with suppliers. *Older (2021).* [L1090.059]

**Payment capture**
- Use "Authorize but not capture" payments in Shopify's payment settings. The card is checked for funds but not charged until the team verifies stock and captures the payment, after which the order is processed. [L0964.074]
- Setting path: Settings > Payments > Payment capture. Set to "Authorize only" (Authorize, do not capture). Verify stock, then capture the payment and process the order. [L0964.075]

### Order status boards in Trello

**Overview**
- Trello (Trello.com) is a free visual project management tool with a Boards → Lists → Cards hierarchy. Cards can be dragged between lists manually or moved via Zapier automation. It gives a visual overview of order progress without daily administrative work. [L0292.002]
- Trello can track orders on cards, with automation that moves a card to delivered after a set delay. [L0359.054]
- Trello can show where each order sits in the process, from order placed to shipped, as order volume grows. [L0970.085]
- Trello can be used to organise tasks as boards and cards. In an automated setup, it can track orders and collect customer review requests so nothing is missed. [L0437.005]

**Five-list structure**

| Order | List name |
|---|---|
| 1 | Paid orders |
| 2 | Sent to supplier |
| 3 | Shipped orders |
| 4 | Delivered and pending review |
| 5 | Reviewed |

Orders progress through these lists as fulfilment stages change. [L0292.003, L0286.007]

**Two-Zap workflow**
- Order tracking automation runs Shopify → Zapier → Trello. Two Zaps handle the workflow: Zap 1 creates cards when orders reach paid status; Zap 2 moves cards to shipped when fulfilment status changes. This removes manual card creation and movement. [L0292.004]
- Zap 1 (Shopify paid order → create Trello order card): card name "Order [order number] [order date]". The description includes customer info (name, email, date placed), order info (product name, brand, SKU), customer notes (if entered at checkout) and ship-to (full address). Board: select your board. List: paid orders. Position: top, so new cards appear above existing cards. [L0292.006]
- Zap 2 (Shopify shipped order → move Trello card): trigger "Order update in Shopify", with payment status paid and fulfilment status shipped. Action: search for the existing order card in Trello and move it to the shipped orders list. Cards transition from paid orders to shipped when Shopify marks fulfilment as shipped. [L0292.007]
- Card data auto-populates from Shopify with order number, customer details, SKU, notes and shipping address. [L0286.007]
- Before activating a Zap, click Test This Step to verify it. For Zap 1, a successful test creates a sample card on your Trello board, confirming the Shopify-to-Trello connection. For Zap 2, the test demonstrates card movement. Review the result, then click Finish or Turn On. [L0292.014]

**Card fields and comments**
- Each Trello card has two date fields for follow-up deadlines, for example "customer will send product photo by October 29". When a deadline passes, Trello sends reminders to team members to check whether the promised follow-up was completed or needs escalation. [L0286.038]
- Use the comment section of a card to record order status changes, backorder dates and follow-ups, for example "Contacted supplier on backorder until October 29". Each comment is timestamped with the team member's name. [L0286.026]

**Alternative board**
- Asana can serve as the order board. New orders land automatically in the first column, and the VA moves each card to the next column as its status changes. Each card carries a due date, and overdue follow-ups show in red, so the owner can spot them without asking. Asana also handles task assignment to team members and to the owner. [L1473.003]

### Shipment tracking and customer notifications

**AfterShip overview**
- AfterShip is a Shopify app for delivery tracking. Once connected, Klaviyo can trigger emails on the delivery event, so the post-delivery request goes out at the right moment. [L0417.017]
- AfterShip (or a similar fulfilment tracking app) connects to Klaviyo and provides order triggers such as shipped, out for delivery and delivered. These triggers drive the shipping and UGC emails. [L0605.008]
- AfterShip is a parcel tracking platform that shows the status of shipments and can send tracking updates to customers. It is useful when orders ship from suppliers with limited tracking information. [L0985.002]
- AfterShip automatically emails customers each time tracking updates, for example dispatched, in transit, out for delivery and delivered. Configure how many emails are sent and when in the app, then enter tracking numbers in the Shopify order dashboard. [L1285.039]
- AfterShip sends branded tracking emails at each stage of delivery, such as out for delivery, delayed and delivered. The low-cost plan cost about one dollar per month for the store in the lesson. It is recommended for every store as a way to reduce "where is my order" questions. [L0910.029]
- A tracking-notification app such as AfterShip can replace about four separate customer contacts per shipment: in transit, arriving in the destination city, out for delivery and delivery attempt missed. Enter the tracking number once, and the automated updates handle these messages for every order. [L1515.046]
- AfterShip is used for order tracking notifications sent to customers. *Older (2022).* [L0665.042]
- AfterShip is a Shopify app that automates tracking emails to customers and provides an embed code for a tracking page on the store. *Older (2022).* [L1281.088]
- AfterShip is used as an order status tracker on a store. [L0964.078]

**AfterShip plans and limits (conflicting dates)**
- Free plan: 50 shipments per month, with paid plans applying above that volume. The 2021 source gives the same limit as the 2025 source. [L0469.059, L0966.066]
- The free plan can be started for order tracking emails. *2024 source.* [L0856.072]
- The 2021 source describes a branded tracking page where customers enter a tracking or order number. [L0469.059]
- Tracking emails can be set up on the free plan; the low-cost paid plan is about $1 per month. Apply the free plan first, and move to the paid plan when shipment volume exceeds 50 per month. [L0856.072, L0910.029, L0966.066]

**Tracking page code and store health**
- AfterShip provides a code for a tracking page on the store. Generic tracking code found online slowed one store, so use a vetted app instead. [L0835.141]
- Add tracking to Shopify orders directly with a tracking number, or use a tracking app that customers can check. The tracking app is the better option for customer visibility. [L0934.172]
- Tracking apps such as Tracktor or AfterShip can be synced with Klaviyo, so fulfilment update emails are sent automatically and can carry offers. [L0604.009]

**Shop and branded tracking page**
- The Shop app lets customers track orders from any store, not only Shopify orders, and can show them different products. It is optional and does not drive large sales volume. [L0965.056]
- A branded tracking page is free ad space that the brand owns. Wonderment's recommendation engine suggests products based on what the shopper bought. [L0955.045]

**Free tracking sites and carrier tracking**
- The free tracking site 17Track can be used in its drop-shipping mode to hide the supplier's origin, such as China or AliExpress, and to replace those words with custom text such as "Express". It does not reliably show delivery dates, because most couriers do not provide them. [L1315.003]
- Track17 and Track123 show tracking numbers to customers. Check with your supplier which one they use. The Source D lesson chooses Track17 because most parcel carriers are visible there and customers can search tracking there. [L1327.035]

**Courier options in Australia**
- Couriers Please and Sendle are often cheaper than Australia Post for large parcels in Australia. Australia Post's eParcel account manager can also offer lower rates than advertised. *Older (2020).* [L1088.068]

### Zapier in customer forms

- WuFu.com is a form creation tool, available in free and paid versions. It integrates with Zapier to capture custom order requests and trigger automations. [L0284.004]
- Typeform creates forms and surveys. It integrates with Zapier as an alternative to WuFu for collecting custom product requests. [L0284.005]
- Zapier is an automation platform with thousands of integrations. It triggers workflows when events occur, such as a form submission, and stacks multiple actions in sequence within a single Zap. [L0284.006]
- Google Sheets integrates with Zapier to populate lead data automatically. This creates a searchable, ongoing database of all custom order requests for tracking, analysis and optimisation. [L0284.008]

### Content distribution and social media limits

**Transcription**
- Rev.com transcription costs $1 per minute of audio or video content. [L0286.021]
- Zapier uploads new videos or audio files to Rev.com automatically and notifies the team in Slack when transcripts are ready. This removes manual upload and tracking steps. [L0286.022]

**Platform limits**
- Blog automation should target only Facebook and LinkedIn, because Zapier templates format content natively for these platforms. Twitter, Instagram and Pinterest automation produces posts that look non-native and manufactured rather than intentional. [L0312.003]
- Zapier automates blog distribution by connecting WordPress or Shopify blogs to Facebook and LinkedIn. New posts publish automatically to selected pages, formatted natively for each platform. [L0312.001]
- The automation includes a 5-minute delay after a blog post is published. This gives time to catch and fix errors before the post goes out to social media automatically. [L0312.006]
- When posting to multiple Facebook pages in one automation, add a 3-minute delay between posts to different pages, so followers are not overwhelmed by simultaneous posts. [L0312.007]

**WordPress procedure**
1. Create a new zap with WordPress as the trigger.
2. Select the trigger event as 'new blog entry'.
3. Connect your WordPress account and select your blog or website.
4. Add a delay action set to 5 minutes.
5. Add Facebook Pages as an action, select 'create a page post'.
6. From the dropdown, choose which Facebook page to post to.
7. Build the template: add opening text (e.g., 'new e-commerce lifestyle podcast'), then use the plus button to insert the blog title, body and URL as dynamic fields.
8. Select the blog URL as the link to share.
9. Turn on the automation.
10. To post to additional Facebook pages, add another action after the first Facebook post with a 3-minute delay, then add another Facebook Pages action with the same template.
11. To add LinkedIn, add another action with LinkedIn as the platform and include the title, body and content URL.

[L0312.009]

**Shopify blog procedure**
- Zapier's Text Formatter, with the event "convert HTML to markdown", removes HTML tags, meta tags and code from Shopify's blog summary field, so the content displays cleanly on social media. [L0312.012]
- Without converting HTML to markdown, social posts from Shopify blog content display HTML code and meta tags, such as charset declarations and closing HTML tags. The content looks unprofessional and is hard to read. [L0312.013]
- The featured image from the blog post is included automatically in the published Facebook post, with no extra configuration in the template. [L0312.016]

1. Create a new zap and name it (e.g., 'Source A merch blog to social').
2. Set Shopify as the trigger.
3. Select the trigger event 'new blog entry'.
4. Connect your Shopify account by selecting it from the dropdown.
5. Choose which blog category to monitor for new posts (default is 'news'); if you have multiple blog categories, select the correct one from the dropdown.
6. Click continue and test the trigger to verify it finds your blog posts.
7. Add Zapier's Text Formatter as the next action.
8. Select Text as the formatter type.
9. Choose the transform option 'convert HTML to markdown'.
10. Click into the input field, scroll down, and click 'show all options'.
11. Select 'summary HTML' as the input to convert (this is the blog excerpt shown on your homepage, not the full blog post).
12. Test the formatter action to confirm the HTML has been removed.
13. Add a delay action set to 5 minutes.
14. Add Facebook Pages as the next action.
15. Select 'create a page post' as the event.
16. Choose which Facebook page to post to.
17. Build the post template: type static text (e.g., 'new blog post is live'), add line breaks, then insert the blog title, the converted summary output (from the Text Formatter step, not the raw summary HTML), and the URL.
18. For the URL: manually copy the base URL up to and including the blog category path (e.g., 'yoursite.com/blogs/news/') and paste it in the link URL field, then insert the blog post handle field from the Shopify trigger to create the complete working URL.
19. Click continue and test the action.
20. Verify the post appears on your Facebook page with the correct text, summary and clickable link.

[L0312.014]

**Social scheduling**
- Buffer handles social media scheduling for posts that are not automated (see the cost table above). [L0286.029]

### Fraud screening and chargebacks

**Shopify's built-in protection**
- Shopify's built-in fraud protection screens orders as low or high risk. Some merchants add ClearSale for extra protection, though one speaker found Shopify Payments sufficient. *Older (2020).* [L1111.080]
- Shopify's built-in fraud analysis flags high-risk orders when Shopify Payments is in use. Flagged orders should be cancelled and refunded rather than processed. *Older (2020).* [L0468.080]
- Shopify's built-in fraud labels flag orders as "high risk" and list the reasons. Stolen cards are not covered by this flag, so a high-risk flag does not prove a fraudulent card. Check the reasons before cancelling. [L0472.039]
- The two sources conflict on flagged orders. The 2020 advice cancels and refunds them. The 2025 caution applies before you act: a flag is a signal, not proof. [L0468.080, L0472.039]

**Third-party screening apps**
- Signifyd is an order-fraud screening app for Shopify stores. It approves or declines orders and can guarantee protection against chargebacks on approved orders. [L0430.003]
- Signifyd is a third-party order guarantee app. It reviews orders for fraud and reimburses the merchant for approved orders that turn out to be fraudulent. It is optional and adds cost per order. *Older (2020).* [L0468.081]
- Signifyd and ClearSale are third-party fraud screening apps that charge different percentages per order. They are an alternative to Shopify's built-in fraud protection and should be compared by rate and order volume. *Older (2019).* [L0960.010]
- ClearSale is a Shopify app that checks orders for fraud. If it approves an order that later becomes a chargeback, ClearSale reimburses the seller for a fee. Its protection is a paid service. [L0472.038]
- ClearSale can be used as chargeback insurance for orders it approves. It reimburses the merchant for fraud chargebacks on approved orders, at about 1 percent per order. It is optional and was not used by that source's own team. *Older (2022).* [L0665.044]
- The 2022 source quotes about 1 percent per order for ClearSale. The 2025 source gives no rate and describes a paid service. Use the rate quoted in the current ClearSale offer when you compare options. [L0665.044, L0472.038]

**Chargeback insurance**
- A chargeback insurance service that works with Shopify can add protection against unauthorised purchases. The lesson recommended it without the speaker using it personally. [L0934.173]

### Chargeback handling with Disputely

- Disputely alert fees appear on the Shopify billing statement rather than as a separate invoice. The alert prices shown were $16.00 and $25.00 each. [L0351.052]
- Disputely is a chargeback handling app. Its charges are lower and its approach friendlier than the alternative, so the lesson uses it. Disputifier (Smart Chargebacks) is the more expensive alternative. [L1327.034]
- In Disputely Billing, the RDR section has fields to add BIN/CAID and ARN entries. The RDR setting must read "Not active". Disputely support can change it when asked by email. [L0351.053]

### Returns, package protection and checkout add-ons

**Loop Returns and Checkout Plus**
- Loop Returns is return and exchange software that drives refunds into exchanges and keeps money in the business. Its Checkout Plus add-on charges a return fee at checkout. [L0955.039]
- Checkout Plus used to be named Offset and was renamed. The two names refer to the same product. [L0955.042]
- Loop provides an online calculator for return fee and Checkout Plus economics. Its sales reps can run a deeper analysis of a brand's numbers. [L0955.041]
- Package protection can be sold as a standalone product through partnerships. Loop has announced an in-house version for coming months. [L0955.043]
- Gorgias, the help desk, shows the Checkout Plus line on the order because it is a Shopify product. A support agent can check whether a customer was charged a fee they should not have paid. [L0955.044]

**Wonderment**
- Wonderment is an order tracking tool that Loop acquired about a year before the lesson. It now sits in Loop's portfolio as Loop order tracking and works with Loop Returns. [L0955.040]

**Market availability**
- Checkout Plus and Wonderment are sold mainly to brands in the US, Canada, the UK and Australia, with some use in New Zealand and mainland Europe. Confirm the app supports your target market before planning a launch there. [L0955.058]

### Store continuity and data

- Rewind backups can export everything from a Shopify store, including products as CSV, before you close or stop paying for the store. The export can later be used to rebuild a similar store. [L0804.052]

### Financing

- Shopify Capital is a cash-flow financing option that can fund stock purchases. Lending facilities can be tight at times, which can cause cash-flow problems for a brand that must buy a lot of stock. [L0660.022]

## Finance, Tax and Business Admin Tools

### Break-even and margin checks before ads

- The Dropship Toolkit calculator takes a product cost and a selling price and shows the profit margin and break-even ROAS, so you can check a product before running ads [L0440.023].
- A dropshipping break-even calculator takes two inputs: COGS (product cost plus shipping to the customer) and the selling price. It returns the break-even ROAS for the product, so it can be used before launching ads [L0875.027].
- A calculator such as Drop Ship Toolkit computes break-even ROAS from the COGS and selling price you enter, which lets you compare candidate products quickly [L1174.034].
- The Ecom Margin Calculator has three input fields [L0987.017]:
  - LISTING PRICE: the price the product shows on the store.
  - COGS + SHIPPING: the total cost per unit.
  - AD SPEND PER ORDER: optional.
- Its outputs are GROSS MARGIN, PRODUCT MARGIN, PRODUCT ROI, TARGET ROAS and TARGET CPA, plus a tier badge (Elite, Sweet Spot, Workable or Insufficient) with a short status message [L0987.017].
- A Sweet Spot result means the product is ready to scale. An Insufficient result means it needs work and suggests a bundle or a higher-margin product [L0987.018].
- An Insufficient result also shows the hint "Low margin? Try adding a bundle offer" [L0987.018].
- The source does not describe the status messages for the Elite and Workable tiers [L0987.018].
- The Source D margin spreadsheet (named KPIs Margin Net Profit BE ROAS) calculates per product: product cost, credit card fees, merchant account fees, shipping costs, fulfilment cost per unit, total cost of product plus fulfilment, percentage cost, expected gross profit per order, gross margin, net profit, break-even ROAS, and target ROAS at 10%, 15% and 20% margins [L1182.014].
- Use the margin spreadsheet for each market and quantity row to check that the selling price meets the margin target [L1182.014].

#### Pricing procedure for one product in the margin spreadsheet

1. Open the margin spreadsheet and create your own copy with File > Make a copy [L1182.004].
2. Confirm that the market row is set to USA with quantity 1 [L1182.004].
3. Get the USA product cost from the supplier [L1182.004].
4. Enter only the two input cells: the product cost and the selling price (AOV). Do not touch any cell marked in red [L1182.004].
5. Adjust the selling price until the gross profit margin shows 70% [L1182.004].
6. If 70% is not reachable, accept 69%, 68% or 67% as the minimum [L1182.004].

### Profit trackers

- Google Sheets is a free way to track product costs, prices and profits if no dedicated app is used. Enter prices manually and compare them over time [L0806.046].
- A profit tracking spreadsheet records the net profit for each sale. Google Sheets, Excel or Numbers can be used [L0970.095].
- Google Sheets is a free tool for tracking profit per sale, and it can use a sales automation [L0856.071].
- Put the profit formula in the first cell of each column, then drag it down [L1115.059].
  1. Use the spread column for MAP minus wholesale [L1115.059].
  2. Use a later column for spread minus shipping [L1115.059].
- The Order Profit Tracker is a Google Sheets spreadsheet with a Profit Per Order tab and a Setup tab. Colored cells calculate automatically, and only the white, uncolored cells are filled in by hand [L1201.015].
- To get your own copy of the Order Profit Tracker [L1201.016]:
  1. Open the shared link, which gives read-only or view-only access [L1201.016].
  2. Go to File > Make a copy and save it to your Google Drive [L1201.016].
- A Shopify backend app can store the wholesale cost on each product, so margin is tracked in the back end while shoppers see nothing (2019, older) [L0663.062].
- B-Profit is installed as follows [L0445.027]:
  1. Open the recommended apps section, which links to the B-Profit listing in the Shopify App Store [L0445.027].
  2. Click Install [L0445.027].
  3. After creating an account, the B-Profit dashboard opens and shows the profit numbers once setup is finished [L0445.027].
- Before connecting Facebook, Instagram and Klaviyo accounts to BeProfit, pause any active ad blocker on the BeProfit website. Otherwise the account connection steps may not load correctly [L0477.036].

### Bookkeeping and accounting

Choose the tool by stage and order volume.

| Stage | Tool | Ids |
|---|---|---|
| Brand-new store, low volume | Spreadsheet (Google Sheets, Excel or Numbers) | [L0970.084, L0970.095] |
| Early stage, no high transaction volume | Google Sheets (free, less sophisticated) | [L0245.035] |
| Growing volume, many transactions | Xero | [L0245.034, L0835.122] |

- Use Excel to track profit and loss from the beginning. Shopify does not know your expenses, so Excel or a third-party bookkeeping service fills that gap (2021, older) [L0834.054].
- The first years were run on Excel with well over $5 million in sales before switching (2021, older) [L0834.054].
- Google Sheets can serve as basic accounting and reconciliation software for early-stage businesses without high transaction volume. It is free but less sophisticated than dedicated accounting software (2020, older) [L0245.035].
- Google Sheets can handle profit tracking at an early stage and was used for over $10 million in sales before switching to Xero (2023). Note that the source gives a different switch point for Excel above [L0835.122, L0834.054].
- Xero provides centralized bookkeeping with access for accountants, bookkeepers and business owners. It costs approximately £25/month (2020, older). It is superior to Google Sheets for businesses with significant transaction volume [L0245.034].
- Xero is a common accounting platform for e-commerce businesses [L0835.122].
- Xero is a bookkeeping and accounting tool that pulls in transaction data. It is not required at the start, and a spreadsheet is enough while order volume is low [L0970.084].
- Xero is the accounting software used by the bookkeeper [L0856.071].
- A brand-new store does not need Xero or QuickBooks. Once money flows through the business, bookkeeping software makes accountant review cheaper (2019, older). Xero is the example given, and QuickBooks is an alternative [L1086.058].
- In the source's setup, operating subscriptions such as Shopify, Grasshopper and Bonjoro are charged to the Capital One card together with supplier orders. Keeping these subscriptions on one card keeps operating expenses in one statement (2021-22, older) [L0903.039].
- A Google Sheets allocation chart can model the Profit First system (2021-22, older) [L1200.038]:
  1. Enter the sales for a period, the COGS percentage, and the percentage for each account [L1200.038].
  2. The sheet calculates the COGS amount, real revenue, and each account's amount [L1200.038].
  3. Check that the percentage column totals 100% before using it [L1200.038].
- The Profit First planning model is built in Google Sheets, so the owner can share the allocations and staff planning tabs with the team. It is edited and viewed in a browser, so no separate software is needed (2021-22, older) [L1334.048].

### Sales tax with TaxJar

- TaxJar is a Shopify app for automatic tax calculation and filing. Set it up once, and it handles tax in all registered states (2023) [L0274.029].
- TaxJar is used to manage sales tax across several US states. Other tax apps with good reviews in the Shopify app store can also be used (2024) [L0360.074].
- To find it, search for TaxJar in the Shopify app store (2023) [L0970.083].
- TaxJar is a Shopify app that calculates state sales tax and tracks sales thresholds across states, so you know when to register in another state (2023) [L0855.109].
- TaxJar is a provider of e-commerce tax and law information with a large resource section of guides, videos and blog posts. Its app connects to Shopify and can collect and remit sales tax, but using the app is optional (2020, older) [L0551.032].
- TaxJar offers a Shopify app and a resource center with detailed sales tax information. Use it to understand sales tax rules, nexus thresholds and collection requirements (2025) [L0972.032].
- TaxJar offers free resources on e-commerce sales tax and a Shopify app for tax calculation. Use its resources to look up exact nexus thresholds for each state (2020, older) [L0961.020].
- TaxJar provides sales tax and nexus information, including a resource center and an app for Shopify. It is used to check sales thresholds per state (2020, older) [L0967.056].
- TaxJar provides current sales tax information by US state and is a reliable resource for nexus thresholds. Check it before assuming how much tax to collect in each state (2021, older) [L1279.044].
- TaxJar is recommended for sales tax handling in the US and integrates with Shopify. Economic nexus thresholds for each state are on its guide, commonly 100,000 dollars in sales or 200 transactions per year, depending on the state (2022, older) [L0665.043].
- TaxJar is a Shopify app and service that calculates, collects and remits sales tax for states where the business has to collect it (2022, older) [L1281.089].
- TaxJar is a resource for looking up state-by-state sales tax rules, including whether shipping charges are taxable. Check the state rules before setting shipping tax (2020, older) [L1088.066].
- TaxJar is a website and Shopify app that collects sales tax and also files it for you. It is the option for a fully hands-off tax service on a Shopify store (2018, older) [L1274.053].
- The sources differ on how optional TaxJar is. One describes the app as optional (2020) [L0551.032]. The other calls it the fully hands-off option (2018) [L1274.053]. Choose the hands-off app if you want the filing handled for you. Use the resources alone if you only need to look up rules.

### Payment processors and checkout financing

- Shopify Payments is the preferred payment processor where it is supported in the seller's country. Check Shopify's supported-countries list, which included roughly 15 to 20 countries at the time [L0359.049].
- Shopify Payments support includes many European countries (2023). The two sources give different country counts, so check the current list before relying on either [L0855.114, L0359.049].
- 2Checkout is a payment processor that can be used as a fallback when Shopify Payments is not available [L0359.050].
- PayPal can be used as a secondary payment method, not the main one (2023) [L0855.114].
- PayPal Credit can be enabled on a store that already accepts PayPal, which makes financing simple to add. It is one of several financing options for high-ticket orders (2020, older) [L1111.081].
- Shopify's built-in financing through Shop Pay Installments and Klarna is available with Shopify Payments. Use it so customers can split payments for expensive products (2024) [L0806.044].
- Affirm, a pay-over-time financing provider, integrates with Shopify and shows the financing option on product pages next to the Buy It Now button. It can be offered as an alternative to PayPal Credit for customers who prefer financing (2019, older) [L1086.059].
- Currency conversion apps from the Shopify App Store show prices in the local currency based on the visitor's IP address. Shopify also planned a built-in version for fall 2018, and Shopify Payments accepts the converted payments (2018, older) [L1274.071].

### International banking and transfers

- Wise is used for international bank transfers and direct deposit to contractors in other countries, such as the Philippines [L0337.037].
- Mercury and Silicon Valley Bank were mentioned as banking options for doing business abroad in the US (2022, older) [L0665.053].

### Business registration and virtual mailing addresses

- LegalZoom is used to form an LLC and gives a virtual forwarding address that some suppliers accept as a business address (2022, older) [L0854.078].
- Stripe Atlas can set up a US business entity and a US bank account for non-US residents, and the account integrates with Shopify. It is a workable route for non-US sellers who need a US entity (2021, older) [L1279.045].

#### Anytime Mailbox US (US plans, prices in US$, verify before buying; 2021-22, older)

| Plan | Monthly | Yearly | Incoming mail included | Recipients |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Bronze (basic) | 9.99 | 109.99 | 20 items per month, each extra item US$ 3.00 | not stated |
| Silver | 14.99 | 164.99 | 30 items per month | Unlimited |

[L1434.013]

- Anytime Mailbox also includes open and scan of mail as PDFs with free unlimited online storage, and it offers check deposit and mail forwarding. These features are optional for a registered-address-only use (2021-22, older) [L1434.014].

#### Gordon Executive Centre (Sydney North Shore, Australia; AUD, verify before buying; 2021-22, older)

| Term | Price excl. GST | Price incl. GST | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Monthly | 50 per month | 55 | Plus GST |
| 3 months | 145 per 3 months (about 11.15 per week) | 159.50 | Plus GST |
| 6 months | 270 per 6 months (about 10.38 per week) | 297 | Plus GST |
| 6 months with 1 month off | 225 (about 8.65 per week) | 247.50 | Special for the six-month plan |
| Set-up charge | not stated | not stated | Check before signing |

[L1434.016]

- Gordon Executive Centre forwards mail on every business day. Standard post within Australia is charged at postage plus 10%. Courier within Australia is charged at cost plus 10% plus GST. International postage or courier carries no GST. Scanning and emailing costs 3 for the first page and 0.50 for each further page, up to 10 per 10 minutes, plus GST [L1434.019].
- Gordon Executive Centre offers a Lite plan for businesses that need only one name and receive little mail. Use it if the address is only needed for registration and contact details [L1434.020].
- Australian virtual address providers that appear in search results for "virtual mailing address" are listed below. Compare prices on each provider's own page, as search snippets may be outdated (2021-22, older) [L1434.021].
  - Virtual Headquarters (advertised from about AUD 12 per week, 60 locations)
  - Gordon Executive Centre (from 5.97 per week for a single name)
  - Aussie Mailman (mail scanning and redirection, Sydney-based, pay per use)

  - Regus
  - Lawpath
  - Alliance Virtual Offices
  - ABN Australia
  - Mail Boxes Etc.

### Supplier admin

- When a supplier asks for a signed account application form by fax, send it from Gmail using a fax-to-email service, which avoids needing a physical fax machine. Search for the service in a quick web search when a supplier requests an outdated method [L1441.016].

### Cost of testing a second store

- A discounted Shopify trial offer (1 USD per month for three months, through an affiliate link) can reduce the cost of testing a second store. Use it only after the first store is profitable [L1316.043].

### Due diligence and valuation

- Shopify analytics are useful, but buyers still want Google Analytics for store traffic data. Empire Flippers requires Google Analytics for every listing [L0554.028].
- Buyers check backlinks for spam and manipulation. Moz Open Site Explorer (written as "boz.com open site explorer" in the source) shows backlinks to your site, including links you built yourself [L1468.027].
- Centurica publishes due diligence reports that buyers can order on a site. A seller can order the basic report six months out to find weak spots and fix them first [L1468.028].
- The Empire Flippers online valuation tool takes the inputs below. It gives a range that the broker compares with previously sold businesses [L0554.027].

| Input | Effect on valuation |
|---|---|
| Seller hours per week (seller only, not team) | Fewer hours are more valuable: 5 hours is better than 50 |
| Company start date | Older company is more valuable |
| Average monthly revenue | Bigger businesses usually get higher multiples |
| Average monthly expenses | Profit margin affects valuation |
| Number of products | More products mean lower risk |
| Traffic | Minor factor |
| Email subscribers | Minor factor |
| Social channels and followers | Minor factor |

[L0554.027]

### Annual review and goals

- The annual review spreadsheet has four tabs: Business Questions, Business Answers, Personal Questions and Personal Answers. Each question in a Questions tab has its matching answer area in the corresponding Answers tab. Write answers only in the Answers tabs. Use the Numbers file on Mac or the Excel file on Windows (2021-22, older) [L0800.024].
- The Course B Goals and Rocks Tracker is a Google Sheets template that puts the 10-year picture, 3-year picture and 1-year plan on one page as a roadmap. It can be downloaded to Excel or CSV for those who work on desktop or do not use Google Drive (2021-22, older) [L1519.009].
- To set up your own goals tracker [L1519.011]:
  1. Do not edit the shared template directly [L1519.011].
  2. In Google Sheets, click File and then Make a copy, which places a copy in your Google Drive [L1519.011].
  3. Rename the copy to your business name [L1519.011].
  4. Alternatively, download the template as Microsoft Excel or CSV and edit it on your desktop [L1519.011].

## Affiliates, Outreach and Promotion Channels

### Refersion: what it does

- Refersion is affiliate management software for e-commerce stores. You set it up on your site, place an affiliate signup link in your website footer, upload banner ad creatives for affiliates to download, and track commissions and sales [L0202.006].
- Refersion is recommended for managing affiliate programs. It organizes banner creatives, tracks affiliate performance, handles payments, and gives partners a login portal where they access promotional assets and generate tracking links [L0288.002].
- Affiliate software such as Refersion generates unique affiliate links, tracks cookies, and provides dashboards showing affiliate sales, click-through rates, and pending payouts. It integrates with Shopify [L0213.011].
- Refersion is a Shopify app for tracking affiliate programs, managing partnerships, commissions, and approvals [L0270.029].
- Refersion is a Shopify affiliate and referral app used to pay partners or customers commissions for sales they generate [L0430.002].
- Refersion is an affiliate tool that pays creators or partners for sales they generate. It suits affiliate-style programs in which payment depends on results [L0790.019].
- Refersion is an affiliate program platform used to manage affiliate partners and track their sales [L0970.087].
- Refersion is a simple tool for running a store affiliate program [L1092.091].
- Refersion is a Shopify app used to create an affiliate program. Niche blogs can join for free, link to the store to earn affiliate traffic, and create backlinks [L0666.012].
- Refersion is preferred over loyalty-program apps for referral programs and affiliate tracking [L0470.088].

### Refersion: pricing

- Refersion requires a paid subscription, but its costs are easily recouped through affiliate-generated sales [L0213.011].
- Refersion is a paid app, not a free one [L0270.029].
- Niche blogs that join an affiliate program through Refersion do so for free [L0666.012].

### Refersion: when to add it (sources disagree)

- **Older advice (Source B, 2021-22):** Refersion is the recommended software. Place the affiliate signup link in the website footer and upload banners so affiliates can use them [L0288.002, L0202.006].
- **Older advice (Source A, 2022):** Refersion is the affiliate platform recommended for stores large enough to run an affiliate program [L1281.092]. A footer link alone adds no sales [L1113.037].
- **Source A, 2023:** Refersion is the affiliate program app the lesson recommends for managing affiliates, but it is not recommended during the first stage of a store [L0934.053]. Affiliate programs are not advisable in the early phase of a new store, and paid placements on relevant sites usually produce faster results [L0934.167].
- **How to apply:** The 2021-22 sources describe how to set up the program. The 2022 and 2023 sources describe when a store is ready for one. Under the 2023 advice, do not start with an affiliate program in a new store's early phase. The sources give no numeric sales threshold [L0934.053, L0934.167, L1281.092, L1113.037].

### Refersion: banners and affiliate links

- In Refersion, go to Dashboard > Manage > Creatives. This tab shows all banner ads available to your affiliate partners. Partners click a banner to copy an embed code and paste it into their website's HTML to load the banner automatically [L0288.003].

**Procedure: upload a creative to Refersion**

1. Go to Dashboard.
2. Click Manage.
3. Click Creatives.
4. Click the button to create a new creative.
5. Upload a banner image file.
6. Enter the landing page URL where the banner links.
7. Add a description.
8. Assign a category if desired.
9. Save.

Refersion automatically appends each partner's unique affiliate code to the end of your landing page URL [L0288.009].

- Refersion personalizes the landing page URL for every affiliate partner by adding their unique affiliate code to the end. You enter the URL once, and the system handles the personalization [L0288.010].

### Other referral and creator-payment tools

- Referral Candy creates affiliate links for products in a Shopify store, tracks the sales generated by each creator, and lets you set per-order or percentage payouts and tiers [L1379.041].

### Affiliate-site tools

- **Hosting and domain:** Namecheap is used to buy the domain and first-year hosting for the affiliate site. At the time of the lesson, domain registration cost 10 dollars and first-year hosting cost 12 dollars [L0514.029].
- **Platform:** Use WordPress with specific themes and plugins for affiliate sites. Shopify is built for taking payments in a store, so it is a poor choice when your visitors are sent to another merchant [L0514.028].
- **Amazon affiliate store:** Shopper Press is a WordPress e-commerce theme that costs around 70 dollars and can import 20,000 or more Amazon products automatically, with prices, images, and descriptions. It is used to build a branded Amazon affiliate store that sits alongside a dropship store [L0923.021].
- **Prerequisites:** Building an Amazon Associates WordPress store with an imported product catalog requires working knowledge of WordPress, WordPress themes, and affiliate marketing. Without these basics the store is hard to set up and maintain, so wait until you have them before building one [L0923.056].
- **Banner advertising (older advice, 2019):** BuySellAds offers banner placements on different websites within many niches. It can be used to buy visibility on niche sites when paid search and social ads are limited [L0467.050].
- **Free consultation as lead bait:** Skype is the video-call tool named for a short free consultation, such as 10 minutes on a specific topic. Limit the slots, because each lead costs you personal time [L0514.040].

#### Email tools for affiliate sites

- **Entreport:** A powerful autoresponder with membership features and a high price. It suits membership sites, but it is more than a pure affiliate site needs [L0514.025].
- **Mailchimp:** Avoid it for affiliate marketing. Its price is similar to GetResponse, but it bans accounts that send affiliate links in their emails [L0514.026].
- **Aweber:** Be careful. On about half of accounts it requires a mailing history before you can disable confirmed opt-in. A new account would ask every subscriber to confirm, which adds friction [L0514.027].

### Finding and verifying partner contacts

- **hunter.io:** Finds email addresses that belong to a website. It helps when a site has no usable contact email for outreach. Use it only when the site itself does not offer an address you can use [L0332.018].
- **ZoomInfo:** A business-information website that can reveal company contact details when a brand's site cannot be found. Search the brand name plus "Zoominfo" written as one word. The free view of contact details can be used without signing up, and the paid subscription is not required [L0940.029].
- **Sales CRM tools:** Tools that have scraped the internet for contact details can suggest possible email addresses for influencers and public figures. Use them as a supplement to manual searches, not as the only source [L0790.021].
- **D7 Lead Finder:** Collects a list of businesses in a chosen city or country, such as dentists, by pulling listings from Google and business pages. It also shows whether a business is running Facebook ads, and it exports the list to an Excel sheet after about three minutes of processing [L0440.020].

### Building a partner target list

**Dream 100 worksheet**

- To get an editable copy of the Dream 100 worksheet, open the template and choose File > Make a copy to save it to your Google Drive. For offline work, choose Download and pick Excel, which opens in Microsoft Excel or Apple Numbers [L0528.017].
- Use these three sheets:

| Sheet | Columns |
|---|---|
| Sheet 1, master list | Category, Name, URL, Email, Phone number, Guest posts and social shares, Paid ads, Notes, Date contacted |
| Sheet 2, ad trial | Name, URL, Email, Ad types, Date trial requested, Date trial accepted, CPC |
| Sheet 3, guest posts | Name, URL, Email, Date contacted, Notes |

- The master list is the starting point. The guest post and paid ad columns are filled in during research. Use the notes column for every response, follow-up date, and status [L0528.018].

**AI-generated drafts**

- ChatGPT, or another AI tool, can generate a first draft of partner names, categories, URLs, and emails from a prompt. Its output often has broken links, wrong or missing emails, and fewer rows than requested, so it only seeds the sheet and does not replace research [L0528.019].
- The free version of ChatGPT is enough to run a partner-list prompt that returns names, categories, URLs, and emails in a spreadsheet-ready format. Use the same prompt in any other AI tool you already have, but expect incomplete and unverified output [L0528.046].

**Partner-list prompt**

```
I run [store URL]. I'm building a target list of 100 people and companies who could send their followers to my online shop. Candidates include celebrities, websites, blogs, YouTube creators, course sellers, events, Instagram accounts, podcast hosts, and associations whose audiences are interested in buying [niche products]. Find these contacts and enter them in a spreadsheet with this layout: Column A holds the category, chosen from the types above such as celebrity, website, blog, YouTuber, course creator, event, Instagram account, podcast, or association. Column B holds the name of the person or business. Column C holds their website URL, when you can locate one. Column D holds their email address, when you can locate one. If no email turns up, leave the cell empty, and never make up emails or URLs. Aim for 100 rows of people and/or companies. If you can't reach 100, give back as many genuine rows as you can and state the exact number returned.
```
[L0528.020]

**Procedure: run the partner-list prompt**

1. Replace [store URL] in the prompt with your store's URL.
2. Replace [niche products] with the products you sell in your niche.
3. Send the prompt to the AI tool. If it returns two responses, compare them and keep the one with more usable rows.
4. Copy the chosen rows into the master list sheet.
5. If the result has fewer than 100 rows, ask the AI to redo the list and include data for 100 people or entities, then compare the counts again.
6. Keep a partial list as a starting point and fill the gaps through manual research.

[L0528.021]

- In Google Sheets, use View > Freeze > Up to row 3 so the header rows stay visible while you scroll down the list [L0528.022].

### Influencer and creator partnerships

**Karo**

- Karo (getkaro.com, Shopify app "Carro") is a collaborative commerce network. Its influencer side finds people in your own customer base and email list who have an audience. Its basic functions can be used for free. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0869.001]
- Brand partnership features were free at the time of recording, but some elements may be paid. Check the current pricing before using them. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0869.012]
- Karo is listed in the Shopify App Store as "Carro" (apps.shopify.com/carro). Its website appeared on screen as getcarro.com, although the narrator called it getkaro.com. Check the address in the app listing before signing up. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0869.016]
- Karo was built mainly for Shopify but also works with a few other e-commerce platforms. Its described setup path, including connecting customers and email lists, assumes a Shopify account. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0869.017]
- On the Karo homepage, the main buttons are "Get Started" and "Learn More," and the Shopify listing button reads "Install On Shopify." In the brand partnership product view, the button "Add To My Store" changes to "Added To My Store" once the product is on your store. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0869.019]

**Vetting creators**

- Tracker is an influencer-marketing platform that costs about $80,000, which is far too expensive for most brands. Many successful brands do without it and track influencer work in spreadsheets [L0790.022].
- TikTok Business Center lets verified business accounts send direct proposals to creators. The speaker reported that around 95 percent of creators responded to such proposals [L1379.042].
- TikTok Creator Tools analytics show a creator's views and follower data. Asking a creator to share a screenshot of this analytics page verifies their performance [L1379.043].
- Social Blade is a third-party site that shows public statistics for social accounts and can be used as a rough cross-check of a creator's growth. Its accuracy was not confirmed, so treat it as a supporting check and verify key numbers with the creator's own analytics [L1379.056].

### Giveaways and list growth

- Gleam and Viral Sweep are contest tools used to grow an email list. They integrate contests closely with social media and email lists, and only one of the two is needed. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L0608.009]
- WooRise is an instant giveaway tool for building the giveaway funnel. Any other instant giveaway tool can serve the same function [L0797.005].
- Klaviyo holds the giveaway entrant lists, tracks orders and revenue with a 30-day attribution window, and stores the segments used to measure the funnel [L0797.014].
- ManyChat automatically sends a direct message to every account that comments a set keyword on a post. It is used to deliver links, such as downloads, to commenters without manual replies [L1457.018].

### Social channels

**Posting and presence**

- Tailwind schedules social media posts to Instagram, Twitter, and Facebook in advance. A team member scheduled posts for the coming couple of weeks [L0337.036].
- A free Facebook business page is used as a basic social presence for the store. It is the free version only, with no paid ads required at this stage [L0437.002].
- A LinkedIn profile only makes sense if the target audience is active there, which is typically the case for B2B businesses. Consumer-focused stores can skip it [L0437.003].
- Facebook offers an option to sell directly on the platform, but the lesson recommended sending traffic to your Shopify store instead, as of early 2023 [L0934.175].

**Facebook groups**

- Facebook groups have a magnifying-glass search icon. Before posting a question, search the group for the same question, since members often have already asked it and received an answer. (Older advice, 2021-22.) [L1498.022]
- Facebook groups cannot be set to read-only. Require admin approval for new posts to stop new questions, and send members to topic-based community channels instead [L0934.176].

**Growth caveats**

- Some free-trial social-media growth campaigns can produce likes and followers cheaply, but these numbers rarely turn into sales. Focus on clicks and purchases, not followers [L0934.177].

**Content research**

- vidIQ is a Chrome extension that adds data to YouTube pages, including trending videos and views per hour. It is used to find topics that are working right now before making a video [L0487.062].
- Google Sheets can track content ideas: one column for the content idea and the next for the link to the source of inspiration. This is enough to track content research from forums and communities [L0472.041].

### Live shopping and shoppable video

**Livecom**

- Livecom 2.0 is a video player that lets marketers add shoppable video (video that can be bought from directly) to landing pages and product pages without waiting for the development team. Its vendor says it avoids the usual site speed loss from embedding video [L0951.006].
- Livecom live streaming can broadcast one stream at the same time to TikTok Shop, Instagram Live, YouTube, Twitch, X, and any other platform that accepts RTMP streaming. Viewers can then be sent to the website to shop from the stream [L0951.007].
- The Tap Cart integration lets a Shopify store host live streams inside the Tap Cart app and sends a push notification to customers when the stream starts. This makes the live stream available on the Shopify site, inside the Tap Cart app, and on the connected social channels [L0951.008].

**LyveCom story video**

- LyveCom hosts the MP4 files itself instead of Shopify, and the story video is embedded with a script rather than a YouTube or Vimeo iframe. Because of this, the store is not slowed down, and shoppers can add variants to the cart and check out through the store's native Shopify checkout. The platform also works with any Shopify theme that accepts a custom HTML or Liquid snippet [L0959.001].

**Procedure: set up a LyveCom story and embed it**

1. Open the LyveCom dashboard.
2. Create a new story campaign.
3. Name the story. The name appears as the overlay on the video (example: Collection Release).
4. Click Next.
5. Select the product to be tagged in the video.
6. Click Finish.
7. Upload the MP4 file to the story (no file size limit; keep the video short because of attention spans).
8. Click Publish story.
9. Choose the embed method: Floating Widget or Embed.
10. For the widget, open the Widget tab, click Copy the code, and paste the snippet on any page of the website.

[L0959.002]

- There is no file size limit for the uploaded MP4, but short videos are recommended to keep a social, app-like experience. Viewers tend to have short attention spans on product videos [L0959.003].

**Product-page embed snippet**

The snippet below is the LyveCom product-page embed as shown in the Shopify product description editor (code view). The position values were hard to read in the source screenshot, so verify them against the snippet copied from the LyveCom dashboard before publishing. [L0959.014]

```html
<script src="https://dashboard.lyvecom.com/widget/widget.js"></script>
<script>
LyveComWidget.mount({
  account: 'YOUR_ACCOUNT_ID',
  stories: true,
  storyEvent: true,
  position: 'left',
  positionLeft: '0', // value unclear in source, verify
  positionBottom: '0' // value unclear in source, verify
});
</script>
```
[L0959.014]

## Other details

### Learning and course tools

- Video Speed is a free Google Chrome extension that adjusts playback speed of any video, faster or slower. Install it to watch instructional content at your preferred pace (older advice, 2019) [L0243.043].
- Kajabi can show a student a list of videos to watch, mark each video as watched, and show mentors exactly what each student has completed. It is useful for tracking progress in a course or mentoring program [L1017.006].

### Setup and official tool sources

- Official websites for the stack: zapier.com for Zapier, trello.com for Trello, and Google Sheets through Google Drive. Slack and Shopify are used through their own sites and apps [L0437.010].
- A basic laptop is enough to run an online store, including a Chromebook, since heavy software is not needed and large product spreadsheets can be handled in Google Sheets online (older advice, 2022) [L1092.094].
- The Shopify App Store offers apps to add features to a store. A store credit (for example $50) can be spent there when offered [L1304.019].

### Shopify apps and store features

- Reviews and pop-up apps are updated in the course. The ecommerce course module on store setup was rebuilt for current Shopify interfaces (older advice, 2022) [L0969.092].
- Shopify apps can lock parts of a store behind customer accounts, for example a dealer section with different prices and products. Shopify is not the right platform for a content membership site (older advice, 2018) [L1274.070].

### B2B and trade customers

- Shopify plug-ins can display hidden trade-only products and prices that only logged-in, approved trade customers can see. Use them to offer business buyers better deals without showing those prices to the public (older advice, 2018) [L1109.038].
- B2B request-a-quote setup, in four steps (2023) [L1283.071]:
  1. Add a "request a quote" link in the header menu.
  2. Create a quote page with logos of past business customers.
  3. Add a contact form asking for name, phone, email, items of interest and quantities per item.
  4. Add a homepage section with the same business logos near the bottom of the page.

### Offer and customer planning

- The CURVE Offer Builder is a simple spreadsheet that records all nine offer elements in one place: marketing messages, product, price, shipping, USP package, risk reversal, customer service, trust factors, and bonuses. It also has a separate marketing-message section, which is completed after the offer structure is set (older advice, 2021-22) [L0411.040].
- The customer avatar sheet has one main sheet and three side-by-side avatar columns, each holding a full set of customer questions. Its layout, row by row (older advice, 2021-22) [L0872.014]:

| Field | What to record |
|---|---|
| Avatar Name | A memorable name for the avatar, used to reference it in team and agency work |
| Gender | Gender of the avatar's customers |
| Age Range | Age range of the customers the business serves |
| Average Income | Income level being targeted |
| Experts Followed | Influencers, creators with large YouTube channels, bloggers, and other people with large audiences in the niche |
| Prior Relevant Purchases | Products the customer has bought before and where they bought them |
| Favorite Books | Books relevant to the niche or passion |
| Favorite Periodicals | Magazines relevant to the niche or passion |
| Favorite Movies/TV | Movies and TV shows relevant to the niche |
| Favorite Online Spaces | Where customers spend time online, such as social networks, forums, subreddits, Quora, LinkedIn, Twitter |
| Primary Problem(s) | The main problem or problems the product solves, which can be several |
| What do they want to gain? | Benefits the customer wants from the product |
| What do they want to be? | The identity or status the customer wants to have |
| What do they want to do? | What the customer wants to do with the product |
| What do they want to avoid? | Negative experiences the problem causes that the customer wants to escape |
| What emotions are they currently feeling? | Emotions the customer feels around the problem |
| What are their beliefs? | Beliefs the customer holds that relate to the problem and the product |
| What are their wants/desires? | Desires that develop from the problem |

  [L0872.014]
- The avatar spreadsheet has space for three customer avatars, so you can keep distinct buyer profiles side by side. This allows separate profiles for, for example, a female and a male customer group, or for B2B and B2C buyers (older advice, 2021-22) [L0872.015].

### Product map

- Keep the product map as a spreadsheet, not a PDF or a separate document per product. A PDF needs a new file for every product and is harder to edit, while a spreadsheet with one tab per product keeps all products in one file (older advice, 2021-22) [L1160.018].

### Email, orders and tracking apps

- A wishlist or gift registry app should cost at most about $15 per month. Paying $99 per month for a registry app is excessive (older advice, 2021) [L0247.079].
- Klaviyo (email marketing) and AfterShip (order tracking) both have setup documentation and responsive support teams. Follow their step-by-step setup guides before contacting support (older advice, 2019) [L0663.028].

### Booking tools for service businesses

- Fresha is a free booking app for service businesses such as barbershops and salons. It lets clients choose a service, pick a barber and book an appointment online, and it can be connected to Google [L0440.024].

### Troubleshooting apps and store errors

- When an SEO or store app shows errors that cannot be fixed directly, in four steps (older advice, 2022) [L0429.086]:
  1. Copy the exact error message.
  2. Search for that message together with the platform name, for example Shopify, including forums and the Shopify community.
  3. Look for fixes documented by users who had the same error.
  4. If no fix turns up, have a specialist handle it.
- Troubleshooting a technical problem in a Shopify app or store setup, in three steps (older advice, 2021) [L0469.115]:
  1. Search Google for the exact product name or the exact error message.
  2. Search Reddit for the same problem, because people who cannot get help from official support often post there.
  3. Contact general support only if these searches do not solve the issue. Common problems are usually documented by others.

### Backups

- Rewind Backups is used to back up store data and code. Backups are not strictly necessary but are recommended (2024) [L1285.040].

### Tools that need verification

- StoreTasker, Niche Verification and Stubgroup were named as tools in the October 2024 coaching call, but the source gives no usage details for them. Check each one's current function before relying on it (2024) [L1105.012].

### Product claims and compliance

- Laboratory testing or expert review can verify product claims before they are advertised. This protects the claim from being challenged and lets it be stated with exact figures. Unverified claims can cause an ad to be refused by reputable media [L1261.050].

### Work setup and cost of living

- Replace car ownership with ride-hailing (Uber) and monthly Airbnb stays. This avoids car costs such as maintenance, smog checks and washing, and is a practical way to cut responsibilities while living abroad [L1515.023].
